？？？：祝你像你的法国同窗一样勇敢，或者你要做胆小鬼？

共同接续 / Shared continuation

Excerpt from the New York Times
《纽约新闻报》节选

Around midnight two days ago, a large fire broke out in the oak forest on the western outskirts. The delay of the warning and the complex terrains full of inflammables in the forest resulted in a fire that lasted a whole night till dawn and 13 deaths.
The victims entered the forest at dusk. Identities of 6 of them have been confirmed: the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers Tomson Joyce, Shelley Gerald, Kelly Keith, and the project manager Russell James. The others' are still being verified.

The Weyerhaeuser used to be a leader in the wood industry, yet it went bankrupt not long ago due to the wood supply crisis these years. After donating the formula of the Resurrection Potion (basic version) to the Maria Sanatorium in New York, the Sacred Heart Sanatorium in Ohio, and the Presbyterian Church Health Center on certain conditions, the Weyerhaeusers sold all of their forestry lands.
On the night of the fire, the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers, and the workers went into the forest to start the disafforestation project before they died. The police has found traces of arcanist's activities and a small amount of gasoline at the scene, which shows the case is an arson attack. More investigations are needed to identify the suspect.

Reporter: James Welles/Dorey Johnson
前日深夜，位于市郊西侧的橡木树林发生大范围火灾，由于预警不及，林中地形复杂且可燃物繁多，抢险困难，火势持续直至清晨时分才得以扑灭。
     于当日黄昏时分进入森林的十三名人员全部遇难。死亡人员中已确认身份的六人，包括韦耶豪瑟夫妇，与其交易者汤姆森·乔伊斯、雪莱·杰拉德、凯利·基思，施工负责人罗素·詹姆斯。其余人员身份均在进一步核实中。

     因近年来的木材供应风波，原本根基深重的韦耶豪瑟在前些日子宣告破产。韦耶豪瑟夫妇将基础版起死回生剂的配方，有偿捐赠与纽约玛利亚疗养院、俄亥俄州圣心疗养所与长老会医疗中心三方后，又将名下的所有林业用地对外抛售。
     火情发生当晚，韦耶豪瑟夫妇与购买者三人带领工作小组进入森林，预备开始铲除作业，随后遇难。经市警署排查与分析，火情现场的残留有神秘学家活动与少量汽油的痕迹，已可确定为人为纵火案件。更多关于犯罪嫌疑人的信息仍在进一步调查当中。

     报道记者：詹姆斯·威尔斯/多丽·约翰逊

14  审美凝视 · 2/2 / Aesthetic Gaze · 2/2

The results are unsurprising: six unconscious thugs lie heaped together in a pile.
毫无悬念，六名失去意识的打手垒在一块，变成了一座壮观的山丘。

But their fight has drawn unnecessary attention. More patrollers close in.
然而打斗却引起了更多的注意，守卫的脚步声从四面八方传来。

Añil: Follow me, if you will. There's a blind spot this way.
野木蓝：请跟我来。这边是视觉的死角。

Añil: Luz   y   sombra,   ¡reforma!
野木蓝：光影复现。

In a split second, an arcane veil settles over the corner of the gallery where they stand, making it appear empty.
一瞬间，神秘术笼罩着他们所在的画廊拐角，让它呈现出空无一人的模样。

Patroller III: Huh? I could've sworn I heard some roughhousing.
巡逻者Ⅲ：奇怪……刚才明明听到这附近有打斗的声响。

Patroller IV: Maybe one of these paintings is haunted.
巡逻者Ⅳ：是不是哪幅画又闹鬼了？

Patroller III: Mr. Pérez's collection isn't exactly ordinary. Place sure feels spooky, anyway.
巡逻者Ⅲ：也许吧。佩雷斯先生的收藏品都是些奇奇怪怪的玩意儿……我时常感觉这栋房子跟鬼屋似的。

Añil: Finding nothing out of place, the two patrollers move along.
野木蓝：守卫巡逻了一番，并没有发现异状，脚步声渐渐远去了。

Lorentz Butterfly: Why help me?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：为什么要帮我？

Añil: I don't believe you snuck in here just to draw their attention.
野木蓝：我想，引起太多的注意，并不是您潜入的初衷。

Lorentz Butterfly: True. But I could still eliminate any witnesses.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：确实如此。但只要处理掉所有的目击者，这也会是一次成功的潜入。

Añil: Fair enough.
野木蓝：……您说得对。

Añil: Most of the gangsters here are unaware of the secrets surrounding this club.
野木蓝：关于俱乐部的秘密和真相，这些帮派的打手们，大多数并不知情。

Añil: But, Ms. Butterfly, whatever it is you wish to find, I will help you find it.
野木蓝：蝴蝶女士，无论您是想要在这里获得什么，我都可以协助您。

Añil: As I believe I have just demonstrated.
野木蓝：在刚刚，我已经展示了我的诚意。

Lorentz Butterfly: You help me? A forger working for this club?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：就凭你吗，一个替俱乐部伪造画作的家伙？

Lorentz Butterfly: You, who can't even wiggle out from under their thumb yourself?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：甚至要借外来者之手，才能找到摆脱俱乐部控制的办法。你能帮我做什么？

She pulls out an invitation to the Turquoise Serpent Club's upcoming auction.
她从怀中取出那封绿松石蛇俱乐部艺术品拍卖会的邀请函。

Lorentz Butterfly: Prove it. Give me a reason to put my faith in you.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：证明自己。给我一个这么做的理由。

Añil: I know the secret to the invitation.
野木蓝：我知晓这张邀请函的真正用途。

Añil: The symbols here represent a special incantation. You must light up the Tecpatl sign to begin the ritual correctly.
野木蓝：这个图案代表着一条特殊的咒语，需要点亮其中燧石刀的符号，才能正确地启动仪式。

Añil: It's the only way to enter the auction venue, designed to keep humans out.
野木蓝：它是唯一进入拍卖会场地的办法，同时，也将无法操纵神秘学辉光的普通人类拒之门外。

Lorentz Butterfly: Tell me something I don't know.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：说点新鲜的。

The forger draws a nervous breath.
伪造者有些紧张地吸了口气。

Añil: The purpose of the auction isn't to sell forgeries. They're merely for decoration.
野木蓝：……拍卖会的真正目的，并非在那些伪作，它们不过是拍卖会的陪衬品罢了。

Añil: Mr. Pérez's true goal is to build the Turquoise Serpent Club into the greatest underground arcane material market in Mexico.
野木蓝：佩雷斯先生计划将绿松石蛇俱乐部，打造成整个墨西哥最大的神秘学材料地下交易市场。

Añil: The auction is only the first step in his plan.
野木蓝：而这场拍卖会，就是他野心的第一步。

Añil: I can bring you there, two days from now at 12 a.m.
野木蓝：两天后，晚上十二点，我可以带您前往拍卖会所在的地方。

The woman examines his face, then provides a simple, satisfied smile.
匪徒女士满意地眯起眼睛。

Lorentz Butterfly: Pleasure to work with you.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：那就合作愉快了。

15  洞穴之屋 · 1/2 / Oztoalli · 1/2

Cemetery
荒郊墓地

Bleak wind sweeps through the quiet streets at the outskirts of the city. The still night has a foreboding gloom to it.
寂静的夜，阴凉的风掠过无人郊野。

It is that very gloom drawing the camera-headed girl toward a long-abandoned cemetery.
面前荒废已久的墓地呈现出一种别样的阴森。

Loggerhead: At the third fork in the road along Silver Sun Street, Morelos District, after hearing three chirps, turn right at 1137 Blue Parrot Lane.
空脑袋：“莫雷洛斯区，银色太阳街的第三个分岔路口，在听到三声鸟鸣后向右转，蓝鹦鹉巷1137号。”

Loggerhead: That would be here, but ...
空脑袋：是这里，没错！不过……

She looks over her notes—a detailed list of instructions relayed by the Vigil.
摄像头女孩看向手中的纸条，上面详细记载了执夜人转述的重要注意事项。

Loggerhead: "Step 1: Elephant spin."
空脑袋：“第一步：大象鼻子转圈。”

Loggerhead: "Specifics: Pinch your nose with one hand and stretch out your other arm like an elephant trunk. Then, bend over and spin around clockwise ten times."
空脑袋：“具体操作：在月光下，一手捏住自己的鼻子，另一手穿过手臂，弯下腰，顺时针旋转十圈。”

Loggerhead: ...
空脑袋：……

Loggerhead: It has to mean something!
空脑袋：这些特殊的举动，一定有它的道理！

Loggerhead: Like in the original Indiana Jones, my 15th favorite movie!
空脑袋：就像《夺宝奇兵》的情节一样。这是我排名第十五位的最爱电影！

Loggerhead: When the time is right, the jewel on the scepter will reflect the sunlight and point to where the treasure is.
空脑袋：当时间来到特殊的节点，太阳光照会被权杖上的宝石折射，指示出圣物真正的所在地……

Loggerhead: But how am I supposed to do that without a nose to pinch? Ah. Never mind. Just do your best! But I intend to have a word with the Vigiles concerning the accessibility of their rituals for Awakened like me.
空脑袋：可是我没有鼻子——不管了，先试试再说！希望这个仪式能考虑到意识唤醒者多种多样的情况。

Loggerhead: One, two, three, four ...
空脑袋：一……二……三……四……

With earnest effort, she bends down and begins spinning in place.
她虔诚地弯下腰，兀自转起圈来。

By the count of ten, Loggerhead wobbles upright. Without realizing it, she's wandered to the center of the cemetery courtyard.
数到十时，机械女孩摇摇晃晃地站起身，在不知不觉中，她发觉自己走到墓地的庭院中间。

Loggerhead: Next, "Whistle the tune of La Llorona."
空脑袋：接下来是：“用口哨吹出《哭泣的女神秘学家》的曲调。”

Loggerhead: Right, that's the one I recorded Mr. Alan playing for me.
空脑袋：阿兰先生给我听了这首歌。我已经把它录了下来。

Loggerhead: *whistling*
空脑袋：♪嘘——嘘——♪

A strained, tuneless whistle drifts over the cemetery. Nothing changes. Her cinematic imaginings of a midnight ritual awaking something in the deep night seem for naught.
一阵费劲且不成型的口哨声漫过死者居所。四周没有任何变化，想象中的“仪式”没有被开启。

A night wind sweeps past, and though it lacks any cold to it, she finds herself shuddering out of habit.
夜风拂过，虽感受不到温度，但她仍按照人们惯常的反应打了个冷颤。

Loggerhead: Nothing, huh. Could be that I was too slow?
空脑袋：不对吗？难道转圈和口哨都要一气呵成？

Loggerhead: Alright, just try, try again! Ms. Black Ibis and Mr. Alan are trustworthy Vigils. They wouldn't be pulling your leg on something like this.
空脑袋：振作点！黑鹮女士和阿兰先生都是可靠的执夜人，这个仪式一定不会出错。

Loggerhead: Besides, I need to get the info from this mysterious exorcist. I've gotta find that mineral pigment before Lorentz Butterfly does!
空脑袋：想要抢在洛伦兹蝴蝶之前找到矿物颜料，我一定要获得这位神秘除灵师手中珍贵的情报！

She spins again, following the steps exactly as directed, and whistles.
她又原地转起圈来，严格遵照自己琢磨出来的流程，重新吹出断续的曲调。

A rustling noise interrupts her broken tune.
窸窣的响声打断了孱弱的口哨调子。

Loggerhead: ...
空脑袋：……

Not far off, a coffin lid begins to creak open.
不远处的棺材盖子开始慢慢打开。

Loggerhead: ...!!!
空脑袋：……！！！

A dark shadow rises from within.
一个黑色的影子缓缓升起。

Loggerhead: AHHHH!!!
空脑袋：啊——！！！

Her shriek pierces the night sky.
惊叫划破夜空。

16  影迷交流会 / Fan Meetup

Excerpt from Movies to Watch
观影清单节选

I need to be fully prepared for my trip to Mexico!
Movies can certainly provide all the necessary information.

1. Underworld (1927)
Type: Crime/Gangster
Reason: The go-to movie if you want to understand the feuds of modern gangsters!
Latham's Note: The famous line "The City is yours" comes from this movie. But in Scarface, it becomes "The World is yours." Isn't that interesting?
2. Scarface (1932)
Type: Crime/Gangster
Reason: I heard this one shows real gang life in Chicago.
Latham's Note: I saw a real Tommy gun! Though I really don't like the scenes involving them.
3. Macario (1960)
Type: Drama/Fantasy/Día de los Fieles Difuntos!!
Reason: One of the earliest Mexican movies to blend Día de los Fieles Difuntos traditions into its story, exploring life and death through fable.
Latham's Note: Poor Macario just wants to eat an entire turkey! Why can't Death grant him this one wish?
4. Like Water for Chocolate (1992)
Type: Magical Realism/Romance
Reason: Mix water, ground cacao, and chili together—that's what this movie feels like!
Latham's Note: In this movie, tears are definitely more important than seasoning!
5. The Public Enemy (1931)
Type: Drama/Gangster (I really did watch a lot of gangster movies for this mission!)
Reason: Hmm ... because it's a gangster movie? And Jean Harlow, even though she's only in it for ten minutes?
Latham's Note: That street chase scene has at least five cuts. I can't believe the director practically turned the movie into a documentary ...
...
为了这次的墨西哥之旅，我需要做好充足的准备！
电影一定能给我带来所有必要的信息。

1.《地下世界》（1927）
   类型：犯罪/黑帮
   选取理由：要想了解现代黑帮的恩怨情仇，挑这部准没错！
   莱瑟姆的笔记："The City is yours"就出自于此。不过，这句名言在《疤面人》里变成了"The World is yours"，是不是很有趣？
2.《疤面人》（1932）
    类型：犯罪/黑帮
    选取理由：听说这部片子描述了真实的芝加哥黑帮生活。
    莱瑟姆的笔记：我看到了真的汤米枪！不过，我一点儿都不喜欢跟枪有关的场面。
3.《马卡里奥》（1960）
    类型：剧情/奇幻/诸灵节！！
    选取理由：最早将诸灵节传统融入剧情，借寓言故事探讨生死的墨西哥电影之一。
    莱瑟姆的笔记：可怜的马卡里奥只是想吃完一整只火鸡！为什么死神就不能让他实现这个愿望呢？
4.《巧克力情人》（1992）
    类型：魔幻现实主义/浪漫
    选取理由：把水、磨碎的可可豆还有辣椒混合在一起，就是这部电影给人的感觉！
    莱瑟姆的笔记：在这部片子里，眼泪一定比调味品更重要！
5.《国民公敌》（1931）
    类型：剧情/黑帮（为了这次外勤，我真的看了很多黑帮片！）
    选取理由：嗯……因为它是黑帮片？还有只出场了十分钟的珍·哈露？
    莱瑟姆的笔记：那场街头追逐戏至少有五个转场，真没想到导演居然把电影拍成了纪录片……
    ……

17  洞穴之屋 · 2/2 / Oztoalli · 2/2

Loggerhead: W-Wait!
空脑袋：等……等等！

Loggerhead: You're not Ms. Ramona, are you? The exorcist that Ms. Black Ibis and Mr. Alan recommended?
空脑袋：你就是黑鹮女士与阿兰先生所说的犯罪现场除灵师——拉莫纳小姐吗？

What steps out of the coffin is no risen corpse.
棺材中钻出的并非复生的死者。

The girl before her wears a dry expression, her hair still a mess from whatever dream she'd been pulled out of. Most importantly, she's a living, breathing arcanist.
面前的女孩面容冷漠，散乱的头发显示她正从一场美梦中被唤醒——最重要的是，她是一个活生生的神秘学家。

Ramona: Metal head with a metal heart.
拉莫纳：一个毫不讲理的钢铁之躯。

Ramona: Guess that's why they made you a hitwoman. No time for talk. Just get straight to the smacking, yeah?
拉莫纳：这就是夜巡特遣管理局派你来打我一顿的理由吗？

Loggerhead: Oh, oh, no! I'm so sorry, Ms. Ramona. See, I'd just spun around 20 times, so my head was a little scrambled.
空脑袋：不，不是这样的。对不起，拉莫纳小姐，在原地旋转二十圈后，我有些晕头转向，才造成了错误的判断……

Loggerhead: Trust me, there's not a violent reel in my body! I would only ever use force as a last resort!
空脑袋：而且，非必要时刻，我也不提倡以暴力解决问题！

Ramona: Clearly not a fan of whistling either. 'Cause that was the worst La Llorona I've ever heard.
拉莫纳：可以看出，保持平衡和吹口哨也不是你的强项。这是我听过最差劲的《哭泣的女神秘学家》。

Loggerhead: Oh! So, the spinning and whistling were part of the ritual after all!
空脑袋：所以说，转圈与吹口哨，这果然是某种神秘的仪式……！

Ramona: Sure are.
拉莫纳：是的，你说得没错。

Loggerhead: What does it do, exactly? Summon something, or is it more unusual?
空脑袋：仪式开启后，究竟会发生什么？是会召唤出什么东西吗？还是说，会导致什么异象？

Ramona: Mostly, it stops Vigiles from crashing the party.
拉莫纳：这是防止夜巡特遣管理局的人上门打扰的仪式。

Ramona: See, ordinary folks don't usually whistle and spin round in a cemetery, let alone twice.
拉莫纳：通常来说，他们并不会真的到墓地前转圈和吹口哨。还做两次。

Ramona: Though even the Vigiles usually figure out this "ritual" just means "don't show up uninvited."
拉莫纳：大多数有理智的人会明白，这是“谢绝拜访”的意思。

She rises up from her coffin and examines the camera-headed girl wearily.
她慢吞吞地从棺材中站起身来，打量着面前被执夜人暗算的机械女孩。

Loggerhead: ...
空脑袋：……

If she had the right equipment for it, her face would be beet red by now.
若不是她拥有的是一个钢铁制成的脑袋，此刻她的脸已经红透了。

Loggerhead: Oh, aha, well, um, I heard you know just about everything there is to know about Mexico City, Ms. Ramona.
空脑袋：拉莫纳小姐，听说你对墨西哥城无所不知。

Loggerhead: Ms. Black Ibis recommended you. See, I have an important mission, and I was hoping you'd help me—
空脑袋：我是由黑鹮女士介绍而来的，有非常重要的任务，希望你能协助调查——

Ramona: There's been a mix-up.
拉莫纳：黑鹮女士可能对我有些误会。

Ramona: Your people need to quit assuming which side I'm on. Yeah, I don't mess around with the gangs, but I don't have any love for the cops either, got it?
拉莫纳：告诉他们，无需揣测我的立场。我不为黑帮工作，但也不会偏向警察的一方。

Ramona: The Vigiles pay for my time and expertise. I'm not interested in mixing myself up with trouble.
拉莫纳：我虽受雇于夜巡特遣管理局，但无意节外生枝。

Ramona: Sometimes I get rid of the stuff regular folks can't see for a few extra pesos. So, maybe I know the city better than most. But I don't concern myself with the land of the living.
拉莫纳：偶尔我会赚些“除灵”的外快，清除一些普通人无法感受到的东西，因而能多了解一些事情。但那些事情，大多数和生者的世界无关。

Ramona: Why don't you run along back to the Foundation, Metal Head? And tell Black Ibis to knock off the recruitment tactics. I'm not buying what she's selling.
拉莫纳：请回吧，这位来自基金会的硬脑袋小姐。黑鹮女士的算盘要落空了，我并不会因此改变主意而加入执夜队。

Ramona: Oh, and tell them to stop mucking up my house too. I'm trying to make a living here.
拉莫纳：另外，请执夜队的人以后不要随意闯入我的家了。

Loggerhead: Your house? You mean this place?
空脑袋：……这是你的家吗？

Loggerhead: Oh, don't get me wrong. I have the utmost respect for everyone's preferences and beliefs. I mean, nowhere's better than a coffin for a long nap. Heheh ...
空脑袋：啊，我并没有别的意思！我尊重所有人的癖好与信仰！棺材确实是个适合长眠的地方……

Ramona: ...
拉莫纳：……

She turns on her heel without a word, leaving no room for explanation.
她毫不留情地转身离去，并没有给对方挽留的机会。

Loggerhead: Oh, wait! Ms. Ramona, I don't work for the Vigiles, and I'm not here on Ms. Black Ibis's behalf.
空脑袋：等、等一下，拉莫纳小姐！事情有些曲折，其实我并不为夜巡特遣工作。

Loggerhead: I'm here looking into details about a smuggling ring.
空脑袋：我来到墨西哥城，是为了调查一起发生在这儿的新型矿物颜料走私案——

Loggerhead: All I need are some leads about this new mineral pigment.
空脑袋：我只是希望你能提供一些与新型颜料有关的线索。

Loggerhead: Oh, and I can pay! I have ...
空脑袋：对了，我会给你提供报酬的！我身上现在有……

She rummages through her pockets, creating an almost ludicrous amount of clattering and clinking as she does.
她开始翻找身上的口袋，发出一阵“叮叮咣咣”的声响。

When she looks up, Ramona has reappeared before her, silent as a ghost.
一抬头，方才走远的墓地主人悄无声息地出现在她跟前。

Loggerhead: Goodness, you came back quick.
空脑袋：噢，你回来了。

Loggerhead: Hah. Sorry, I ... I didn't bring much with me. Can I write you an IOU?
空脑袋：抱歉，这次出门我没有携带太多值钱的东西——可以先给你写一张欠条吗？

Ramona: A new type of mineral pigment, huh?
拉莫纳：你刚刚提到了，新型矿物颜料。

Loggerhead: Right on the money. It's some kind of special arcane material!
空脑袋：是的，我正在追查一种全新的神秘学材料！

Loggerhead: Ms. Black Ibis told me there wasn't any information on it at the Bureau, but that you might have heard something.
空脑袋：黑鹮小姐告诉我，夜巡特遣管理局最近没有相关的案件，但也许你会知道些什么。

Loggerhead: So, have you got any leads? Anything on this new type of pigment, Manus Vindictae, or arcanist smuggling gangs?
空脑袋：有任何相关的消息吗？颜料走私，重塑之手，或者……神秘学家黑帮之类的？

She racks her mechanical brain for every possibility.
她绞尽脑汁地列举着所有可能性。

Ramona: It's not much to go on, but the talk about a new type of pigment did remind me of something.
拉莫纳：本来是没有的，但“新型”这个说法……让我忽然意识到了一些事。

Loggerhead: Is it something serious?
空脑袋：有什么不对吗？

Ramona: You heard about those arcane murders that have been happening around the city?
拉莫纳：你在墨西哥城的这段时间，有没有听说过最近的神秘学连环案件？

Ramona: Well, we've heard more than enough for everyone. City's buzzing with rumors.
拉莫纳：这也不是什么需要保密的事，因为这些案件，已经在四处都传得沸沸扬扬了。

Ramona: First, it was some poor sap they found in the suburbs. Cops say he drowned. Weird thing was, there wasn't so much as a puddle for kilometers around.
拉莫纳：大半个月前，一位流浪汉溺死在荒郊，案发现场的方圆数公里找不到任何一片湖泊。

Ramona: Five days later, a businessman—a real rich type—found dead in his mansion. He had that place guarded like a palace, but no one saw a damn thing.
拉莫纳：五天后，一位富商死在自己宅邸的玻璃房内，出入口全程有人看守，没有人见过凶手的踪影。

Ramona: Somehow, this guy falls into a vivarium filled with all kinds of illegal Critters. I'm surprised they even figured out he drowned. There wasn't much left of him when I got there.
拉莫纳：由于他在死后跌入了自己饲养违法魔精的生态缸，现场的可怕程度和能发现的线索数量成反比。

Loggerhead: Oh, good gracious! I hadn't heard anything.
空脑袋：啊！这是我第一次听说……

Loggerhead flinches. She didn't expect things to take such a sharp turn.
机械女孩瑟缩了一下，并不明白话题为何会忽然急转直下。

Ramona: Yeah, well, I get a front-row seat on every new crime—comes with the territory.
拉莫纳：我见过很多种死亡现场——我的工作性质如此。

Ramona: This city's special. Got its own way of doing things. Folks here aren't strangers to the supernatural.
拉莫纳：墨西哥城有自己的生存法则。对应地，这里一直有很多奇异的案件。

Ramona: But no matter how weird things got, they used to follow some kind of rhyme or reason. Now it seems like even the Vigiles don't got a clue what's up and what's down anymore.
拉莫纳：不过，再离奇的死法，也遵照死亡的规律。而现在它逐渐失控了，就连经验最丰富的执夜人，都无法解释这些溺水而亡的怪案。

Ramona: They're wasting their time sniffing around after the local gangs. Hmph, but with how deep those roots run, that's a long trail to nowhere.
拉莫纳：他们只能耗费所有的精力，和本地的黑帮纠缠不休，却奈何不了对方根深蒂固的势力。

Ramona: Meanwhile, the bodies keep piling up, with not a single clue worth chasing, and then—
拉莫纳：怪事频出，案件毫无进展，就在这个时候——

Ramona: Then there's you, suddenly showing up, talking about stuff I've never heard of before.
拉莫纳：你正好出现了，携带着一则前所未闻的信息。

She holds her gaze on this strange visitor from the Foundation.
她凝视着面前的基金会来客，若有所思。

Ramona: Could be, with the "Storm," something new slipped its way into the city, like you.
拉莫纳：也许，在这场“暴雨”之下，有某些我从未见过的东西已经潜入此地。

Loggerhead: What? Oh, no, no, no. Ms. Ramona, there's no way I could be the killer! I just got here!
空脑袋：拉莫纳小姐，你……你不要误会！我才刚到达墨西哥城没多久，绝不可能是什么连环案件的凶手！

Ramona: ...
拉莫纳：……

Ramona: Yeah, I know. Is that metal head of yours busted? You don't exactly look the part of a murderer.
拉莫纳：我想你已经完全忘记自己说过了些什么。我不是这个意思。

Ramona: Point is the Vigiles are running blind and chasing their own tails. I'm not looking forward to having to cleanse any more messy crime scenes.
拉莫纳：目前执夜队仍然像无头苍蝇一样做着无用功，让那些可怕的案件现场恢复原状也变成了一件苦差事。

Ramona: But this pigment of yours might just be the breakthrough they need.
拉莫纳：也许你从基金会带来的消息，能让执夜队找到案件的突破口——

Loggerhead: Gosh! So then you're gonna join my investigation, right?
空脑袋：噢！拉莫纳小姐，你愿意参与合作调查了，对吗？

Ramona: I didn't say that. All I said—
拉莫纳：不，我没这么说过，我让你回去找——

Having learned from last time, Loggerhead throws herself at the mercy of the exorcist before she can say no.
吸取了先前的教训，没等对方的拒绝完全说出口，机械女孩已经热烈地凑到对方身前。

Loggerhead: But it sounds like you also care a lot about this, right? We could help each other.
空脑袋：但您听上去似乎也关心着事件的真相，对吗？我们可以互相帮助！

Ramona: Ah, for God's sake. Are there any ears on that hunk of metal?
拉莫纳：也许你的机械身体在组装的时候忘记安耳朵了。

Loggerhead: Shucks! Where are my manners? I haven't introduced myself! I'm Latham, nice to meet you, Ms. Ramona.
空脑袋：我好像忘记自我介绍了。拉莫纳小姐，我的名字是莱瑟姆！

Loggerhead: On behalf of the Timekeeper, I hereby thank you for your help. Oh, let me add it to my to-do list. Thank-you letter for Ramona.
空脑袋：我代表司辰，感谢你的协助！噢，得赶紧记下，回去要写的感谢信又多了一封。

Ramona: Ay no.
拉莫纳：……不。

18  鬼魂歌 · 1/2 / Ghost Song · 1/2

Vigiles Cadet I: Another case? That makes five. And it's only been three days since the last.
见习执夜Ⅰ：这才过去了三天。第五起了。

Vigiles Cadet II: The intervals are getting shorter.
见习执夜Ⅱ：更可怕的情况是，相较上一次，作案间隔再次缩短了。

???: You two slack-jaws owe me an explanation.
？？？：如今这样的情况，我想两位执夜人应该好好解释一下。

Crime Scene
案发现场·Ⅴ

Vigiles Cadet I: Sorry, Ms. ...
见习执夜Ⅰ：对不起，这位……

"Special Foundation Investigator": Jones, Special Investigator for the "Storm" Era Historical Monitoring Department at the Buenos Aires Branch of the St. Pavlov Foundation and Field Agent Administration.
“基金会调查官”：圣洛夫基金会布宜诺斯艾利斯分部“暴雨”时代历史监测部门并委外合约人才管理组织特遣调查官，琼斯女士。

"Special Foundation Investigator": Do I need to show you my ID again?
“基金会调查官”：需要再看一遍我的证件吗？

The string of titles leaves the cadet's head spinning.
一连串的称呼让仍处于见习阶段的执夜人头晕脑胀，连忙摇头。

"Special Foundation Investigator": So this is the fifth case, huh?
“基金会调查官”：如果你们说的没错，这已经是系列案件的第五起了。

"Special Foundation Investigator": And you're telling me that after a whole month and two applications for backup, you got squat to show for it?
“基金会调查官”：你们入驻这个时代的重要城市已经一个月了，还申请了两次增派人手。在这段时间内，夜巡特遣管理局的调查就一点进展都没有吗？

Cold sweat beads over their foreheads; they sputter over an answer between themselves.
两名见习执夜人冷汗直冒。

Vigiles Cadet II: Actually, it hasn't been a month yet, Ms. Investigator.
见习执夜Ⅱ：其实并没有满一个月时间，调查官女士——

"Special Foundation Investigator": Call me Ms. Jones.
“基金会调查官”：叫我琼斯女士。

"Special Foundation Investigator": So, gimme the latest.
“基金会调查官”：算了，和你们说这个没有意义。给我讲述一下案件的情况吧。

Vigiles Cadet I: Yes, Ms. Jones.
见习执夜Ⅰ：好的，琼斯女士。

Vigiles Cadet I: The incident occurred at around 8 p.m. last night. Lots of people home, but no one reported hearing anything unusual.
见习执夜Ⅰ：案发时间是昨晚八点左右，那时，文化公寓附近的居民都在家里休息，没有人听到异响。

Vigiles Cadet I: The victim exhibited clear signs of drowning, but there's no water sources nearby, and no evidence of the body having been moved.
见习执夜Ⅰ：与之前一致，受害者有着明显的溺水症状。但现场中并无任何水源，也没有二次搬运尸体的痕迹。

Vigiles Cadet II: We believe that everything occurred here.
见习执夜Ⅱ：我认为这里就是第一现场。

Vigiles Cadet II: We found nothing using human investigation methods. So, it must be another arcane crime.
见习执夜Ⅱ：人类的侦查手段没有找到任何线索。这是一起超出常理的神秘学犯罪。

She turns from them to scrutinize the crime scene, treading the fine line of stern authority like she was born to it.
“调查官”面色严肃，审视着案发现场。

"Special Foundation Investigator": What's the status of the arcanum detection?
“基金会调查官”：神秘学检测如何？

Vigiles Cadet II: Damning. There's arcane traces all over the scene.
见习执夜Ⅱ：仪器显示，案发现场残存大量的神秘术辉光。

Vigiles Cadet II: Like a ritual was held here.
见习执夜Ⅱ：就像是……有人在这里举行了什么仪式一样。

Vigiles Cadet II: We tried to follow the fluctuations' trail, but they just disappeared. Our perp must have scrubbed it clean before leaving.
见习执夜Ⅱ：我们尝试使用仪器追踪辉光波段，但它消失得非常干净，凶手在离开案发现场后，熟练地清除了神秘术的痕迹。

"Special Foundation Investigator": Ah, that does make things tricky.
“基金会调查官”：我明白了，这确实非常棘手。

"Special Foundation Investigator": So, drowning, huh? Seems like solving the mystery of this ritual could be the key to cracking the case.
“基金会调查官”：溺水的症状……也许找到凶手所举行的仪式，就是案件的突破口。

Vigiles Cadet II: There's no record of this arcane fluctuation's particular signal in the Vigiles Bureau archives.
见习执夜Ⅱ：这个仪式的辉光频段，夜巡特遣管理局没有任何相关的记录。

Vigiles Cadet II: So it's either very rare or a brand-new form of arcane skill.
见习执夜Ⅱ：要么它非常罕见，要么是全新的神秘术……

"Special Foundation Investigator": Hand the case file over. I'll see if I can wrangle some help from the Foundation.
“基金会调查官”：我会想办法申请基金会神秘术部门的协助的。劳烦你们将案件详细资料交给我。

Vigiles Cadet II: That would be great, Ms. Jones! Please contact us if you have any new leads.
见习执夜Ⅱ：这实在太好了，琼斯女士！如果您那边有什么线索的话，请随时和我们联系。

"Special Foundation Investigator": You got it.
“基金会调查官”：当然。

She collects their files and then turns to leave briskly. As she does, she brushes past a Vigil rushing toward the scene.
“调查官”收起资料，脚步匆匆地离去了，正好与走廊上赶来的执夜人擦肩而过。

Black Ibis: Sorry I'm late. Something came up on the way.
黑鹮：路上出了些状况，我们来晚了。

Vigiles Cadet I: Ms. Black Ibis, Alan!
见习执夜Ⅰ：黑鹮女士，阿兰先生，你们来了！

Black Ibis's Cadet: Who was that?
副手：刚才那位是？

Vigiles Cadet I: Ms. Jones from some branch of the Foundation. She's investigating this case as well.
见习执夜Ⅰ：是基金会分部……某个部门的琼斯女士，来了解案件情况的。

Vigiles Cadet I: She said she's going to contact the Foundation for additional support.
见习执夜Ⅰ：好消息是，她说会去申请神秘术部门的协助！

Black Ibis: Hm. Another new face from the Foundation. Quite a few of them showing up these days, aren't there?
黑鹮：又是基金会的生面孔。最近出现在这儿的基金会成员可真不少。

Black Ibis: Or are things getting so divided that everyone's sending their own teams?
黑鹮：还是说，他们内部的分歧，已经多到了无法忽视的地步——

Having no reason to suspect ill-intent, Black Ibis soon puts the incident out of mind.
执夜人很快把这个小小的插曲抛在脑后。

Black Ibis: Carlos, I need you to identify the victim's details: friends, family, employer.
黑鹮：卡洛斯，你去调查死者的亲属关系。

Black Ibis: Lín Huì, you stay on the scene and continue searching for any additional arcane energy fluctuations.
黑鹮：林慧，你继续使用仪器记录现场，并且探查附近异常的神秘学能量波动。

Vigiles Cadet I & Vigiles Cadet II: Yes, ma'am.
见习执夜Ⅰ&Ⅱ：是，长官。

She lets out a soft sigh as she waves the other cadets off, leaving only her trainee.
两人离开后，黑鹮轻轻叹了口气。

Black Ibis's Cadet: You do know this is pointless, right?
副手：看来你已经提前意识到我们在做无用功了。

Black Ibis: We will need it for the documentation anyway. But if the pattern holds, these victims will have no relation yet again.
黑鹮：如果和之前的案件一样，那么我们最终就会发现，这些受害者的社会关系没有丝毫重叠。

Black Ibis: This is no ordinary crime of passion.
黑鹮：这并非普通的寻仇。

Black Ibis's Cadet: All that means is that we haven't identified the killer's criteria.
副手：这只能说明我们并没有发现凶手挑选受害者的标准。

Black Ibis: That's the worst part—knowing that we know nothing.
黑鹮：是的。我们一无所知——这才是最可怕的。

Black Ibis: Do you think it could be our old "friend" Manus Vindictae?
黑鹮：你认为，这一切和我们的老朋友，重塑之手有关联吗？

Black Ibis: Pigment smuggling, the resurrection ritual ... Maybe Latham is right. Maybe there are Manus secrets hidden in the dark corners of this city.
黑鹮：颜料走私，“起死回生术”……或许就像莱瑟姆所说的一样，这座城市的角落里，隐藏着重塑之手的秘密。

Black Ibis: And these cases play some part in it.
黑鹮：而这些案件，都是某个“秘密”的一环。

The Vigil pauses. Something clicks.
执夜人停顿了片刻，灵光一闪。

Black Ibis: Just like Paris before the fifth "Storm."
黑鹮：就像当年——第五次“暴雨”前的巴黎一样。

Black Ibis's Cadet: You suspect Madam Bouanich and her jewel hunter could be involved again?
副手：你仍在怀疑，布翁尼女士和她身边的那个女孩？

Black Ibis: Always. Something tells me she's hiding a great deal about the Depths of Myth incident.
黑鹮：我一直都没有放下过我的怀疑。在那场审讯里，我的直觉告诉我，她肯定有什么东西瞒着我们。

Black Ibis: Her true identity, her movements, her involvement with the Manus, each could be essential to our investigation.
黑鹮：布翁尼的真实身份、行动轨迹、重塑之手在其中的参与程度……无论是什么，那一定至关紧要。

Black Ibis's Cadet: But we haven't detected any arcane fluctuations like that incident.
副手：目前局里的监测仪器，并没有监测到任何类似于迷思海泄露案件的神秘能量波动。

Black Ibis's Cadet: If there were any fluctuations as serious as those, we would've noticed them in no time. But this is already the fifth case. I don't think they're related.
副手：案件已经发生了五起，放心吧，如果是迷思海泄露那种规模的波动，我们第一时间就会发现的。

Black Ibis: Hmm. You make a good point. Maybe I'm just paranoid.
黑鹮：也对，可能是我多想了吧。

Vigiles Cadet II: Ma'am, the record is complete.
见习执夜Ⅱ：报告长官，现场已经记录完毕。

Black Ibis: Well done. Contact the local hospital to take away the victim's body.
黑鹮：辛苦了。联系当地医院收殓受害者遗体吧。

Black Ibis: Let's go, Bruno. And call the exorcist. Let's make sure this stays hush-hush.
黑鹮：我们走吧，布鲁诺，把犯罪现场除灵师叫来。到她登场的时候了。

Loggerhead: Don't worry, Ms. Ramona. I'm fully aware of how important it is to protect the integrity of the crime scene!
空脑袋：请放心，拉莫纳小姐。对于保护第一现场的重要性，我已经有了充分的了解！

Loggerhead: Before we left, I replayed a load of my favorite detective movies: Alibi, Death on the Nile, even a filmed production of The Mousetrap!
空脑袋：在出发之前，我特意重温了许多我喜爱的经典探案作品，包括《不在场证明》、《尼罗河上的惨案》和最负盛名的舞台剧《捕鼠器》～

Loggerhead: Let's get started! I'm sure we'll find some crucial—
空脑袋：让我们开始行动吧！我们一定能够在案发现场找到许多至关重要的——

Ramona interrupts her detective fantasy.
话音未落，除灵师毫不留情地中断了对方的探案想象。

Ramona: Look, I don't know what kinda deal you made with the Vigiles to let you muck around in their crime scene, but here's my deal: I'm not here to help you with your investigation.
拉莫纳：也许你和执夜人有过什么约定，他们才会允许你进入现场。但听清楚了，我从来没有答应过要协助你的调查。

Ramona: Now stay outta my way. I got a job to do.
拉莫纳：现在，莱瑟姆小姐，请你不要妨碍我工作。

Loggerhead: Oh, haha, right, the job.
空脑袋：噢，是的，工作！

Loggerhead: Okay, well, ah, is there anything I can help with? I'll do whatever you want.
空脑袋：那……有什么我帮得上忙的吗？我也想做一些力所能及的事情。

Loggerhead: Oh, how about some footage of the scene? I've got it all recorded.
空脑袋：你是否需要现场的影像？我已经把这里的情况完完整整地录下来了！

Her enthusiasm lands on deaf ears.
机械女孩的热情提议没有得到丝毫的回应。

Loggerhead: Ah, Mr. Alan was right. You definitely need to have a way with words to deal with her.
空脑袋：阿兰先生说得对。这果然非常需要沟通技巧……

The exorcist produces annatto seeds, wormwood, and marigold from the case she carries, arranging them in order at the center of the apartment.
除灵师从随身携带的箱子里拿出胭脂树籽、苦艾草与万寿菊，按顺序摆放在公寓中央。

The candle is lit, its glow washing over her face and eyes. The flame flickers, releasing a strange aroma, and the air grows even more still.
蜡烛被点燃，照亮她的瞳孔与脸庞。火苗颤动，散发出奇特的香味，气氛因此变得更加静谧。

Loggerhead: What are these? Some herbs for casting arcane skills?
空脑袋：这些是什么？看起来都是些用于施展某种神秘术的草药……

Ramona: Arcane crimes cause huge spikes in energy fluctuations.
拉莫纳：许多神秘学犯罪案件，都会造成强大的能量波动。

Ramona: The tremors are so strong that they form a kind of vortex of sorts, pulling any nearby invisible entities to them like a whirlpool.
拉莫纳：这种能量的余颤，如同一个巨大的漩涡，会将附近所有无法观测的特殊存在都吸引过来。

Ramona: And once it's started, it'll keep on going, 'till you use the right arcanum.
拉莫纳：只有使用神秘学的方式，才能让其平息。

Ramona: If you leave it, well, the vortex only gets stronger.
拉莫纳：如果没有进行这样的除灵工作，长此以往，此处的神秘学能量余颤会变得越发强大——

Ramona: Soon enough, this place will be "haunted." Least that's what regular people would call it.
拉莫纳：最终，这栋公寓会变成世人口中所谓的“凶宅”。

Ramona: That's why they pay me to do what I do.
拉莫纳：这就是执夜人雇用我的意义。

Ramona takes to the work with professional detachment, but Loggerhead feels something strange, a creeping chill that puts her ill at ease.
在她冷静的阐述之中，机械女孩产生了一种本不该有的错觉——周遭的温度似乎越来越低了。

Loggerhead: By "invisible entities," you mean ...
空脑袋：你说的“特殊存在”，指的是……

Ramona: They're like hidden arcane energy fluctuations? Or unsettled pneuma.
拉莫纳：该叫它们隐秘的神秘学能量波动？还是称呼它们为不安分的“灵”呢？

Ramona: I guess what you're fishing for is—
拉莫纳：当然，对于人们而言，更常见的说法是——

The air above the candle's flame begins to warp, twisting into a form both human and not, in the most unsettling way.
被蜡烛的焰火照耀的空气开始扭曲，产生难以言喻的异状。

Loggerhead: Ghosts? I didn't know you could just reveal them like that.
空脑袋：这是……鬼魂！原来是通过这种方式让它们显现的……

Ramona: Call them whatever you like. It's all the same.
拉莫纳：我想，该如何称呼，这就看大家的喜好了。

19  水晶之城 / The City of Crystal

Memorandum on the 1936 Depths of Myth Incident
1936年迷思海事件备忘录

Depths of Myth
The "Archive of Imagery," the "anima mundi," the "shared dream," the "sea of collective unconscious."
All living things connect here ... right?

Laurent Roseau
Parapsychology researcher, revered professor, and mentor to many.
Member of Manus Vindictae.
Rather than using the "Storm" to struggle in a physical world destined to be flawed, he prefers to let the Depths of Myth shape every dream into reality.

1936
Two Parises, two French Blues, one experiment.
One "Storm."

Brume
Female, taxi driver. Birth name: Adèle Tavernier.
Witness to the incident caused by Manus Vindictae. During the fifth "Storm," she was brought to the sanctuary by Beryl Bouanich.
Brume's testimony indicates that Beryl Bouanich has never had any contact with Manus Vindictae.
The place where she grew up and everything she once knew were swept away by the "Storm."

Beryl Bouanich
Female, renowned diviner, crystal merchant.
During the fifth "Storm," she brought Brume to the sanctuary.
She has no connection to Manus Vindictae.
In Bouanich's visions, everything is already written.
迷思海
意象宝库、世界灵魂、所有人的梦境、集体潜意识的海洋。
所有活着的将在此处连结。是这样……吗？

劳伦·罗西
超心理学研究者，受尊敬的教授，诸多学生的导师。
重塑之手成员。
比起利用“暴雨”在注定有瑕疵的物质世界中挣扎，他更喜欢由迷思海将集体的理想化为现实。

1936年
两个巴黎，两枚法兰西之蓝，一个实验。
一场“暴雨”。

灰调蓝
女，出租车司机，本名阿黛拉·塔沃尼尔。
“重塑之手”引发的事件亲历者。在第五次“暴雨”中，由贝丽尔·布翁尼带往“暴雨”庇护所。

她所生长之处、所熟识的一切，都被“暴雨”冲刷殆尽。

贝丽尔·布翁尼
女，著名占卜师，水晶商人。
在第五次“暴雨”中，将灰调蓝带往“暴雨”庇护所。
她与“重塑之手”没有任何关联。
在布翁尼的预见中，一切皆已命定。

洛伦兹蝴蝶：嗯？

阿莱夫：但这并不能削弱过往的记忆对我的影响。
阿莱夫：譬如此刻，我仍然感受到自己处于那场永恒的大火之中，火舌灼烧着我的皮肤。
阿莱夫：而阿玛尔菲塔诺……它仍在碎裂。

21  鬼魂歌 · 2/2 / Ghost Song · 2/2

拉莫纳：我以为，你的机械身躯，是你坚持与我一同进入神秘学犯罪现场的最大保障。

拉莫纳：不过，看来你并不害怕鬼魂。

空脑袋：等等！拉莫纳小姐，你手中的蜡烛看起来有点不对劲——

The flame flickers harder, as if caught in a strong wind yet refusing to snuff out.
拉莫纳：虚弱的烛火颤动得更加厉害，像被狂风吹拂着，却始终没有熄灭。

拉莫纳：虚妄消却，不辩自明。

拉莫纳：此地，此刻，示我，现我。

Her voice is ethereal, as if speaking to another world entirely. The tiny flame settles, and the last wisp of smoke bends at an unnatural angle, pointing the way.
拉莫纳：她的声音空灵，烛火被她安抚，渐渐停止了颤动。残留的烛烟发生了奇异的倾斜，指明一个方向。

Ramona: Hmm. Weird.
拉莫纳：这……真奇怪。

Ramona: There's another fluctuation here, besides the pneuma that's been drawn over.
拉莫纳：除了被吸引至此的“灵”，这里还残留着某种特殊的余颤。

Loggerhead: Looks like the candlelight's leading us toward the kitchen.
空脑袋：烛火指示的方向，好像是文化公寓的公用厨房。

Loggerhead: Are there still ghosts there? Didn't we just scare them all off?
空脑袋：难道这栋公寓里还有鬼魂吗？刚刚的除灵……并没有彻底驱逐那些……

Ramona steps into a long-idle kitchen before crouching to pick through a pile of firewood. After a time, she pulls out a half-burned scrap of canvas cloth.
除灵师迈入闲置已久的厨房，她蹲下身体，在一堆木柴中挑出未燃尽的布条。

A strange symbol surfaces from the still-warm ashes.
奇异的符号在带着余温的灰烬中浮现。

Ramona: I've seen this before.
拉莫纳：我见过这个印记。

Ramona: Somewhere.
拉莫纳：——在某个地方。

Loggerhead: Have you found something, Ms. Ramona?
空脑袋：拉莫纳小姐，你找到线索了吗？

Ramona: Yeah, maybe. I know someone who can answer my questions.
拉莫纳：也许吧。有个人可以解答我的疑惑。

Ramona: But I wouldn't call it good news. This symbol won't take us anyplace safe.
拉莫纳：但这可不是什么好消息。这个符号代表的，并不是什么好地方。

Her revelation is dry and fatalistic, as if she were tugged unwillingly to the answer.
她漫不经心地回答。

拉莫纳：我知道接下来要去哪里了。

22  墨西哥式奇物 / Mexican Souvenir

拉莫纳：他们需要一个与逝者沟通的方式，来寄托自己的思念，因而期望我能看见鬼魂，能与他们说上话，替他们传达那些未能道出口的话语。

Añil: Ah! My apologies, Ms. Ramona. You're right. It was nothing but wishful thinking.
野木蓝：是啊……我很抱歉，拉莫纳小姐。这个问题只是一种强加的期待。

He unconsciously lets his eyes fall on her Vigiles Bureau attaché badge. It holds no authority, but it sets the tone of their conversation.
野木蓝的视线从对方身上夜巡特遣管理局的徽章移开。

Añil: So, is there something I can do for you?
野木蓝：您这次前来，是有什么事吗？

Ramona: Yeah, I got a couple questions.
拉莫纳：我确实有一些事情想要问你。

Ramona: These symbols, I think you might know 'em.
拉莫纳：这个记号，对你来说应该不陌生吧。

Añil: An arrow, a bow, and a net.
野木蓝：一支箭镞，一把猎弓，一张网状的袋子。

Añil: According to the Codex Chimalpahin, these are the gifts given to the Aztecs by the sun god Huitzilopochtli.
野木蓝：这是《奇马尔帕因手抄本》记载中，太阳神维齐洛波奇特利送给阿兹特克人的礼物。

Ramona: It's got the emblem of the Turquoise Serpent Club, too.
拉莫纳：也是绿松石蛇俱乐部的符号。

Ramona: Which is the biggest smuggling ring in Mexico City, controlled by that arcanist Pérez.
拉莫纳：那个由神秘学家佩雷斯掌控的，墨西哥城最大的地下走私集团。

She tucks away the scrap of cloth.
访客将布条收起。

Ramona: Can't be too many folks around who know you work for him. But those occult paintings at his club had to come from someone, yeah?
拉莫纳：你替佩雷斯工作，为俱乐部伪造神秘学名画。知道这件事的人并不多。

Ramona: But your boss's been acting strange the last few weeks, right? More cutthroat than ever. He's planning something big, everyone knows it, and an auction to the tune of an "Ancient Bloody Civilization" is the best way to start it.
拉莫纳：大半个月前，佩雷斯的动作越来越大，明眼人都知道，他的野心在谋划更多的东西，而一场冠着“深邃、血腥的古老文明”名头的拍卖会就是最好的作秀。

Añil: Hm, then it is true. Nothing in this city escapes you.
野木蓝：看来这个城市中的一举一动都不能瞒过您的眼睛。

Añil: And should I presume this interrogation has been commissioned by the Vigiles Bureau?
野木蓝：这是来自夜巡特遣管理局的调查吗？

Ramona: The Vigiles? Nah, what do you take me for?
拉莫纳：执夜队？不，我才不在意那些穿制服的家伙在干些什么。

Ramona: You think I shed a single tear over "injustice"? Think I care about your boss's little auction? Nah. You and I both know that arcanist gangs aren't to be messed with, and I'm not in the business of making messes.
拉莫纳：我并非所谓的“正义之士”，不打算阻止这场拍卖会。再说了，这些根深蒂固的神秘学家黑帮可不是好惹的，这一点你应该比我更为清楚。

Ramona: But strange things are brewing in the shadows.
拉莫纳：只是，这座城市最近的异象，在你所感受不到的地方正酝酿着——

Ramona: On street corners, behind people's smiles, in the boarded-up buildings.
拉莫纳：那些热闹街巷的阴影里，人们笑容的背后，废弃的空屋中。

Ramona: The pneuma is stirring up all around town.
拉莫纳：“灵”的能量变得躁动不安起来。

Ramona: Hovering over the city like a shroud, echoing in every howl and shriek in the night.
拉莫纳：它们在午夜扭曲，盘旋在城市上空，尖啸着……

Ramona: Like nothing I've ever felt before. Who knows what'll happen if things stay this way?
拉莫纳：这是以前从来没有过的光景。再这样下去，我不确定会发生什么。

Añil: It's your arcane gift warning you about the coming crisis.
野木蓝：是您的神秘学天赋在向您预警，即将到来的危险。

Ramona: I wouldn't call it a gift, especially when you're born into a so-called "psychic" family.
拉莫纳：这算什么好天赋？特别是，当你出生于一个……所谓“通灵世家”的时候。

She leaves it at that, unwilling to say more.
面对自己的出身，少女不愿多提，迅速跳过了这个话题。

Ramona: Look, I know you know something about these arcane murders. There's already been five of them. And it's gotta have something to do with these weird spikes in the pneuma.
拉莫纳：最近在城里甚嚣尘上的流言，接连五起的神秘学谋杀案，你不可能没有听说过。我怀疑“灵”的异常，正是和这起连环案件有关。

Ramona: That's how I found these symbols, left behind at one of the crime scenes.
拉莫纳：这个符号，是我在其中一个犯罪现场偶然发现的。

Ramona: So, I wanna know the connection between the club and these killings.
拉莫纳：所以我要弄清楚，俱乐部和这起案件真正的联系。

She wrestles the question out, realizing she's about to step far deeper into this growing mess than intended.
业余侦探收起了懒洋洋的神色。

Añil: If they are indeed related, that is, if it truly is the Turquoise Serpent Club who's responsible, what will you do?
野木蓝：如果它们真的有联系——我是说，假如确实是绿松石蛇俱乐部所为，您会怎样做？

Ramona: I'm not paid to solve crimes. The Vigiles got plenty of investigators. If they can't figure things out, that's on them.
拉莫纳：那就不是我该担心的事情了。那些穿着制服和皮鞋、戴着徽章的家伙们，接下来是他们的工作。

Añil: Have to admire someone willing to go above and beyond their duty.
野木蓝：您是一个热心的人。

A gentle smile flickers across his face, then fades just as fast.
野木蓝面上浮现了温和的笑意。那点笑意很快像一阵风一样消失了。

Añil: But I must apologize, miss.
野木蓝：但很抱歉，拉莫纳小姐。

Añil: You know I work for the club. And you know that means I can't provide any help.
野木蓝：如您所言，我既然替俱乐部工作，就无法为您提供任何的帮助。

Ramona: That so? Didn't expect you to be so loyal.
拉莫纳：哪怕这份伪造的工作，并非是你自愿？

Ramona: If I remember, you used to be a plain old pigment supplier, until Pérez started snatching up every artist in town. I figured you didn't come into the job willingly. Was I wrong?
拉莫纳：野木蓝，没记错的话，你以前是一名颜料商人，和黑帮也没有什么瓜葛。自佩雷斯大规模绑架、威胁墨西哥城中的画家之后，你才开始替他伪造名画。

Ramona: You're not so different from those other artists, really. Just a little more flexible. That's why Pérez gave you a longer leash.
拉莫纳：和那些被绑架的画家一样，你不是自愿替俱乐部干活的。只是你更识时务一些，佩雷斯才没有把你一直关在俱乐部里。

Ramona: If they can prove Pérez guilty, the Vigiles will—
拉莫纳：如果能证实佩雷斯和这桩案件的关系，夜巡特遣会——

Añil: I'm afraid it's time for you to leave. This conversation will remain between us. You have my word.
野木蓝：请回吧，我会对今天的对话保密的。

Ramona: ...
拉莫纳：……

She rolls her eyes, though the emotion is as much directed at herself as Añil. Playing detective isn't in her job description.
女孩止住了话头，意识到自己方才的举动有些过于激动，甚至是愚蠢。

Ramona: Hm. Sorry for wasting your time. See you later.
拉莫纳：打扰你了，野木蓝。我该走了。

Añil: The Day of the Dead is coming.
野木蓝：亡灵节快到了。

Añil: Please, take some decorations with you. I wouldn't have you leave empty-handed.
野木蓝：回去的时候，请带上一些装饰品吧，这样也不算白跑一趟。

Ramona: Heh. Quite the salesman, huh? Still making deals even now.
拉莫纳：你是个出色的推销员，即使在这种时候也不忘工作。

She takes the candle with a smirk and tosses two Sharpodonties his way.
她嘲讽着接过对方递来的彩蜡烛，随手抛下两枚利齿子儿。

The little house in her dusty, long-forgotten cemetery, the quiet of her coffin—a familiar place to unwind after work.
熟悉的墓地，无人的空屋，破败的棺材，得以让疲惫的身心得到舒展。

—Usually. Tonight, however, she'll find no such rest.
——本该是这样的才对。

Loggerhead: Ms. Ramona, you're back! How'd your investigation go?
空脑袋：拉莫纳小姐，你回来了！调查进行得还顺利吗？

Loggerhead: The guy you went to see—is he a friend, or one of your informants? I knew there was something else going on.
空脑袋：你去找的那个人——对方是你的朋友吗，还是你在城市中布下的眼线？我就知道这一切没有这么简单。

Ramona: What're you still doing here?
拉莫纳：……你怎么还在这儿？

Ramona: When I said "Don't follow me," I didn't mean "Camp out at my place and jump me later."
拉莫纳：莱瑟姆小姐，我让你不要尾随我，并不是让你返回我的住所等我的意思。

Loggerhead: Oh, I know. It must have been one of your ghost buddies.
空脑袋：我知道了！你一定是去找你认识的鬼魂了——

Ramona: I don't have ghost buddies.
拉莫纳：我并不认识什么鬼魂。

Ramona: *sigh* It's the symbols we found at the crime scene: an arrow, a bow, and a net. They're the symbols of a powerful smuggling ring.
拉莫纳：我们在犯罪现场找到的那个符号，箭镞、弓与网，这是一个走私黑帮的代表符号。

Ramona: The Turquoise Serpent Club ... I know a guy who I thought was being forced to work for 'em.
拉莫纳：绿松石蛇俱乐部……而我正巧认识一个被迫为它工作的人。

Loggerhead: I see, I see. Then, everything's going well, then? Ha, lucky us.
空脑袋：原来如此，调查进展出乎意料地快，真是幸运！

Ramona: Don't get your gears all spun up.
拉莫纳：别高兴得太早了。

Ramona: He seems way too loyal for someone who's got a gun to his head. I got nothing from him, 'cept this thing.
拉莫纳：他不愿意为我提供俱乐部的信息，甚至还要我照顾他的生意。

Ramona: Take it. Saves you some souvenir shopping. Now beat it.
拉莫纳：这个送你，就当你来一趟墨西哥的纪念品了。你该离开了。

She tosses the candle to Loggerhead, who scrambles to catch it.
她百无聊赖地将买来的蜡烛抛给了对方，空脑袋手忙脚乱地接住。

Loggerhead: Wow! What's this?
空脑袋：哇——！这是什么？

Ramona: Just a knick-knack.
拉莫纳：一点小玩意儿……

She pauses, watching as the candle emits a clear sign of arcane fluctuation, no surprise to her in a cemetery filled with spirits, yet it forces her to think.
她的话头突然顿住。在充斥着孤魂与亡灵的荒郊墓地之中，蜡烛引起的神秘学波动尤为明显。

Ramona: Wait. I've changed my mind.
拉莫纳：等等。我改变主意了。

Loggerhead: Huh?
空脑袋：咦……？！

Loggerhead has barely enough time to register the item in her hand before the exorcist snatches it back.
“纪念品”在她手上停留不过两秒，又被赠予的人收了回去。

She places the candle in her palm and studies it closely. Then she puts it away.
除灵师将彩蜡烛放在手心，再次仔细地端详着，随即，她将它收起。

Ramona: I take it back. He might not be as loyal to the club as I thought.
拉莫纳：我要收回那句话——他对俱乐部或许没有那么忠诚。

Loggerhead: What do you mean?
空脑袋：这是什么意思？

Ramona: I'll get you something else to take home.
拉莫纳：我会再给你买别的纪念品的。

23  “清洁注意事项” / Cleansing Notes

The "Necessary" Guide to Exorcism
“必要”的除灵指南

1. Agave

Handling Note: Mind your hands around the large leaves. They're sharp.
Practical Uses: Commonly used in brewing, medicine, and rituals. Its fermented sap produces pulque, a drink Mexicans call "the lifeblood of the land."
Exorcism Uses: Smear the sap on door frames and floors after a space has been "cleansed" to shut out any "unseen presences."

2. Epazote

No special handling notes.
Practical Uses: Aids digestion and can expel or kill intestinal parasites.
Exorcism Uses: After an exorcism, if "their presence" still lingers in the room, sprinkle some epazote. Let's just say it smells as "special" as it sounds.

3. Rue

No special handling notes.
Practical Uses: Commonly used to ward off evil. Can be worn on the body, brewed as tea, or sprinkled on the ground.
Exorcism Uses: The final step of an "exorcism," it helps with sealing against future incursions.

4. Copal

Caution: Make sure the smoke spreads throughout the entire room.
Practical Uses: A natural resin native to Central America, used in sacred rituals since the time of the Aztecs and the Maya. Now widely used during Día de los Fieles Difuntos as a medium to connect the living with the dead.
Exorcism Uses: As copal smoke rises, "they" find their way back to where they belong.

5. Rosemary

For the living only.
Practical Uses: Healing.
Exorcism Uses: If the "scene" feels wrong, or you're dealing with a survivor who's lost control, a little rosemary helps you find yourself again faster.
1.龙舌兰

使用注意事项：别被大叶子割伤了手。
现实用途：常用于酿酒、制药与各种仪式，其汁液发酵后制成的普逵酒被墨西哥人视作“土地的体液”。
除灵用法：把汁液涂抹于已“处理完毕”的空间，在门框、地面留下标记，将“看不见的存在”彻底隔绝在外。

2.墨西哥香菜（Epazote）

无特殊注意事项。
现实用途：可促进消化，能驱除或杀死肠道寄生虫。
除灵用法：除灵仪式完成后，如果房间里仍萦绕着“它们的气息”，那就撒点儿墨西哥香菜，毕竟，这玩意儿的味道和它的名字一样“特别”。

3.芸香

无特殊注意事项。
现实用途：常作驱邪用，可随身佩戴，也可泡水饮用，或直接洒在地上。
除灵用法：“除灵”的最后一道工序，意思是“这儿的净化已经结束了。”

4.科巴脂

需特别注意——得让烟雾扩散至整个房间。
现实用途：原产于中美洲的天然树脂，自阿兹特克与玛雅时期起便用于祭祀仪式，如今作为连通生者与亡者的媒介，在诸灵节中被广泛使用。
除灵用法：当科巴脂的烟雾自房子里升腾，“它们”便能重回归处。

5.迷迭香

仅限生者使用。
现实用途：疗愈。
除灵用法：若“现场”给人的感觉不对，或遇上情绪失控的幸存者，来点儿迷迭香，它会让你更快地找回自我。

24  美洲漫步 / Roaming in America

The night of the art auction.
艺术品拍卖会当晚。

It's already a quarter past twelve—fifteen minutes past their agreed meeting time. Yet the mysterious bandit is a no-show. Añil grows more anxious with every passing minute.
约定的十二点已过一刻，匪徒女士仍未露面，等候者心下越发焦躁。

Añil: ...
野木蓝：……

A click comes from the side door.
侧门传来“咔哒”的声响。

Añil: I thought ... Never mind.
野木蓝：我以为……算了。

Añil: Ms. Butterfly, you're late.
野木蓝：蝴蝶女士，您迟到了。

Lorentz Butterfly: Sorry, my "luck" always seems to lead me into trouble.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：抱歉，关键时刻，我的“好运气”总会让我遇上一些事情。

Lorentz Butterfly: Once, I was tracking down a woman in Barcelona—an arcanist who was dragged into some Critter lover's parade, I almost broke a leg.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：曾经，我在巴塞罗那追杀——尾随一名神秘学家，她被卷入魔精爱好者的游行队伍。为了找到她，我险些被撞断一条腿。

Lorentz Butterfly: And then in Casablanca, I was a split second from shooting my target dead. But the moment I pulled the trigger, he turned to stone. Turns out his old rival cast a petrifying incantation on him.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：还有一次，在卡萨布兰卡，我扣下扳机，子弹将要穿过目标的那颗头颅时，石化咒在他身上显现，他的仇家先我一步将他变成了一块石头。

Her apology is a half-hearted politeness. She pays him little mind as she stows away the badge she used to pick the lock.
没有诚意地道歉后，她将用来撬锁的基金会徽章收起。

Añil: You have quite the intriguing past.
野木蓝：您的过往非常精彩。

Lorentz Butterfly: Has the auction started?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：拍卖会已经开始了？

Añil: Yes. The guests have all arrived.
野木蓝：是的，宾客们俱已入场完毕了。

Lorentz Butterfly: Excellent. When the lights are all fixed on the stage, it'll be the perfect time to sneak in.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：好极了，聚光灯对准舞台，现在正是潜入的最佳时机。

Lorentz Butterfly: Though, I don't hear a sound. You sure you didn't take me to a funeral by accident?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：但我在这栋建筑里没有听见任何的声响，这里静得跟墓地一样。

Añil: This isn't the auction venue.
野木蓝：拍卖会并不在这儿举行。

The forger leads her toward the giant Sun Stone exhibit.
造假先生带领着身份不明的匪徒女士走至巨大的太阳石展品前。

Añil: After the Spanish conquered Tenochtitlán, the new archbishop buried the Sun Stone, a symbol of Aztec civilization, to erase their "pagan heresy."
野木蓝：西班牙人在攻陷特诺奇提特兰城后，大主教便将代表着阿兹特克文明的太阳石掩埋，以清除异教的存在。

Añil: The stone was rediscovered in 1790 during repairs to the Mexico City Cathedral.
野木蓝：1790年，人们在整修墨西哥城的主教座堂时，在大广场的地底下重新挖掘出这块巨型石刻。

Añil: Thus it's said that the Cathedral was built on the Templo Mayor of the Aztec civilization.
野木蓝：相传墨西哥城大都会大教堂，正是修建在昔日阿兹特克的马约神庙之上。

Lorentz Butterfly: Don't tell me this massive rock above me is the only genuine artifact in the building.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：不要告诉我现在悬在我头上的这一块大石头，是这栋建筑里唯一的真品。

Lorentz Butterfly: What will you say next? That you stole it from the museum?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：你们从博物馆里把它偷出来了？

Añil: Haha, of course not.
野木蓝：哈哈，当然不是……

Añil: It's merely another forgery to decorate the club. When foreign buyers feel immersed in an "exotic ancient civilization," they start to loosen their purse strings.
野木蓝：这不过是佩雷斯先生为俱乐部做的一些点缀罢了。当异国的买家们沉浸在“古文明”氛围中，往往出手就会变得阔绰。

Añil: I joined its production, not certain I was entirely successful in recreating its complex color.
野木蓝：我参与了这件仿制品的制作，尝试复原它复杂、斑斓的色彩。

Añil: This Sun Stone is the entrance to the auction.
野木蓝：这块阿兹特克太阳石，正是拍卖会的入口。

Añil: If you will, miss, I believe you know how to activate it.
野木蓝：请，蝴蝶女士。我相信您有启动它的办法。

25  古老日历 / Ancient Calendar

The Story of the Sun Stone
太阳石的故事

The Aztec Sun Stone is often called the "Aztec Calendar." But it's really more of a cosmic map carved in basalt—or, as the Aztecs understood it, a guide to how the world works.

The stone was unearthed in Mexico City in the 18th century. It measures over 3.5 meters across and weighs more than 20 tons. The center of the Sun Stone is Tōnatiuh, the god of the sun. His gaze is stern, his outstretched tongue carved to resemble a Tecpatl. The message is clear: offer the sun your most precious gift—your blood—or receive nothing in return.

The concentric rings around Tōnatiuh record the four "suns" that came before—one transformed into a jaguar and devoured the people, one engulfed the world in a hurricane, one rained fire from the heavens, and the last drowned the world in a massive flood. Each sun's reign ended in an apocalypse, and none of their subjects survived.

Moving outward, you'll find religious symbols, patterns marking the cycle of day and night, and colors representing celestial bodies ... They unfold layer by layer, like a cosmic machine disassembled and laid bare—precise, ancient, orderly. Together, they capture the Aztec understanding of the world: the universe resets, all things are reborn, and humanity is nothing more than a speck of dust within it.
阿兹特克太阳石常被称作“阿兹特克日历”。它其实更像一张刻在玄武岩上的宇宙结构图，抑或阿兹特克人所信奉的世界运行指南。

这块石头出土于18世纪的墨西哥城，现存国家人类学博物馆，直径超过3.5米，重量在20吨以上。太阳石的中心是太阳神托纳蒂乌，祂神情严肃，伸出的舌头和燧石刀十分相似——如果不把最宝贵的事物，也就是血献给太阳，那么太阳不会赐予你任何祝福。

围绕祂展开的同心圆，记录着此前已经沉没的四个“太阳”——一个被美洲虎吞噬，一个被飓风撕碎，一个被火焰焚毁，最后一个被洪水淹没。每个“太阳”的结局都是末日级别的灾难——没有任何一个“太阳”能稳定地延续下来。

再往外，你会瞧见宗教日符号、暗示昼夜交替循环的图形，以及代表天体的颜色……它们层层展开，如同一台被拆解后再摊开展示的宇宙机器——精密、古朴、有序。它们代表了阿兹特克人眼中的世界及他们对宇宙的理解——宇宙重启，万物新生，人类不过是其中相当细小的一粒微尘。

26  艺术乌托邦 · 1/2 / "Elysium" of Art · 1/2

A line of pure energy flows along the earth toward a green land deep in the valley—their destination.
纯净的能量沿着土地流动，流向山谷深处的绿地，此行的终点。

A flash of light cuts through the darkness.
黑暗中骤然亮起一束光。

The world spins.
天旋地转。

Añil: Urgh! Pull yourself together. Where are you?
野木蓝：呃啊……我得冷静些。这里是哪里？

Añil: Double vision, heart palpitations, loss of perception of time and space, inability to distinguish dreams from reality. These are all common side effects.
野木蓝：眼前出现重影，心悸，失去对时间与空间的感知，分不清现实和梦境，这些都是仪式常见的副作用……

Añil: Take three deep breaths and wait for them to pass. *panting* Take some medication distilled from agave.
野木蓝：深呼吸三次，静待神秘术的影响消失。必要时，可以服用龙舌兰草心蒸馏萃取的药物……

The rush of teleportation gives way to the faint buzz of the microphone and the chattering din of a lively room.
片刻后，嘈杂的麦克风声响与人群的欢呼一同涌入耳朵。

Art Auction
艺术品拍卖会

Añil: Here we are, the auction site, Ms. Butterfly.
野木蓝：我们到拍卖会现场了，蝴蝶女士。

Añil: Miss, are you alright? Miss?
野木蓝：蝴蝶女士，您还好吗？女士……？

But there's no one beside him. The mysterious intruder has already melted into the crowd.
而身侧的潜入者早已不见踪影。

Añil: ...
野木蓝：……

Añil: Seems I can only wish you good luck.
野木蓝：既然如此，那就祝贺您行动顺利了。

Onstage, the club's owner boasts through the speakers about the items at auction, yet curiously, remains hidden from view behind a tall screen.
舞台上，俱乐部的主人站在屏风后，介绍着拍卖会诱人的一切。

Mr. Pérez: Distinguished Pipiltin, my ancient noble guests—
佩雷斯先生：各位尊敬的“皮尔利”，古老的贵族们——

Mr. Pérez: Welcome to the Turquoise Serpent Club!
佩雷斯先生：欢迎来到绿松石蛇俱乐部！

Mr. Pérez: You, fine receivers of our invitation, are the descendants of gods and the true masters of the city! And so, tonight, we will have a feast of the arts, for arcanists only!
佩雷斯先生：手持邀请函的你们，是神明的后裔，也是这座城市真正的主人！
今夜是属于神秘学家的艺术盛宴！

The invitees burst into applause. Arcanist smugglers, gangsters, and government officials alike rub elbows on the club's dance floor.
底下受到邀请的神秘学家走私犯、黑帮与官员们爆发出热烈的喝彩。

Smuggler I: Masters of the city, huh? I like the sound of that. This guy knows what he's doing. Must have some good stuff to sell.
走私犯Ⅰ：哈哈，城市的主人，我喜欢这个说法。看来这场拍卖会里有不少好货。

Smuggler II: It's said that there's nothing he can't find or do within the confines of the city.
走私犯Ⅱ：听说在这座城市里，没有佩雷斯先生找不到的东西，也没有他做不到的事情。

Smuggler II: You have any idea of the money this guy made smuggling alchemical materials? Enough to make the people living in the Palace of Iturbide jealous, I'll say that!
走私犯Ⅱ：你知道他先前凭着走私炼金术材料在墨西哥城捞了多大一笔钱吗？就连坐在伊图尔维德宫里的那帮家伙都眼红了！

Smuggler II: Everyone here wants a slice of his pie.
走私犯Ⅱ：这个拍卖会里，就没有不想掺一脚的人。

Mr. Pérez: The club thanks you all for our many pleasant dealings over the years.
佩雷斯先生：在过去的时间里，俱乐部与诸位合作愉快。

Mr. Pérez: In good faith for our future cooperation, I have prepared a series of arcane artworks for your perusal, all as true as gold!
佩雷斯先生：为了延续这种友好的合作，我为在座的大家准备了一批如假包换的神秘学艺术品。

Mr. Pérez: For the fine ladies and gentlemen who are not interested in art, allow me to tell you a little secret. These paintings are only a ticket to the second half of our event.
佩雷斯先生：对艺术品不感兴趣的淑女与绅士们，也无需担心，这些画作不过是下半场的入场券罢了！

Mr. Pérez: Buy one piece, and you've won yourself a chance for deeper collaboration and even the opportunity to have a "face-to-face" chat with yours truly.
佩雷斯先生：只要拍下一件艺术品，咱们就有更多合作的机会，以及“面对面”谈话的空间。

Mr. Pérez: Ah, but perhaps I should not say "face." I must maintain some of my allure. Haha!
佩雷斯先生：当然，并不是真的“面对面”！谈话的时候得隔着屏风。

The audience laughs politely on cue.
观众们都发出配合的哄笑声。

Mr. Pérez: Now, to introduce our fabulous art pieces, please welcome the rising star of Cubism, a prodigy returning from his studies in France, the greatest painter of the Turquoise Serpent Club. I give you—Muriel!
佩雷斯先生：下面有请立体主义新秀，法国留学归来的明日之星，绿松石蛇俱乐部的大画家，穆列尔！由他亲自为大家介绍要竞拍的画作。

The first lot is carried onto the stage. At the same time, a boy is shoved in front of the microphone. Beneath the glaring lights, he looks pale and increasingly helpless.
第一幅竞拍品被运送至台上，同时，一名少年被推搡着，走至麦克风前。
刺眼的灯光下，他面色苍白，沉默而无措。

Muriel: ...
穆列尔：……

Mr. Pérez: Muriel, what are you doing? You're up!
佩雷斯先生：穆列尔！别傻站着！是你发言的时候了。

Muriel: It's called Non-Aristocratic.
穆列尔：……它叫《非贵族私产》。

Muriel: The title of this painting is Non-Aristocratic.
穆列尔：《非贵族私产》，是这幅画的名字。

He turns toward the auction table timidly. He attempts to gather his thoughts, causing his words to sputter out in broken fragments.
少年茫然地看向拍卖台，尝试厘清自己的思绪，但除了只言片语，他脑海中空无一物。

Muriel: It's inspired by an artist I greatly admire.
穆列尔：它来源于一位我崇敬的艺术家说过的话。

Muriel: "Works that exist only on easels, and all other art loved by intellectuals, are the private property of the aristocracy."
穆列尔：“那些仅存在于画架上的作品以及其他一切被知识分子钟爱的艺术，都是贵族私产。”

Muriel: Art belongs to the public. Unfortunately, I was unable to achieve this during my time in the club.
穆列尔：艺术……是属于公众的。很遗憾，在俱乐部的这段时间，我没有能力改变绘画的介质……

Muriel: Perhaps that's why I captured María, my tutor and friend, as she created this statue.
穆列尔：也许正因如此，我在画中记录下了我的导师、朋友玛利亚创作大型雕塑时的模样。

Muriel: Do you see this, Teresita? I know you're here.
穆列尔：特蕾莎，你看到了吗？我知道你就在这里……

He darts his eyes around the room, but no one seems to be paying any attention to his rambling.
少年的视线急切地在场地里逡巡，却没有人在意他颠三倒四的介绍。

The audience is swept up in the exciting news of this opportunity for cooperation.
所有参与者都被“合作”的喜讯淹没了，观众席上一片哗然。

Smuggler I: You heard that? This is a chance to work with him.
走私犯Ⅰ：你听到了，是合作的机会！

Smuggler I: If we get distributorship, we're in the big bucks.
走私犯Ⅰ：如果我们能得到地区的分销权……！

Smuggler II: Damn! Now everyone wants that lousy artwork. We need to get one no matter what.
走私犯Ⅱ：该死，那些狗屁“艺术品”变得抢手起来了。今晚无论如何，我们都要拍下其中一件。

Añil: Muriel seems even rougher than the last time I saw him.
野木蓝：……与上次俱乐部时所见相比，穆列尔的状态看起来更差了。

Muriel: Sorry, Mr. Pérez.
穆列尔：抱歉，佩雷斯先生。

Muriel: I don't recall what comes next.
穆列尔：我不记得接下来要说什么了。

Mr. Pérez: You idiot! I told you to concoct an interesting story, then describe all the expensive materials you used and what arcane skills were involved!
佩雷斯先生：蠢货！我不是告诉过你吗，编一个足够有趣的故事，然后介绍它用了什么昂贵材料，有什么特殊的神秘术！

Mr. Pérez: Get him off the stage, now! I won't have my auction ruined by some half-wit boy who's lost his mind.
佩雷斯先生：算了，赶紧把他带下去，别让他搞砸了我的拍卖会。这家伙离疯不远了。

The incident does little to dampen the audience's enthusiasm. The painting is soon auctioned off at a high price.
小小的插曲并没有影响观众的热情，画作很快被一位观众高价拍走。

Mr. Pérez: Congratulations to the fine lady who just won the bid for this great work!
佩雷斯先生：久等了，诸位。让我们恭喜刚刚拍下艺术品的女士！

Mr. Pérez: Our next item is a very special painting. Very, very special indeed.
佩雷斯先生：接下来的拍卖品，是一幅特殊的画作。非常、非常特殊。

Mr. Pérez: I'd love to share how I acquired such a fine piece, but I fear that might piss off a couple bigwigs, mm? So I'll keep it zipped, but let me tell you this: this next piece is more than just a painting.
佩雷斯先生：我很想告诉你们我是从哪儿得到它的，但过程可能会让一些穿制服的大人物们不高兴。因此我只能告诉你们：它不仅仅是一幅画作。

The club's owner revels in the suspense before theatrically calling for the next lot.
俱乐部主人卖足了关子，才将竞拍品送上台来。

Lady by the Lake: Oh, my! Thank goodness!
《湖边的女人》：噢，天啊。谢天谢地！

Lady by the Lake: My prayers have been heard. The soundproof fabric has finally been removed!
《湖边的女人》：祈祷奏效了……终于有人将我头上的隔音布掀开了！！

Lady by the Lake: I-I'll stay quiet this time; you have my word. In fact, I'll be utterly silent unless spoken to.
《湖边的女人》：我保证，以后会尽量少说话，至少在大家不想听的时候闭嘴……

Smuggler I: An Awakened!
走私犯Ⅰ：意识唤醒者！

Smuggler II: What am I looking at exactly? Is that a face?
走私犯Ⅱ：它长得可真抽象。这是一张人脸吗？

Onstage, the painting mutters to herself.
舞台上，重见天日的画作女士紧张地自言自语，尚未意识到身为拍卖品的命运。

Lady by the Lake: Ah, this light is rather dazzling. I feel a little faint.
《湖边的女人》：我的头很晕……这里的灯光太刺眼了。

Lady by the Lake: I must say, this really isn't the right sort of place to store a painting.
《湖边的女人》：要我说，这儿实在不是一个适合存放画作的环境。

Lady by the Lake: Wait, am I on a stage? Why are you all looking at me?
《湖边的女人》：等等，我是在舞台上吗？为什么底下的人都盯着我？

Lady by the Lake: What on earth is going on here?
《湖边的女人》：究竟发生了什么……？！

Moments earlier, Lorentz Butterfly slipped away from Añil and into the crowd; now in short order, she finds herself backstage.
片刻之前，在神秘术后遗症仍困扰着野木蓝时，杀手女士早已潜入秘密的后台。

Lorentz Butterfly: The air here feels different.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：空气的味道和之前相比不同了。

Lorentz Butterfly: It's moist. This place is full of plants.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：略带湿润的气息……看来我们正处于植被茂密的地带。

Lorentz Butterfly: Not enough to gather the specifics of the location, but we've gone quite some way from central Mexico City.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：虽然不能确定具体的位置，但这里离墨西哥城的城市中心，一定有相当的距离。

Lorentz Butterfly: Explains why I never found much there.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：怪不得之前在城市内的搜寻，并不能找到拍卖会的场地所在。

She slinks along the empty hallway.
她沿着无人的走廊向前走去。

Patroller III: Auction's started. Mr. Pérez has taken the stage.
巡逻者Ⅲ：开始了。佩雷斯先生上台了。

Patroller III: *sigh* Wish I was sitting down there. I hear they're giving out unlimited drinks. I could use a tequila.
巡逻者Ⅲ：真羡慕那些在前头参加拍卖会的人，听说场地里有喝不完的龙舌兰……

Patroller IV: "Taken the stage," hah. He's still hiding behind that screen.
巡逻者Ⅳ：那不是“上台”。他只是躲在屏风后说话。

Patroller III: What?
巡逻者Ⅲ：什么？

Patroller IV: I said what I said. Don't you think it's strange?
巡逻者Ⅳ：我说佩雷斯先生。你不觉得很奇怪吗？

Patroller IV: Ever since that fire last month, he hasn't shown his face.
巡逻者Ⅳ：自从一个月前的那场火灾后，他就再也没有在人前出现过了。

Patroller III: Hmm. You didn't hear? Mr. Pérez thinks it was an inside job.
巡逻者Ⅲ：你没听说吗？佩雷斯先生认为，那场火是自己人放的。

Patroller III: There's no way someone else got into the gallery that night. It could only have been one of his higher-ups.
巡逻者Ⅲ：在那天晚上，根本不可能有外人能进入画廊内。有嫌疑的人选只有俱乐部高层。

Patroller III: Still hasn't sniffed out the rat, though, so he's being extra careful for now.
巡逻者Ⅲ：至今为止，佩雷斯先生都没能抓到那名内鬼。自火灾之后，他就变得十分谨慎了。

Patroller IV: Yeah, what do I look like, a moron? Of course I know! But what I'm saying is, he hasn't talked to anyone face-to-face. No one! Luisa, Javier, Pablo ... They can't all be traitors!
巡逻者Ⅳ：我当然知道你说的这些！我的意思是，所有人。包括他的心腹，路易莎、哈维尔、巴勃罗……他都不再用真面目见他们。难道所有人都是内鬼吗？

Patroller IV: Leila said she heard some weird sounds coming from Mr. Pérez's room when she was on night patrol. More than once.
巡逻者Ⅳ：听晚上巡逻的蕾拉说，曾经有好几次，在路过佩雷斯先生的房间时，她都听见了一些奇怪的动静。

Patroller IV: She said it sounded like some kind of scurrying thing was moving inside the walls.
巡逻者Ⅳ：就像是有什么怪物，在墙后奔跑一样。

Patroller III: A monster in the walls?
巡逻者Ⅲ：墙中的怪物……？

Patroller IV: I'll bet he got burned bad in that fire and doesn't want anyone to see.
巡逻者Ⅳ：更有可能的是，在那场火灾里，他受了很重的伤，而且不想让人知道。

Patroller IV: Hey, I dare you to sneak backstage and find out.
巡逻者Ⅳ：你有没有胆子偷偷潜入后台，看看怎么回事？

Patroller III: You crazy? I'm staying put right here. There's no bet worth risking my neck.
巡逻者Ⅲ：嘿，嘿！打住，原来你说这么多，就是为了这个！我可不想看不见明天的太阳！

???: I could do you both the favor—find out what happened to him.
？？？：我可以帮你们去看看，那个家伙身上发生了什么。

Whether taking her as an attendee or a guard they don't recognize, the two of them momentarily don't think past the helpful offer.
热心助人的女士从躲藏的角落中走出。

Patroller III: No kidding. Hah. You'd do that? That'd be great!
巡逻者Ⅲ：真的吗？那太好了，谢谢你！

Patroller IV: You should really be calling him "Mr. Pérez" here in the club though.
巡逻者Ⅳ：容我提醒，在俱乐部里，请尊称他为“佩雷斯先生”，而不是“那个家伙”！

Patroller IV: Wait, who are you?
巡逻者Ⅳ：等等，你是谁？
