28  艺术乌托邦 · 2/2 / "Elysium" of Art · 2/2

Lorentz Butterfly: Don't worry, my friends.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：放心吧，朋友们。

Lorentz Butterfly: I'll figure it all out for you.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：你们最关心的问题，我会帮你们弄清楚的。

A shame, really. The two patrollers she put down can't exactly say thank you.
很可惜，面前两名不省人事的巡逻者再也无法出声言谢了。

She steps over her new "friends" and heads toward the club's backstage.
她绕开新结识的“朋友”们，走向拍卖会的后台。

Backstage Corridor
后台走廊

Mr. Pérez: A talking painting, you say? Don't let your eyes fool you! This is an arcanist!
佩雷斯先生：……那就让我告诉你们：它不仅仅是一幅画作。它还是一位神秘学家！

Mr. Pérez: An Awakened from the Bateau-Lavoir, in Montmartre, Paris!
佩雷斯先生：一幅在巴黎蒙玛尔特的“流动洗衣房”诞生的画作！

Lorentz Butterfly: Hm?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：……嗯？

Lorentz Butterfly: What?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这是？

There's no one behind the screen.
出乎意料的是，她清晰地瞧见，舞台上的屏风后空无一人。

Lorentz Butterfly: What's going on here?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：什么鬼……？

Yet the nonexistent "club owner" continues hawking their art as if nothing had happened.
与此同时，不存在的“俱乐部主人”仍在热情洋溢地推销着来源不明的艺术品。

"Mr. Pérez": This is the artist's understanding of the essence of the world: a reconstruction of reality, the beauty of an unconventional brushstroke!
“佩雷斯先生”：这是画家对事物本质的理解。对真实世界的重构，反传统的几何之美！

"Mr. Pérez": And, perhaps what may interest you much more: we have discovered that within the frame of this painting, there is a vast pocket of hidden space!
“佩雷斯先生”：更重要的是，我们发现，这幅画作的内部，存在着一个巨大的空间！

Lorentz Butterfly scans the room, finding a small, unremarkable machine off to the side.
她看向一旁不起眼的小巧机器。

Codes, a translator, a speaker ... The learned member of the Institutum Lorentz identifies their purpose at a glance.
密码条、转译器、发声元件……博学的洛伦兹研究所成员很快辨认出它的功效。

"Mr. Pérez": A space where you might store whatever you like, so long as it isn't breathing.
“佩雷斯先生”：你可以在这里储存任何东西，只要它是无生命之物。

"Mr. Pérez": Conspicuous arcane materials, controlled items, or even your rival's severed head!
“佩雷斯先生”：无论是引人注意的神秘学材料，还是受当局政府控制的危险物品，甚至是——对手的尸体！

"Mr. Pérez": The greatest of all secret safes, not simply behind the painting, but rather, inside the painting!
“佩雷斯先生”：多好的秘密仓库：没人想到你会把东西藏在画里！

Lorentz Butterfly: An audio production machine, a cupboard, and—
洛伦兹蝴蝶：一台会说话的转译机器，一张纸板，以及——

A faint noise emerges from the pile of notes.
纸条堆中传来微弱的动静。

???: Who is this woman?
？？？：这个女人是谁——！！！

???: And how did she get back here? For God's sake, get her out of here!
？？？：她是怎么进来后台的！该死，得想办法把她赶出去！

Lorentz Butterfly: Ha. Seems I've found the real Mr. Pérez.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：哈，看来我找到真正的佩雷斯“先生”了。

29  有人在周围走动 / Alguien que anda por ahí

The teleportation ritual drops them midair, leaving Loggerhead to tumble to the ground in a clatter of metal.
传送仪式在半空中结束，她摔了下来，发出属于金属碰撞的闷响。

The auction has just begun. Impassioned words from behind the screen heat up the atmosphere, drawing every eye to the stage.
拍卖会刚刚开始，屏风后传出的激昂陈词让气氛变得热烈，所有客人的目光都聚焦在舞台上。

Ramona: Get up and quit quivering.
拉莫纳：站起来，冷静一些。

Loggerhead: Oh, right, right. We need to look like regular guests here.
空脑袋：我明白了，拉莫纳小姐，我们应该假装是这里的常客。

Loggerhead: So we take a seat, cheer and clap with the others, raise a paddle to bid some small amount that nothing will ever sell for.
空脑袋：找一个空位置，跟着大家一起欢呼、鼓掌，然后在合适的时机举牌，出一个绝对不会被成交的价格。

Loggerhead: That way, we won't catch too much attention. Just do enough to slip by! Reminds me of a movie from 1928.
空脑袋：这样既不会引起注意，也不用担心显得太过格格不入！就像1928年上映的——

Ramona: Shut it. Just shut it. I swear if you bring up another movie plot, I will sock you.
拉莫纳：闭嘴。不要再提起那些你从电影里看来的情节了。

She only intended to get the camera-headed girl's nerves in check, but her sharp words catch the attention of a guest nearby.
本被用作缓解紧张的言语，却引起了身侧宾客的注意。

Gentle Guest: Pardon me, ladies, but have you never attended an auction before?
温和的宾客：女孩们，你们先前从来没有参加过拍卖会吗？

Loggerhead: Um, no. I mean, we kinda know what's going on, but ...
空脑袋：呃，不是……我们对这些都很熟悉，只是……

Ramona: Don't you worry yourself over us, miss.
拉莫纳：女士，我想我们的情况与您无关。

Gentle Guest: Oh, don't get me wrong. I mean no offense. Actually, this is my first time too.
温和的宾客：噢，别紧张，我没有恶意。事实上，我也是初次参加这样的场合。

Gentle Guest: Not so brushed up on the rules myself, let alone the argots.
温和的宾客：对于拍卖会台面下的规矩，我也不甚了解。更别提那些大家约定俗成的暗话……

The woman turns to them with a gentle smile. She fidgets with her handkerchief, looking a little lost.
搭话的女士朝她们露出了温和的微笑，她捏着手绢，看上去有些无措。

Ramona: Then what brings a lady like you here?
拉莫纳：既然如此，像您这样的女士，又为何出现在这里？

Ramona: You were invited like everyone else, right? You must know you're on gang turf.
拉莫纳：这里是神秘学家黑帮的地盘，作为宾客的你不可能不了解。

Gentle Guest: Yes, miss, that I do know. However, I have my reasons for coming.
温和的宾客：我当然知晓俱乐部的真面目。只是——我有不得不参加拍卖会的理由。

Gentle Guest: Camila, my daughter, suffers from a rare disease. Her body rejects its own arcane power.
温和的宾客：我的女儿卡米拉，她得了一种很罕见的病，这使她的神秘学力量与她的身体互斥。

Gentle Guest: Her blood is escaping her body. There's nothing we can do to stop it.
温和的宾客：她的血液日夜逃出她的身体，无论做什么都无法阻止。

Gentle Guest: I have visited every arcane doctor in the city, and not one of them has a clue. They take it as a horrible and unbreakable curse. My dear Camila, there's not much time left for her.
温和的宾客：我求遍了城市里的神秘学医生，但没有人知道是怎么回事，这是一种可怕的、无解的诅咒，留给卡米拉的时间已经不多了。

Loggerhead: Oh, I'm so sorry, ma'am.
空脑袋：天啊，女士，我很抱歉。

Gentle Guest: It's alright, truly. On one of my desperate visits to priests and witches while I was searching after a rumor about a miracle cure, I happened upon some smugglers.
温和的宾客：没关系。在我拜访祭司和女巫、寻找罕见药物的过程中，我认识了一些走私商。

Gentle Guest: They told me I'd find what I was looking for at the Turquoise Serpent Club.
温和的宾客：他们告诉我，绿松石蛇俱乐部一定能找到我想要的东西。

Gentle Guest: And now, it seems spending just a few pesos on some art could win me a chance to strike a deal with the biggest smuggler in the city. As long as I find a phoenix feather, there is still hope for my dear child.
温和的宾客：而如今，只要拍下一件艺术品，我就有机会和本地最大的走私商做交易。如果能够找到祭司所说的凤凰羽毛，我的孩子就康复有望了……

The woman finds herself getting increasingly worked up. Realizing she's on the verge of tears, she stops herself.
像是终于得以倾诉，她显得有些激动。

Gentle Guest: Oh, where are my manners? I'm talking your ears off.
温和的宾客：抱歉，孩子们，我本不该说这么多的。

Gentle Guest: You seem quite unlike the other guests here.
温和的宾客：只是，你们看起来和大多数来参与拍卖会的宾客都不一样。

Gentle Guest: We've all come here with a purpose. Some of us are desperate to achieve those goals by any means necessary. Please be careful.
温和的宾客：来到这里的人，每个人都有自己的目的，有人走投无路，也有人会不择手段。容我提醒，你们千万要小心。

Ramona: Same to you, ma'am. Hope your daughter gets better soon.
拉莫纳：您也是，女士。希望您的女儿早日康复。

Gentle Guest: Thank you very much. Hm? Is it raining outside, or did a leak just spring? It suddenly smells damp.
温和的宾客：谢谢你。奇怪，外头是下雨了吗？还是拍卖会哪里漏水呢？我感觉到了水的气息……

In the dim room, she clutches her handkerchief tight as her breathing grows heavy.
昏暗的会场中，她紧攥着自己的手绢，呼吸变得剧烈起来。

Gentle Guest: I ... I ...
温和的宾客：我……我……

Gentle Guest: *gasp* What's happening to me?
温和的宾客：呼啊……孩子……这是怎么回事……

Loggerhead: She-She can't breathe!
空脑袋：她好像……她不能呼吸了！

The camera-headed girl reaches out to steady the woman, but Ramona seizes her arm.
机械女孩想要伸手扶住对方，却被除灵师死死抓住了手臂。

Ramona: Don't touch her! Something's off here.
拉莫纳：别碰她，她的情况有些不对劲！

Ramona: The arcane power squeezing her is too strong! It'll suck you in if you get too close!
拉莫纳：她身上盘踞着一股强大的神秘术能量。如果你靠得太近，你也会被卷入其中的——！

Gentle Guest: I see a lake ... water ... and something below ... It's strange and so very deep ...
温和的宾客：我看见了，水底的……湖……

Gentle Guest: Ah ... Oh ... Camila ...
温和的宾客：啊……啊……卡米拉……

Her gentle features twist in agony as she chokes for air. Rough gasps escape her throat like a broken bellows.
缺氧的痛苦使她温和的面目扭曲，她的喉咙发出濒死的“嗬嗬”声，像一座破损的风箱。

Then, all at once, she stops breathing.
几乎是瞬间，她就停止了呼吸。

Loggerhead: No, no, no! Don't! Ma'am! What on earth is going on?!
空脑袋：不不不！别这样……女士……！发生了什么……？！

She collapses to the floor, drawing the attention of those nearby. The crowd draws a wide, panicked circle around her.
失去意识的身体摔到地上，引起了周围人的注意，一时间骚动四起。

Smuggler III: Oh! What's happening over there?
走私犯Ⅲ：什么动静？

Smuggler IV: Someone's fallen! My God. She's ... She's not breathing!
走私犯Ⅳ：怎么有人倒下了？！神啊，她……她没有呼吸了！

Smuggler IV: Murder! Murder!!
走私犯Ⅳ：有人，有人谋杀！！！

Panic spreads through the room.
惊呼立刻传向四面八方，引起海浪一般的恐慌。

Soon enough, every eye in the room turns toward the exorcist and the camera-headed girl.
顷刻间，全场的目光都定格在除灵师与机械女孩身上，犹如灼烧的火焰。

Ramona: Drowned without a drop of water, just like all the others.
拉莫纳：溺水窒息死亡，然而现场却没有一滴水迹——和那起神秘学连环命案的特征一模一样。

Ramona: The murderer must be someone in the auction house.
拉莫纳：那个人——凶手一定就在现场。

Ramona: The question is, who?
拉莫纳：凶手到底是谁？

???: You filthy murderer!
？？？：你这个该死的凶手！

???: Dirty thug!
？？？：不怀好意的歹徒！

???: Intruder! Bandit! Robber!
？？？：闯入者！绑匪！强盗！

Backstage Corridor
后台走廊

Lorentz Butterfly: This room's a bit small for all five, wouldn't you say?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这里可没这么多人。

The unbothered murderer, thug, intruder, bandit, and robber raises an eyebrow.
毫不介意的凶手、歹徒、闯入者、绑匪、强盗女士挑了挑眉。

Lorentz Butterfly: Are you finished with your whining? It's time for you to choose your interrogation method.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：说完了吗？是时候选择你喜欢的审讯方式了。

Lorentz Butterfly: Good news: You're in safe hands. I'm quite skilled at peeling people while keeping them alive.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：好消息：我的医学手艺还不错——能在完整扒下一张皮的时候，让受审者还活着。

Lorentz Butterfly: Bad news: I've never tried these techniques on a mouse!
洛伦兹蝴蝶：坏消息：我从来没在老鼠身上试验过！

Lorentz Butterfly: How about something more sleek, modern. Electrocution, perhaps?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：或者来点简单、现代、高效的审讯方式如何，比如说电击？

She mutters to herself as she rummages through her case.
审讯者自言自语，开始在随身携带的箱子中翻找起来。

Before her, the mice huddle together in a trembling ball. At last, the largest of them squeaks out.
在她面前，老鼠们害怕地瑟缩成一团。终于，其中一只老鼠鼓起勇气来与她交涉。

Mamá Pérez: S-Stop! What kind of interrogation is this?!
佩雷斯妈妈：停、停下！怎么会有这样的审讯？！

Mamá Pérez: You haven't even asked a question! This is pure torture!
佩雷斯妈妈：你甚至没有提出任何一个问题，就要先行动手！

Lorentz Butterfly: Given your connection to the murders in the city, I think the means justify the ends.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：考虑到最近城中的多起命案，都似乎和你这位俱乐部幕后老板有关，我略施手段也无可厚非。

Mamá Pérez: You think I know which ones you're talking about? The club makes trouble "disappear" every week!
佩雷斯妈妈：什么命案？我记不清了……！俱乐部每个星期都要处理一大堆碍事的家伙！

Mamá Pérez: But that's just how things go in this business!
佩雷斯妈妈：但我发誓，那都是黑帮之间，正常解决问题的方式！

Mamá Pérez: So please, Ms. Giant, please calm down! I'll tell you whatever you want. Just ask me!
佩雷斯妈妈：两条腿的女士，你冷静一些，你究竟想要知道些什么，我都可以告诉你！

Lorentz Butterfly: Alright, then I have some questions.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：哦，那你说吧。我听着。

She reluctantly puts away her tools, eyeing the mice with a half-smile.
她有些遗憾地收起那些用途不明的道具，似笑非笑地看着老鼠窝。

Lorentz Butterfly: How did you become "Mr. Pérez," and what is the connection between the club and Manus Vindictae, and—
洛伦兹蝴蝶：关于你们是如何成为“佩雷斯先生”，绿松石蛇俱乐部和重塑之手的关系，以及——

Lorentz Butterfly: Where do you ship your special mineral pigment?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我要知道，一种矿物颜料的下落。

Lorentz Butterfly: I need the type you smuggle for the Manus. Give me all you have. Every ounce.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：那种你们协助重塑之手走私的颜料，把它交出来。一盎司都不能少。

Mamá Pérez: That pigment? You're after it too.
佩雷斯妈妈：矿物颜料？难道你也在找——

The eager mouse representative is cut off.
老鼠代表急切的话语被打断了。

Something seems to have gone wrong in the audience. Screams and frantic footsteps follow.
台下的观众席似乎出了什么事故，传来人们慌乱的尖叫与脚步声。

Patroller V: Mr. Pérez! Mr. Pérez!
巡逻者Ⅴ：不好了，佩雷斯先生！！

Patroller V: A guest has just died in the auction house! Can't believe someone had the guts to off her on our turf!
巡逻者Ⅴ：现场有人出事了！该死，竟然有人敢在我们的地盘上对来宾动手。

Patroller V: Wasn't any ordinary death, either. Folks are saying it's another of those serial killings!
巡逻者Ⅴ：那名宾客的死状有些奇怪，简直就像最近在疯传的那起连环案件一样……！

Patroller V: The whole place is in chaos. We've got guests asking us to open the teleportation ritual so they can leave. Ms. Luisa wants to know your orders.
巡逻者Ⅴ：现在所有人都乱作一团，不少贵宾要求我们打开传送通道，放他们离开，路易莎女士让我来询问您的意见……

Patroller V: Wait, what are you two doing lying around here? You're supposed to be on duty!
巡逻者Ⅴ：等等，你们两个负责巡逻的家伙，怎么躺在这里？

In the backstage hallway, the unconscious patrollers are discovered.
后台走廊处，昏迷不醒的守卫被传话者发现了。

Lorentz Butterfly: A sixth case, then.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：第六起案件——就在刚刚。

Lorentz Butterfly: This should be interesting.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这下有意思了。

30  噤声尖叫 / Scream

The reluctant detective strains to recall every detail amid the chaos.
一片混乱中，非职业侦探竭尽全力回忆着刚刚所经历的一切细节。

Ramona: The murderer's gotta be somewhere in the auction house. That's the only way they could control their arcane skill so exactly.
拉莫纳：凶手一定就在拍卖会的现场，才能精确地控制神秘术施展的时机。

Loggerhead: Golly. You don't mean to say that the murderer's somewhere in this room?
空脑袋：你的意思是，凶手就在我们附近……？

Her reels skip in shock, nearly coming out of place.
机械少女不存在的心跳骤然一停。

Ramona: The ritual might've been set ahead of time. All they had to do was wait for their prey to fall into the trap.
拉莫纳：凶手提前布置好了与谋杀有关的仪式，只等目标到达拍卖会——

Ramona: Prey? Right. The killer must have a reason for this, right? But what?
拉莫纳：目标……对，凶手一定有挑选受害者的标准。只是，这个标准究竟是什么……

Loggerhead: She was here for the auction. Oh, heck! They must be killing off club members!
空脑袋：刚刚的女士是来参加拍卖会的宾客……我知道了，受害者一定都是俱乐部的人！

Ramona: Please, I'm still thinking things through.
拉莫纳：……请你不要打断我的思路，莱瑟姆小姐。

Ramona: If all the victims were known club members, the Vigiles wouldn't have been running around in circles. It's too obvious.
拉莫纳：如果受害者都来自绿松石蛇俱乐部，如此明显的线索，执夜队不可能没有发现。

Ramona: I may not like them very much, but those Vigiles aren't half as thick as the Foundation. Some of them aren't even half-bad.
拉莫纳：虽然我与执夜人们并不熟悉，但他们不是像基金会职员一样的傻蛋……好吧，至少小部分不是。

Loggerhead: Hey! We aren't dummies either.
空脑袋：基金会的人们当然不是——

Her protest isn't noted as Ramona carries on.
除灵师并未理会空脑袋的反驳。

Ramona: But there's one more question that gotta be answered.
拉莫纳：真正需要解决的问题还有一个。

Ramona: What is this ritual exactly? The energy around it was huge, but they were able to cast it without anyone noticing. That takes true skill.
拉莫纳：这个仪式，究竟是何种术法？它的灵性是如此磅礴而扭曲，却能够在没有引起任何人注意的情况下完成，如此精妙，如此不可思议——

Ramona: There's gotta be some clues around here!
拉莫纳：线索一定就在现场！

She looks around. Chairs lie on their sides. People rush for the exits. Lights flicker and shake.
她环视着四周。翻倒的座椅，匆忙逃窜的人群，不停闪烁的光线。

The air is filled with curses, screams, and twisted pneuma, while a bitter, biting wind scratches at her senses. She remains clueless.
会场充斥着脏话、尖叫声、扭曲的灵，如同最苦涩、阴冷的风穿过她的感官……毫无头绪。

A smuggler's sudden accusation only makes the situation worse.
而围观者的发言让她们落入了更糟糕的境地。

Smuggler III: Hey, you two. Yeah, you! I saw what you did!
走私犯Ⅲ：你们两个……我刚刚看到你们两个了！

Loggerhead: You pointing at us, sir?
空脑袋：这位先生，你是在指着我们吗？

Loggerhead: It-It wasn't us.
空脑袋：不、不是我们做的……！

Smuggler III: Don't act like you're innocent.
走私犯Ⅲ：别装得好像什么都不知道的样子。

Smuggler III: You were talking to her just before she died.
走私犯Ⅲ：在那个人出事之前，我看到你们一直在围着她说话！

Smuggler III: You must have killed her. You're from the Foundation. You're here to kill us all so you can take over the underground arcane materials racket.
走私犯Ⅲ：是你们下的手吧，基金会的人！你们想要控制墨西哥城的地下神秘学材料市场，把我们全部都杀死！

Ramona & Loggerhead: ...
拉莫纳&空脑袋：……

Ramona gives up her train of thought to deal with the escalating situation.
还未成型的推理被打断了。

Ramona: See? This is why I didn't want you tagging along, Metal Head.
拉莫纳：莱瑟姆，我果然不该带上你。

Loggerhead: Hey, that's not fair. I've helped you a lot, like, like, um ...
空脑袋：你不能这样！一路上，我可帮了你不少的忙。比如说，比如说……呃。

Loggerhead: You're not gonna leave me behind, are you?
空脑袋：你应该没有在偷偷计划把我丢下吧……

Smuggler III: Guards! These two right here! Take them down!
走私犯Ⅲ：守卫，快、快把她们抓住！

Smuggler III: You're trapped in the Turquoise Serpent Club now. There's no escape.
走私犯Ⅲ：这里可是绿松石蛇俱乐部。你们休想走出这里一步！

Ramona: I hate fighting!
拉莫纳：我讨厌运动……！！！

Another attacker falls. Out of breath, she lowers the exorcism tool she's been using as a makeshift weapon.
又一个偷袭者倒下，女孩气喘吁吁地放下手中被充作武器的除灵工具。

Ramona: *pant* I'm not a fan of making new crime scenes, especially when I'm the one that's going to have to take care of them later.
拉莫纳：比起制造犯罪现场，我更擅长的是处理它。

Ramona: We gotta get out of here before we draw any more attention. Latham! Hey, Metal Head!
拉莫纳：我们得趁引起更多的注意前，马上离开这里。莱瑟姆——莱瑟姆！

Loggerhead doesn't respond.
被呼唤的机械女孩毫无回应。

Instead, her attention is drawn to a faint cry for help coming from the stage.
事实上，她被舞台上微弱的呼救声吸引了全部注意力。

Lady by the Lake: Help!
《湖边的女人》：救命——！

Lady by the Lake: Please, my dear clubmates, do not leave me here all alone!
《湖边的女人》：求你们了，亲爱的俱乐部伙伴们，不要把我丢在这儿！

Lady by the Lake: Take me away. Throw me back to the deepest, darkest corner of the warehouse. I promise, I'll not say a word about it.
《湖边的女人》：把我带回去吧，哪怕是重新放回仓库最里头也可以……我保证不多说什么反对的话！

Loggerhead: That painting, she's an Awakened.
空脑袋：它，她是一位意识唤醒者……

The club has descended into bedlam, with no one around to pay any attention to the increasingly frantic painting.
罪犯们忙于戒备，没有任何一人搭理这幅聒噪的作品。

Lady by the Lake: Oh, Badía, I know you!
《湖边的女人》：嘿，我知道你，巴迪亚！

Lady by the Lake: You remember me, don't you? The painting from the northwest corner of the warehouse. Next to Antonia and the Cactus Cat.
《湖边的女人》：你还记得我吗，我是放在西北边角落的那幅画！就在《安东尼娅与仙人掌猫》隔壁！

Lady by the Lake: A grubby corner, I must say, haunted, even. None dare walk it but you. You always volunteer to patrol the area, so the others call you a freak.
《湖边的女人》：由于你平时抢着去仓库区巡逻，他们私下都说你是怪咖。没有人爱干这个活儿，因为仓库区从没打扫过，而且总是闹鬼。

Lady by the Lake: But I know why. It's so that you can write those letters to your love in private.
《湖边的女人》：但我知道，你喜欢躲在仓库的角落里给女朋友写情书……

Patroller II: Ah. Zip it, you overgrown doodle! I don't wanna hear another word!
巡逻者Ⅱ：够了，闭嘴！不要再说了！

In the dim light and madness, the auction stage seems like an island of refuge.
昏暗的灯光下，用作展示拍卖品的舞台犹如一座孤岛。

Loggerhead: Can we take her with us?
空脑袋：我们可以把她一起带走吗？

Ramona: Her? You mean the painting?
拉莫纳：她？那幅拍卖品？

Ramona: You outta your mind? She's in the middle of the stage with a goddamn spotlight on her. We may as well get up there and say, "Hey, come get us!"
拉莫纳：你疯了吗，那里可是舞台的正中心。只要我们站上去，马上就会成为整个现场的焦点。

Ramona: And dying for a blabbermouth painting is not worth what I get paid.
拉莫纳：我的工作可不包括去送死。

Loggerhead: But we can't just leave her there!
空脑袋：但我们不能就这样，把她丢在这里。

Loggerhead: She isn't just some arcane tool. She's a living arcanist! She doesn't deserve to be bought and sold like an object!
空脑袋：她不是什么秘密仓库，她是一位神秘学家！她不应该被这样四处贩卖——！

Loggerhead charges onto the platform.
《湖边的女人》：摄像头女孩冲上了展台。

A gunshot rings out. A bullet skims across her metallic body.
一声枪响，子弹与她的钢铁身躯擦肩而过。

Ramona: Urgh!
拉莫纳：唔……！

Ramona: ¡Desvío   Neumático!
拉莫纳：灵性移转！

Loggerhead: Ramona, don't worry about me.
空脑袋：拉莫纳，你不用担心我！

Loggerhead: I'm an Awakened. So, they can't hurt me so easily, at least not with normal bullets.
空脑袋：我是意识唤醒者，不会轻易受伤的！至少这些普通的子弹伤害不了我！

Ramona: ...
拉莫纳：……

Ramona: Heh, guess I forgot it for a second, like I forgot how impulsive you can be.
拉莫纳：刚刚有一瞬间忘记了这件事。就像你的一时冲动一样。

Ramona: At least tell me you've got a plan.
拉莫纳：希望你的冲动有更详细的规划。

Ramona: What are you gonna do next? I'll cover you.
拉莫纳：告诉我接下来你准备做什么。我会掩护你。

Loggerhead: Oh, hey! We're like those partners in the detective movies.
空脑袋：噢！我们这样就像侦探片里的搭档一样——

Loggerhead: *exhale* Focus, Latham. You're a seasoned Foundation employee, an outstanding senior member of the Arcanum Containment Department.
空脑袋：呼……集中注意力，莱瑟姆。你是成熟的基金会职员，神秘学藏品管理部的优秀前辈！

Loggerhead: I grabbed a load of arcane tools before I left. I've gotta have something useful.
空脑袋：在出发之前，我携带了数十样神秘学道具，一定有某样可以派上用场……

Loggerhead: My reels, film developer, Critter-Be-Gone potion, recording plant, infinitely looping drugstore receipt, Encyclopedia of Mexico, seasoning packets, hand-cranked rechargeable battery ...
空脑袋：胶片、显影液、驱魔精药水、录音植物、无限购物小票、《墨西哥风土百科全书》、调料包、手摇充电改良式蓄电池……

Ramona: You're made of steel. The hell you need seasoning packets for?
拉莫纳：你只是机械之躯，并不需要进食，为什么需要调料包？

Ramona: Just how much crap did you bring with you?
拉莫纳：你究竟随身带着多少乱七八糟的东西？

Loggerhead: Invisible cloak, crystal phial, sextant, Kangaroo Foam No. 5 Fire Extinguisher ... Ah, here it is!
空脑袋：隐身斗篷、水晶瓶、六分仪、袋鼠泡沫五号灭火剂……噢，有了！

Loggerhead: Thank goodness the receptionist lady turned down my request to check the disks.
空脑袋：还好夜巡特遣管理局的接待员女士拒绝了我软盘检查的请求！

Flustered, she pulls that teleport disk from her pocket and thinks of a destination.
她手忙脚乱从口袋中掏出那张传送软盘，在心中默念起目的地。

Loggerhead: Teleport!
空脑袋：转念即至！

They disappear all at once from the scene, leaving the club in an uproar.
两名“凶手”逃离了现场，只留下一地狼藉。

Smuggler III: Damn it! They escaped.
走私犯Ⅲ：该死，她们逃走了！

Smuggler III: And they took that painting with them.
走私犯Ⅲ：这两个凶手甚至还偷走了俱乐部的拍卖品。

Smuggler III: So they are here to sabotage the auction. Those Foundation bastards—can never trust them!
走私犯Ⅲ：看来她们的真实目的就是破坏这场拍卖会。基金会果然从来都不安好心。

Confused Guest: Well, at least we're safe from them now. Still, seems like it's time to leave.
疑惑的宾客：既然凶手已经不在了，俱乐部也该放我们离去了。

Confused Guest: Or is the auction still on? Are you letting us leave or not, Mr. Pérez?
疑惑的宾客：还是说，佩雷斯先生把我们留在这里，是想要继续拍卖会？

Angry Guest: Have you lost it? This auction's a disaster, a joke, I tell you.
愤怒的宾客：怎么可能？！太离谱了，这次的拍卖会可真是一场闹剧。

Angry Guest: I intend to have a long talk with Mr. Pérez after this.
愤怒的宾客：过段时间，我得和佩雷斯先生好好聊聊。

"Frightened Guest": I can't stay here any longer. For goodness sake, a woman just died!
不安的“宾客”：这里才刚发生了命案——我可不想一直待在这里……

With the apparent murderers gone, the club settles into an uneasy quiet, whispers spread, and people begin to search for an exit.
人群窃窃私语着，拍卖会现场逐渐骚动起来。

At last, the club's security takes the stage and provides the necessary, if foregone, conclusion.
终于，剩余的俱乐部高层出面处理了此事。

Patroller II: We're sorry to inform you that today's auction has been put on hold.
巡逻者Ⅱ：很抱歉，拍卖会暂停了。

Patroller II: Mr. Pérez was injured in the chaos and is currently unable to host the auction.
巡逻者Ⅱ：佩雷斯先生似乎……在混乱中受伤，无法继续主持拍卖会。

Patroller II: Ms. Luisa has asked us to take everyone out through the emergency exit.
巡逻者Ⅱ：刚刚路易莎女士吩咐我们，带诸位前往安全出口，离开这里。

Patroller II: This way, ma'am.
巡逻者Ⅱ：女士，请随我来，往这边走。

"Frightened Guest": Oh, thank you.
不安的“宾客”：噢，真是太好了……

Pigment Case: %￥#@*…
颜料箱子：%￥#@*……

The "frightened guest" presses down on her case to muffle the alarmed squeaks coming from inside.
不安的宾客女士连忙按住手中的箱子，让它无法再发出更多声音。

"Frightened Guest": Pardon the noise. I'm allergic to the glow of arcane skills, so I always carry some fresh mandrake with me. Their juice can ease the pain.
不安的“宾客”：请见谅。我有先天辉光过敏症，总得随身携带一些新鲜曼德拉草应急。它的汁液有镇痛作用。

"Frightened Guest": Though they can be a little rambunctious.
不安的“宾客”：但它们偶尔会发出一些恼人的声响。

Patroller II: No need to explain, ma'am. The Turquoise Serpent Club respects the privacy of our members.
巡逻者Ⅱ：放心，女士，绿松石蛇俱乐部不会过问任何一位宾客的隐私。

Patroller II: Go through this door. It'll take you back to central Mexico City.
巡逻者Ⅱ：从这扇门走出去后，您就能回到墨西哥城市区了。

Realizing the futility of their situation, the tiny voices in the case finally fall quiet.
眼见再无引起任何人注意的希望，箱子中的挣扎变得微弱了起来。

Pigment Case: ...
颜料箱子：……

"Frightened Guest": Thank you, sir.
不安的“宾客”：谢谢你，热心的先生。

31  魔盒里的国王 · 1/2 / Box-Bound Ruler · 1/2

Performers and audience members leave one by one. The wild night falls back into silence.
演员与观众相继散场，疯狂的夜晚终于重归寂静。

Under the dark sky, a person carrying secrets takes a careful, winding path before knocking on the side of a courtyard gate.
夜色之中，身怀秘密之人谨慎地绕行多地，才敲响了一个院落的门。

Añil: I've come as requested, Ms. Butterfly.
野木蓝：我依约前来，蝴蝶女士。

Añil: I ensured I wasn't followed by any—
野木蓝：我确信身后没有尾随的人——

Lorentz Butterfly: I have cameras set up. I would've known if you were. Shut up and get in.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：别废话了，进来。我早就在四周安放了监测器。

Abandoned Room
废弃房间

Lorentz Butterfly: We've been waiting here for an hour.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我们已经在这里等了一个小时了。

Añil: "We"?
野木蓝：“我们”……？

Lorentz Butterfly: We have company. Your employer, in fact. Allow me to introduce you to "Mr. Pérez."
洛伦兹蝴蝶：是时候重新认识一下你的雇主，真正的佩雷斯先生了。

Lorentz Butterfly: The mysterious mastermind behind the Turquoise Serpent Club.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：让我们来欢迎，绿松石蛇俱乐部的主人——

She smiles and opens the battered pigment case in front of her.
她微笑着打开面前破旧的颜料盒。

The mice, waiting for this moment, lunge out in a desperate attack against their kidnapper.
蓄谋已久的老鼠们早已咬断了身上的绳索，愤怒地涌出箱子。

Mamá Pérez: This is our chance, my children! Kill the bipedal monster!
佩雷斯妈妈：孩子们，杀了这个直立行走的家伙！

33  魔盒里的国王 · 2/2 / Box-Bound Ruler · 2/2

The pointless fight ends. Bruised and battered, the little mice are tossed back into the case.
无用的抗争结束了，鼻青脸肿的老鼠一家被重新控制起来。

Mamá Pérez: Argh! Kitty claws! Just who the hell are you, biped?!
佩雷斯妈妈：噢！****[鼠类脏话]！该死的人类，你究竟是谁？！

Mamá Pérez: Did Cruz send you? Oh, I get it now. You're with Miguel, aren't you?
佩雷斯妈妈：你是谁的人？克罗斯那家伙的手下吗？还是米格尔？

Mamá Pérez: He's bent out of shape about the auction. Thinks I'm stepping on his block. Tch!
佩雷斯妈妈：我知道他最近一直在眼红我的拍卖会，觉得我插手了他地盘的交易。

Mamá Pérez: How much did he pay you, huh? I'll offer double, no, triple! Or are you with the government? If so, I'm open to negotiating a very handsome price for your cooperation.
佩雷斯妈妈：他给了你多少钱，我可以给你双倍……不，三倍！价格都是可以商量的！还是说，你是政府的人，想要黑吃黑？

Lorentz Butterfly: Huh, it's hard not to find the sight of a rodent begging for its life amusing.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：嗯哼。一窝老鼠在和我谈条件。

The smile she flashes down at them borders on unhinged. In response, the pleading mouse turns to the other face.
蝴蝶女士无动于衷，不置可否。老鼠们转而看向她身后一直不语的男子。

Mamá Pérez: Wait a second. You—I know you, boy! You're Añil, the one I hired to forge paintings for the club!
佩雷斯妈妈：等等——小子，我认得你，你叫野木蓝，是给俱乐部供画的家伙！

Mamá Pérez: How dare you betray me, you furless cretin!
佩雷斯妈妈：*****[鼠类脏话]，你这个叛徒！

Añil: Miss, you really mean to claim that they are Pérez?
野木蓝：这就是……佩雷斯先生？

Añil: The legendary Pérez, the feared boss who controls the Turquoise Serpent Club and most of Mexico City's black market with an iron fist?
野木蓝：那个掌控着墨西哥城大半地下市场的走私犯，俱乐部的幕后老板，无所不能的佩雷斯先生……

A wave of absurdity washes over him, though he can't bring himself to laugh.
一阵荒谬感涌上心头。

Lorentz Butterfly: An iron fist, gripped in the paws of a few tiny mice.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：一直掌握着你命运的人，就是眼前的这窝小老鼠。

Lorentz Butterfly: If I'm correct, these little troublemakers managed to replace the real Pérez about a month ago.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：没猜错的话，在大约不到一个月前，那次俱乐部所谓的内乱之中，原来的佩雷斯就悄无声息地被这窝小老鼠们顶替了。

Lorentz Butterfly: Since then, they've stayed hidden, mimicking Pérez's voice from behind a screen. No one's noticed until now.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：它们躲在屏风的后头，用机器模仿着佩雷斯说话的声音，下达着一条条指令，操纵着所有人的生死。

Añil: No wonder the club's started making so many strange moves.
野木蓝：所以最近，俱乐部那些激进的动作……

Lorentz Butterfly: Yes. Under their leadership, the club has absorbed several gangs and expanded its influence in the black market. It was their idea to start forging all those paintings and hold the auction.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：没错。是它们巩固了俱乐部的势力，扩大墨西哥城的地下神秘学材料市场，吞并其余的黑帮；也是它们召集城市内的画家伪造假画，举办拍卖会。

Lorentz Butterfly: Hm. If you ask me, they've far outdone the original Pérez.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：嗯？从这个层面上来说，它们可比原来的佩雷斯强多了。

Lorentz Butterfly: Oh, the irony. Any tears coming?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：感觉如何？想哭吗？

Añil: ...
野木蓝：……

He refuses to answer.
他拒绝回答这个问题。

Mamá Pérez: Oye, you're right. The club's recent success is all thanks to me.
佩雷斯妈妈：嘿，你说得不错，这些可都是我的功劳。

Mamá Pérez: See what we mice can do? How great we are! No one knows how to run a gang better than a rodent.
佩雷斯妈妈：不要瞧不起老鼠！你得承认，我们比人类更擅长经营黑帮！

Mamá Pérez: You know why? Because you giant two-leggers don't have a head for this business, while we brilliant burrowers were born to rule the underground.
佩雷斯妈妈：直立行走的生物是如此愚蠢，动作迟缓，不如我们天生适合掌控“地下世界”——毕竟我们是穴居动物！

Lorentz Butterfly: I don't recall asking for your opinion.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我好像没有问你话吧。

Lorentz Butterfly: Come to think of it, I need only one mouse for my purposes, one that's quiet and obedient.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我觉得这儿的老鼠有点太多了。想要问话，留下其中一只听话的就可以了。

Lorentz Butterfly: As for the rest, I happen to have a colleague who enjoys nothing more than feasting on rats. Eight of them, actually. We're not close, but I'm sure you'd make a perfect gift.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：剩下的，就送给我的九头蛇同事当墨西哥伴手礼吧。虽然我们不熟。

She hovers her hand over the case, letting her fingers dangle above them menacingly and fall like a claw machine in search of a toy.
手的阴影在颜料盒上游移，仿佛在商店中精心挑选着礼物。最终，它朝着其中一只缓缓落下。

Mamá Pérez: W-W-Wait! Leave my children out of this, please!
佩雷斯妈妈：等等，等等——不要碰我的孩子！

Mamá Pérez: I-I apologize for my rudeness, miss. Clearly, you're an extraordinary arcanist, unlike most of your cheese-brained kind.
佩雷斯妈妈：我道歉！你和别的蠢货都不一样，是个了不起的、凌驾于他人之上的神秘学家！

Mamá Pérez: You deserve to rule the underworld just as much as I do. I'll gladly hand you this club on a silver platter if that's what you desire.
佩雷斯妈妈：你值得这一切，你才应该掌管地下王国！哪怕你想要俱乐部的一切，我都可以给你！

It is exactly what she wants to hear. In a split second, her face turns from menace to quiet satisfaction.
了不起的神秘学家只是似笑非笑地看着它。

Lorentz Butterfly: Hm. Rodents are quite adaptable when they need to be.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：看来鼠科动物的适应能力确实很强。

Lorentz Butterfly: But I'm not interested in running a gang.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：可惜了，我对当这里的地头蛇没有什么兴趣。

Mamá Pérez: Surely there's something I can help you with, miss. You're looking for a pigment, aren't you?
佩雷斯妈妈：直立行走的女士，你之前说，你在寻找一种矿物颜料的下落？

Mamá Pérez: I'll tell you everything I know, all of it, just as long as you guarantee my children's safety.
佩雷斯妈妈：我可以知无不言，把一切都告诉你。前提是，你要保证我的孩子们的安全。

Mamá Pérez: Though the odds of finding that particular arcane pigment again are a million to one. In any case, let's start from the beginning.
佩雷斯妈妈：很遗憾，你已经不可能找到那种颜料了！
这一切得从佩雷斯一家是如何获得智慧开始说起——

Mamá Pérez: We mice are, of course, far superior to all humankind. I don't refute it. But not every mouse has got the smarts that my family does. What made my children and me so special, you ask? It all began with a squeak-tacular accident.
佩雷斯妈妈：老鼠虽然天生比人类和神秘学家都强大，但并非每只老鼠都拥有智慧。
佩雷斯一家的特殊，始于某种惊鼠的偶然。

Mamá Pérez: On a frightening, fateful night, as I ran for my life from a snarling hellbeast, the true nemesis of mousekind, I found myself in an unfamiliar room.
佩雷斯妈妈：某一个夜晚，因为被邪恶的毛绒生物驱逐，我躲进了一个从未去过的房间里。

Mamá Pérez: The floor was so clean I nearly slipped, and the furniture gave off a rich, soothing scent. But the walls above, good heavens, they were covered with the heads of beasts beyond any counting!
佩雷斯妈妈：地板干净得让老鼠脚打滑，墙上挂着数不清的猛兽头颅，比每天晚上逐猎我们的邪恶毛绒还要大得多！就连家具也散发着好闻的木头气味。

Mamá Pérez: Still, I pulled myself together and climbed up onto the dining table, hoping to find something to eat. Who would have thought there'd be nothing but a small plate of glowing pigment up there?
佩雷斯妈妈：我爬上了桌子，想要找点吃的。桌面上什么吃的都没有，只有一个小瓷碟，而一抹散发着辉光的膏状物体就盛在瓷碟里。真奇怪，这里只有一碟颜料。

Mamá Pérez: The pigment looked odd, I admit, but my hunger overtook my fear, so I ate it. And the moment it touched my tongue, the world as I knew it fell away.
佩雷斯妈妈：鬼使神差地，也许是太饿了，我把颜料吃了下去……在它进入嘴巴的那一瞬间，我眼前的世界完全改变了。

Mamá Pérez: For the first time in my life, I had other thoughts beyond mating and eating, beyond survival! Thoughts that only true intelligence could evoke!
佩雷斯妈妈：第一次，除了觅食、繁殖和生存，我的脑海中出现了别的念头。那是只有智慧才能带来的念头。

Mamá Pérez: I stayed there for a couple more days, and thanks to my keen eye, I learned I was in this Pérez's office, and that he ran half of Mexico City's smuggling business.
佩雷斯妈妈：往后几天，我一直躲在那里。老鼠们都拥有着卓越的观察力，我很快弄明白，这里是佩雷斯先生的办公室，而他经营着墨西哥城半数的走私生意。

Mamá Pérez: A strange masked person in a black robe brought him that miraculous substance. It seems, at their request, Pérez was using a lumber mill as a front while they extracted a unique silver ore from Valle de Lágrimas—the Vale of Tears.
佩雷斯妈妈：那种能让鼠获得智慧的颜料，由一个戴面具的黑袍人带来。佩雷斯先生应下了这桩生意，用锯木厂作为掩饰，帮助面具人在眼泪之谷挖掘一种特殊的秘银矿石。

Mamá Pérez: The masked person refined the ore into a pigment and had it shipped far away, to lands they called "Yu-Rope" and "Yu-Ess." And in return, the club received a portion of it.
佩雷斯妈妈：然后，戴面具的人将矿石炼制成颜料，运至很远的地方，传说中的欧洲和美国。作为回报，俱乐部也能获得一些颜料的成品。

Mamá Pérez: To get my hands on the pigment, and Pérez's smuggling business on the side, I staged a coup. We replaced him without even breaking a sweat.
佩雷斯妈妈：我只是简单地谋划了一场内乱，就顶替了原本的佩雷斯，接手了他的走私王国和颜料交易。

Mamá Pérez: Further proof that you bipeds are far duller than us brilliant, quadrupedal mice!
佩雷斯妈妈：哈哈，这些直立行走的家伙和老鼠比起来，还是差得远了！

Mamá Pérez: Everything was going smoothly, and we had a steady supply of pigment, until that masked stranger up and vanished, taking the secret to its creation with them!
佩雷斯妈妈：本来一切都无比顺利，直至最近，那个戴面具的人消失了——连同制作颜料的方法一起。

Mamá Pérez: I turned the city upside down, trying to track them down. I thought no one could hide themselves from me.
佩雷斯妈妈：我翻遍了墨西哥城的每一寸地皮，并不相信这座城市里还有我找不到的人。

Mamá Pérez: But they did—poof! Gone without a trace. Like they never even existed.
佩雷斯妈妈：但事实就是如此：他——或者她，随便吧，它就这样消失了！你懂吗？！就像是鬼魂一样失去了踪迹！

The little mouse finishes with an excited flourish. Her listeners pause, each deep in contemplation. Añil speaks first.
在听完佩雷斯的故事后，与面前激动的老鼠不同，两位旁听者各自陷入了沉思。

Añil: It must be because of the Vale of Tears' silver ore. I've heard of it before.
野木蓝：眼泪之谷的秘银矿，我听说过它的传说。

Añil: The locals called the place the Man-Eating Maw. Every miner who ventured into its tunnels never returned.
野木蓝：曾经，人们称呼这座矿山为“吃人的口”，因为被殖民者奴役去挖矿的当地人总是有去无回。

Añil: In the 18th century, there was a massive mine collapse. No one's managed to find any silver there since.
野木蓝：十八世纪，眼泪之谷发生过一次巨大的塌方事故，从那之后，再也没有人能够找到秘银矿的入口。

Añil: Rumor is the silver they found there shimmers with an extraordinary glow and is highly malleable, two qualities that make it an exceptionally rare alchemical material.
野木蓝：传闻那儿产出的秘银有着特殊的辉光颤动，极其纯净、耀眼，有着无穷无尽的延展性，就像……丝线一样，是非常罕见的炼金材料。

Añil: But I've never heard of it being made into a pigment, let alone it being used to grant mice intelligence.
野木蓝：但我从来没听说过，它能制成颜料。而这种矿物颜料，甚至能赋予动物智慧。

Mamá Pérez: As I'm sure you've noticed, miss, my children, they no longer speak as I do.
佩雷斯妈妈：直立行走的女士，你也瞧见了：我的孩子们已经无法说话。

Mamá Pérez: It seems the intelligence granted by the pigment must be maintained through regular consumption.
佩雷斯妈妈：这种由颜料所赋予的智慧，需要定期食用颜料才能维持。

Mamá Pérez: Without it, they've turned back into ordinary mice, one after the other.
佩雷斯妈妈：如今，它们的智慧正在逐渐消失……没有了颜料，我的孩子们正在变回普通的老鼠。

The young mice stare up at their mother with blank faces, frightened but unaware.
箱子中的老鼠们懵懂地看着自己的母亲。

Mamá Pérez: The same tragedy will befall me soon. I can feel it. Whether in a month or a matter of weeks.
佩雷斯妈妈：我能感觉到，用不了多久，再过上一个月？也许只要半个月，我也会变回普通的老鼠。

Mamá Pérez: No, I won't let that happen.
佩雷斯妈妈：我不能接受这样的结局。

Lorentz Butterfly: That explains your recent desperation.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这正是你着急的原因。

Lorentz Butterfly: Rushing the auction, expanding your smuggling operation, all in search of a substitute for this arcane pigment.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：急着举办拍卖会，扩大走私规模，尝试找到替代矿物颜料的神秘学材料。

Lorentz Butterfly: But your efforts were in vain. How sad, hmph.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：很可惜，一无所获。

Mamá Pérez: Oye, miss, you think rubbing salt in a victim's wound is fun?
佩雷斯妈妈：嘿，这位女士，难道你只是为了说风凉话而来的吗？！

Lorentz Butterfly: It is one of my hobbies.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：不可否认这算是我的爱好之一。

Lorentz Butterfly: So, Manus Vindictae must've last appeared about a month ago.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：将近一个月前，重塑之手出现了——

She puts it together quickly. All of this must have happened shortly after the "Storm" reset the era to the 1920s.
那时间点，正是“暴雨”将时代重返至1920年不久之后。

Manus Vindictae arrived, already aware of the hidden mine that had yet to be discovered during this period, with the alchemical knowledge necessary to refine its bounty into the very mineral pigment that she had been searching for.
重塑之手携带着不属于这个时代的炼金技艺，来寻找一座已被掩埋、在后世才会重现的矿藏，制作出特殊的矿物颜料。

The Institutum researcher weighs her next move.
跨时代的混沌研究者思索了片刻。

Lorentz Butterfly: I will help you. But you need to tell me the exact identities of every Manus member who's dealt with the club.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我可以帮你。告诉我负责走私的重塑人员都有谁。

Mamá Pérez: Well, it's not that I don't want to tell you, but even I, the ever-resourceful Pérez, know little about them. I doubt you'll be able to smoke them out either.
佩雷斯妈妈：不可能的，就连佩雷斯先生掘地三尺都找不到的人，你能有什么办法？

Still doubtful but very much cowed with fear, she takes out a list and hands it over.
话虽如此，老鼠头目仍将信将疑地取出一份名单，递给面前的人。

Lorentz Butterfly: Calvaire, Richard Lenoir, Ledru-Rollin, Fitzwilliam, Taira, Diggers ...
洛伦兹蝴蝶：卡尔维尔、勒德-罗朗、里沙尔·勒努阿尔、菲茨威廉、平、挖掘艺术……

Lorentz Butterfly: Just aliases, not their real names.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这都是些重塑之手成员的代号。

Mamá Pérez: Deliberately cryptic aliases at that! Freakish bipeds.
佩雷斯妈妈：是的，什么古怪的代号都有——真是个装神弄鬼的地方！

Mamá Pérez: They came to this city discreetly, disguised as tourists, journalists, or some harmless arcane society members.
佩雷斯妈妈：他们伪装成游客、记者、民间神秘学组织的成员，分批进入这座城市。

Mamá Pérez: They never stayed long. Once a batch of pigment was ready, they vanished soon after. I even asked a friend in the government to help track them down, but they couldn't find any trace of them.
佩雷斯妈妈：在取得颜料之后，他们并不会在墨西哥城多作逗留。我在政府的朋友没能找到任何留下的线索。

Lorentz Butterfly scans through the names, searching for a glimmer of recognition.
蝴蝶女士的视线扫过这些奇形怪状的名字，仿佛在砂砾中寻找不存在的钻石。

Lorentz Butterfly: Fitzwilliam.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：菲茨威廉。

Lorentz Butterfly: Where else have I seen that name? Besides Pride and Prejudice?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：除了《傲慢与偏见》，我还在哪见过这个名字呢……？

Mamá Pérez: Fitzwilliam? That fellow claimed to be a "cultural journalist" who came to the city to write reports about local festivals.
佩雷斯妈妈：菲茨威廉？这家伙是一名风土记者，打着记录节庆的名号来到这儿。

Mamá Pérez: He isn't just good with a pen, though. He's a skilled smuggler. So skilled, in fact, that his slippery fingers also made off with quite a few valuables from my club.
佩雷斯妈妈：别说，在走私之余，这个重塑的家伙貌似还写出了一篇像模像样的报道。可恶的是，他临走前骗走了俱乐部的不少好东西！

Lorentz Butterfly: Ah yes, that report.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：是了，报道。

Lorentz Butterfly: It seems likely that to get the foreign diplomat to agree to the interview, he offered him something from the club as a gift.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：为了完成这篇报道，让外交官接受他的采访，他将从俱乐部里获得的某样东西，赠送给了那位外交官作为交换。

Lorentz Butterfly: Our "Mr. Darcy" has already left the city, but the diplomat will stay until the end of the festival.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：“傲慢”先生已经带着走私的物品离开了。但外交官因为公务，仍会在墨西哥城逗留至节庆结束。

Lorentz Butterfly: The key to everything lies in that gift.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：那件他赠予外交官的物品，才是一切的关键。

Mamá Pérez: The gift? Why bother with that? Isn't the pigment what we're really after?
佩雷斯妈妈：我不明白，这究竟有什么联系？咱们不是在找颜料的下落吗？

Mamá Pérez: Or do you mean the diplomat is a Manus member too? Is the gift itself the pigment?
佩雷斯妈妈：难道这位外交官，也是那个组织的人？还是说他将颜料送给对方了？

Lorentz Butterfly: Once we find the answer to one, we'll uncover the answer to the other.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这两件看似无关的事情，其实是一件事。只要我们找到他，就能知道其中的联系了。

Mamá Pérez: I-If you're sure we're on the right track, I'll do all I can to help you out.
佩雷斯妈妈：如果这是真的，我愿意倾尽全力帮你！！

Mamá Pérez: Our grand operation to retake the pigment will start at sunrise tomorrow. No, make that now. What are you waiting for? Free me so I can gather my men and—
佩雷斯妈妈：我们日出就出发……不，现在就出发！马上让我回俱乐部召集人手——

Lorentz Butterfly: You're not going anywhere, Mr. Pérez.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这不可能。

The correction comes with a cold smile.
她微笑着打断了老鼠头目的幻想。

Lorentz Butterfly: You won't be part of this operation. And don't even dream about freedom until I get my hands on the pigment.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：“佩雷斯先生”，在我真正找到那种矿物颜料之前，你是不可能重获自由的。而我也不会告诉你更多计划的详情。

Lorentz Butterfly: Better pray I solve this before you lose the last of your wits like your kids.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：你最好祈祷，在你完全丧失智慧之前，我能够解决这件事。

Mamá Pérez: No! You can't do this to me, you two-legged demon!
佩雷斯妈妈：不……！*****[鼠类脏话]，你不能这样残酷！！

She lowers the lid of the pigment case down, slow yet firm, until their cries become muffled squeaks once more. Then she flips the latch down.
颜料箱被毫不留情地重新盖上，只余喊叫声在其中回响。

She walks to the window to take in the last moments of the night.
度过了忙碌夜晚的女士走至窗台旁。

A dry night wind blows through the city as the day dawns on the Day of the Dead. The mask of menace she had worn through her interrogation is gone entirely. All that's left is a woman staring out a window, content but unsmiling.
干燥的晚风吹拂着这座处于亡灵节庆的城市。
与方才恐吓黑帮头目时的模样不同，此刻，她的脸上只余冷漠与乏味。

Añil: You seem confident, Ms. Butterfly.
野木蓝：蝴蝶女士，您看起来胸有成竹。

Añil: You've already found some critical clues pointing to things beyond this pigment, haven't you?
野木蓝：您在名单中发现了线索。不仅仅是您在寻找的矿物颜料，而是……

Lorentz Butterfly: Yes, something that connects all the murders.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：而是那起轰动的连环谋杀案。

Lorentz Butterfly: And a soon-to-be victim. Alive, for the time being.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：没错，我找到了一名仍然活着的受害者，找到了所有凶案的关联。

Añil: Incredible! You pieced it all together from just a single glance at that list?
野木蓝：仅凭一张名单，您就将它们串联在了一起。这太不可思议了。

Añil: But shouldn't you do something right away? You might still be able to save them.
野木蓝：只是为什么……您不马上行动？您为什么不阻止这一切？

In response, she passes him the list dryly over her shoulder.
蝴蝶女士并没有回答他的问题，只是将那份走私名单交给他。

Lorentz Butterfly: "Save them"? Why would I do that?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这重要吗？我的朋友，你又是在担心些什么呢？

Lorentz Butterfly: Don't bother trying to figure me out. Wastes both our time. The murders, the Vigiles, the gangs, and this whole underground world mean nothing to me.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：无需浪费力气来试探我。谋杀案，夜巡特遣管理局，神秘学家黑帮，隐藏在城市底下的秘密……这些你们所在意的事情，对我来说都没有意义。

Lorentz Butterfly: This drama will draw its curtain tomorrow, All Souls' Day, when the city's residents welcome back their departed faithful.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：明晚的诸灵节，就是迎接成年亡灵的时间，也是这场戏落幕的时刻。

Lorentz Butterfly: Interrupting such a heartfelt occasion would just be cruel, don't you think?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：中断它未免太不近人情了，不是吗？

Lorentz Butterfly: No, I'll infiltrate the diplomat's mansion tomorrow night and attend the festival myself, though I guarantee you won't be able to find me in the crowd.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：相反，明晚，我会潜入外交官的府邸，藏在你无法发现的地方，也许是人群，也许是建筑的死角。而在所有人都感到惶惶之时——

Lorentz Butterfly: When the killer strikes and the audience screams, I'll be sitting in the best seat in the house.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我会在暗处观众席的最佳位置上，亲眼见证凶手的落幕表演。

Lorentz Butterfly: And prove, as always, that my deductions are flawless.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：如此，得以验证我的推演必定是完美无瑕的。

Añil: I think I see your reasoning now, Ms. Butterfly.
野木蓝：……我明白您的计划了，蝴蝶女士。

The large device she carries makes an audible hum, only noticeable in the silence between them.
身旁，她随身携带的巨大仪器，正在不停地运作着。

Añil: Pardon me for asking, but what is this device you're carrying about?
野木蓝：也许有些冒昧，但我有些好奇，您一直随身携带的这台仪器究竟是……？

Lorentz Butterfly: A "Demonic Scale"-based additive manufacturing machine, powered by tens of thousands of arcane skills operating simultaneously.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这是一台经由厄难种子改造，与数万项神秘术共同运转的增材制造技术仪器。

Añil: I've never seen such technology.
野木蓝：我从来没见过这样的工艺。

Lorentz Butterfly: In layman's terms, you might think of it as a kind of automatic arcane printer for physical items.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：简单来说，这是一台神秘学实体打印机。

Lorentz Butterfly: But it's merely a prop for tomorrow night's performance.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：不过是为明晚做一些小准备罢了。

An ornate letter engraved on its casing catches his eye.
一个镌刻在仪器外壳的装饰字母引起他的注意。

Añil: M, the initial of your name, isn't it, miss?
野木蓝：M——这是您的签名吗，蝴蝶女士？

Añil: What does it stand for? Mystery? Madness? Magic?
野木蓝：它代表着什么？神秘的，疯狂的，还是有魔力的？

Each guess slides off of her unanswered.
M女士没有回答，但也没有阻止他继续猜测下去。

Añil: Mary? Mia? Mona, perhaps?
野木蓝：玛丽，米娅，莫娜……

???: M—
？？？：M——

???: Marguerite.
？？？：玛格丽特。

34  寂静准则 / Code of Silence

Mining Area, Chukotka
楚科奇矿区

Hush Squad Commander: Marguerite!
寂静小队军官：玛格丽特！

Marguerite: Hmm?
玛格丽特：嗯？

Hush Squad Commander: Explain what you're doing out here.
寂静小队军官：你在这儿干什么？

The year is 1987, just after the second "Storm."
第二次“暴雨”后，1987年。

A cold wind sweeps across the Siberian ice fields, lifting snow from the frozen ground and carrying it far away.
西伯利亚冰原上，彻骨的寒风吹起冻土层上的雪屑，它们在空中打着转儿，被席卷向远方。

Marguerite: Nothing.
玛格丽特：没什么。

Marguerite: One of them, a girl, has the same name as me.
玛格丽特：只是有个女孩，她说她也叫这个名字。

She wipes away the writing left in the snow.
她将雪地上的字迹擦去。

Hush Squad Commander: Have you forgotten the Hush Squad's number-one rule? No communication with mission targets!
寂静小队军官：芝诺寂静小队的第一条准则：不许与任务目标有任何的沟通。

Hush Squad Commander: When the LT assigned you to my squad, he said you had the right stuff to be an excellent Hush Trooper.
寂静小队军官：威廉中尉将你交到我手上的时候，他说你是这么多年来他见过最适合当寂静者的苗子。

Hush Squad Commander: Said you could spot a target in a ghillie suit from two kilometers away.
寂静小队军官：他从未见过有人能像你一样，能够如此轻易地分辨六千尺外藏在草丛中的人影，那件本用于隐蔽的吉利服对你来说却像靶子一样。

Hush Squad Commander: More importantly, he said you were smart. So be smart, and don't meddle in things that aren't your concern, understand?
寂静小队军官：最重要的是，你是个聪明人，应该知道我想说什么。别跟那些扶不上墙的家伙一样，总是想要多管闲事。

Marguerite: Mm-hmm.
玛格丽特：嗯。

Hush Squad Commander: Break's over. Let's move.
寂静小队军官：休息时间结束了，准备出发吧。

Marguerite lifts her rifle and trudges behind her commander into the blistering wind. At last, they arrive at the transport vehicle.
玛格丽特背起枪，深一脚浅一脚地跟上军官的脚步。两人走至运输车前。

Hush Squad Commander: What's going on? Why aren't we moving?
寂静小队军官：发生了什么？怎么还没出发？

Hush Trooper: It's the engine. Not even our warming incantation can manage against this cold.
寂静者：天气太冷了，就连温暖咒也用处不大。发动机出了些问题……

Hush Trooper: We might have to stop and spend the night here.
寂静者：我们可能要在这里过夜了。

The subordinate reports, shivering.
下属打着哆嗦汇报道。

Hush Squad Commander: Unacceptable!
寂静小队军官：绝对不行！

Hush Squad Commander: We're still at least three days from the Loop Laboratory's relay station. We can't afford to lose another. We have to get these transfers done on schedule.
寂静小队军官：这里距离回环实验室的转运站至少还有三天的路程。我们得在冬季到来之前完成所有的转移工作，一天的时间都不能拖。

Hush Squad Commander: Or there's no guarantee we'll have enough time to take shelter when the "Storm" hits.
寂静小队军官：否则，谁也不能保证，当“暴雨”忽然来临的时候，我们有足够的避难时间。

Hush Trooper: But I think the deserters need some time to rest as well. Most of them have fallen ill.
寂静者：但……车厢里的那群逃兵，大半都生病了。

Hush Trooper: If we keep going like this, they might not make it to the destination.
寂静者：再这样下去，等我们到达实验室的时候，他们不一定还活着。

Hush Trooper: I think they could use some sleep and proper medica—
寂静者：他们需要休息、需要吃药——

Hush Squad Commander: That is above your pay grade, trooper. We were ordered to transfer these deserters dead or alive.
寂静小队军官：拉丽莎，别做多余的事！上头给我们的指令只是转移逃兵，可没有说要保证他们的死活。

Their commander snaps at her before she can finish.
军官冷漠地打断了她。

Hush Squad Commander: Marguerite, go fix the engine.
寂静小队军官：玛格丽特，你来帮忙修发动机！

Hush Squad Commander: I'll contact Command to confirm the location of our nearest post. I want this convoy burning rubber by the end of the hour. Understood?
寂静小队军官：我去和上头联系，确认最近的补给点。一个小时后我们必须启程。

Marguerite: Yes, ma'am.
玛格丽特：是。

Without a word, Marguerite inspects the broken engine. Nearby, Larisa looks worried.
玛格丽特沉默地检查着故障的发动机，一旁，拉丽莎面露忧愁。

Hush Trooper: *sigh* Sure is cold up near the mines, huh?
寂静者：矿区真的太冷了，不是吗？

Hush Trooper: If I hadn't joined Zeno, I'd probably be sitting in a warm classroom right now.
寂静者：如果不是加入了芝诺，这么冷的天，我现在说不定正坐在大学的教室里听课呢。

Hush Trooper: *sigh* I wonder what university life is like.
寂静者：我还没有上过大学……

Marguerite: Don't dream too much. University can be just as strange as anywhere else.
玛格丽特：大学不一定和你想象中的一样美好。里面同样也充满着奇怪的人和事。

The distant young woman keeps her gaze lowered.
冷淡的少女头也不抬。

Marguerite: Back in 1999, I was auditing a philosophy class at Cambridge, just to watch some bird-headed professor declare out-of-the-blue that teaching philosophy was an absurd profession.
玛格丽特：99年我在剑桥大学旁听哲学课时，台上的鸟头教授忽然得出结论：哲学教授是世界上最荒谬的职业。

Marguerite: He quit and joined the Laplace Scientific Computing Center.
玛格丽特：然后他就离职了，一名哲学教授，最终加入了拉普拉斯。

Hush Trooper: Haha, that's a good one—going to university in 1999. How old would you have been? 13? 14?
寂静者：哈哈哈……这怎么可能？你一定在骗我。1999年的时候，你才13……还是14岁？

Hush Trooper: How'd a prodigy like you end up stuck in the Hush Squad?
寂静者：如果是那样，你一定是一名天才。怎么会加入寂静小队呢？

Marguerite: Could be I just enjoy the work. It's calming.
玛格丽特：这儿让我感觉内心平静。

Marguerite: Could be that I hope someday to find my purpose here.
玛格丽特：也许我可以在这里找到以后想做的事。

Marguerite slams the hood shut, and the engine roars to life.
发动机轰鸣起来，她用力地合上前车盖。
车子修好了。

Hush Trooper: Yeah, sure, if walking across an ice field at forty below isn't calming, I don't know what is.
寂静者：平静？你是指在零下四十度的冰原上漫步吗？

Marguerite: Study your target, predict their movement, track them down, and then ...
玛格丽特：研读目标的资料，推演他的行动，追踪，然后——

She mimics firing a gun with her hand.
少女比了个开枪的手势。

Hush Trooper: That's what makes you calm? You've got to be joking.
寂静者：你一定是在开玩笑。

Marguerite: Yeah, just joking.
玛格丽特：是的，只是一个玩笑。

Hush Trooper: *sigh* All I wanted was to join the Flying Witches.
寂静者：我……唉。在加入芝诺的寂静小队之前，我其实一直想当一名飞行女巫。

Marguerite: Then why are you here, stealing intel and carrying out assassinations?
玛格丽特：那你为什么在这儿，干着情报和暗杀的黑色行动？

Hush Trooper: The lieutenant promised that the Hush Squad comes with a generous salary and death benefits.
寂静者：中尉告诉我，寂静小队能得到更多的补贴，包括牺牲后的抚恤金也是。

Hush Trooper: You know, at the time, my mom was sick.
寂静者：那时候，我的妈妈生病了，病得很严重……

Hush Trooper: And then, the "Storm" struck. Guess I didn't really need the money in the end.
寂静者：当然，后来“暴雨”落下了。我再也不需要替妈妈筹医药费了。

A short, dry laugh escapes her lips, but Marguerite doesn't match it.
她自嘲地笑了一下，玛格丽特只是冷淡地看着遥远处。

Hush Trooper: Do you have any idea what this Loop Laboratory does, Marguerite?
寂静者：玛格丽特，你知道回环实验室是个什么地方吗？

Hush Trooper: We've transferred over a hundred deserters there since summer, but I've never seen any of them come back out.
寂静者：从夏天到现在，我们已经往实验室转移了上百个逃兵，但我从来没听说过有谁从里面出来。

Hush Trooper: Probably none of my business. I once helped with a Zeno recruitment drive in a war zone. I saw a lot of things there.
寂静者：我知道这些都不该多嘴。但先前，我也曾经替芝诺在战区招募成员。

Hush Trooper: Those soldiers, they lost everything in the war. They only joined Zeno because they heard they'd get proper meals, not exactly what I'd call willing recruits.
寂静者：有很多在战争中失去了一切的青年和孩子，他们只是听说能有一口热饭吃，就义无反顾地加入了芝诺。他们甚至不清楚自己将来要干什么。

Hush Trooper: These arcanist deserters we've been moving, they just weren't ready to face death yet.
寂静者：也许那些从战场上逃走的神秘学家，他们只是还没有做好面对死亡的准备。

Hush Trooper: Marguerite, I'm telling you this because I trust you.
寂静者：玛格丽特，我知道你和他们都不一样。你从不告状，所以我才和你说这些……

Marguerite: I know what it does.
玛格丽特：我知道。

Hush Trooper: Huh? What do you mean?
寂静者：你——什、什么？！

Marguerite: The Loop Laboratory.
玛格丽特：我知道回环实验室是个什么地方。

Marguerite: It has another name: the Amber Room.
玛格丽特：也许你听说过它的另外一个名字——琥珀屋。

Marguerite: At the end of the battle in 1999, a massive number of soldiers were lost to the "Storm." It was a devastating blow to Zeno's military strength.
玛格丽特：1999年，那场空前的战争让“暴雨”落下，无数士兵在雨中阵亡，芝诺实力大减。

Marguerite: From then on, the higher-ups have been trying to find a woman named Urd, or to be exact, the woman she was before being reversed by the "Storm."
玛格丽特：自那之后，芝诺高层一直在寻找一位名为兀尔德的女士，想要找到尚未被“暴雨”所冲刷、最初的她。

Marguerite: That's why they built the Loop Laboratory near the Don River. It's a facility that can resist the "Storm."
玛格丽特：为此，他们在顿河边建造了能够免疫“暴雨”的回环实验室。

Marguerite: The "deserters" aren't being transferred there for any ordinary training or dry runs.
玛格丽特：那些所谓的“逃兵”，他们被转移去实验室，接受的并不是普通的训练。

Marguerite: The things they do to the deserters' bodies involve unprecedented arcane powers related to the "Storm" itself.
玛格丽特：而是某种与“暴雨”相关的，前所未有的神秘术——

Before she realizes it, the listener's back is damp with cold sweat.
不知不觉中，冷汗已经浸湿了旁听者的后背。

Hush Trooper: I-I don't think I understand. How do you know all this?
寂静者：我，我听不明白。不对，玛格丽特……你是怎么知道这些的？

Marguerite: I read the classified files sealed in Warehouse 0 at headquarters.
玛格丽特：上次回总部的时候，我看了他们封存在0号仓库的机密文件。

Hush Trooper: You went into Warehouse 0?! They could have shot you just for stepping in there! Tell me you're just joking!
寂静者：0号仓库……？！这可是要枪毙的重罪，玛格丽特！快告诉我，你只是开玩笑的！

Marguerite: You got me again.
玛格丽特：开玩笑的。

Larisa breathes out a sigh of relief.
拉丽莎狠狠地松了口气。

Marguerite: I've got another joke: sometimes I eavesdrop on Zeno's encrypted telegrams, just for fun.
玛格丽特：换个说法吧，我偶尔会监听一下芝诺的加密电报作为消遣。

Marguerite: The problem is, the encryption patterns are so repetitive that cracking them's become boring.
玛格丽特：芝诺的加密模式非常单调，通过简单的筛选就能推断出密令的真实含义。

Hush Trooper: *sigh* I honestly can't tell which of your jokes is more disturbing.
寂静者：玛格丽特，我一时不知道你的哪个玩笑比较可怕。

Marguerite: The European branches have been bending over backwards to look for the original copies of The Tome of Mercury and The Path to Truth.
玛格丽特：不久之前，欧洲分部大张旗鼓地在找《水银之书》与《通往真理的道路》这两本炼金术著作。

Marguerite: But to find their owners—the famous Cavendish family of alchemists, we'll need the "Storm" to take us further back in time.
玛格丽特：但想要找到持有原始手稿的卡文迪许家族，要等“暴雨”将我们带回更早的时代了。

Marguerite: Meanwhile, a squad from the Buenos Aires branch has been searching for a rare arcane metal, likely a special silver ore from Mexico's Vale of Tears.
玛格丽特：布宜诺斯艾利斯分部有一支部队则一直在申请与搜寻贵金属相关的神秘术援助，他们必然是在秘密寻找眼泪之谷的秘银矿。

Marguerite: And the Egypt branch has sent Roar Lion Tails and Sacred Scarab Wings to the Loop Laboratory in droves.
玛格丽特：埃及分部则向实验室递交了圣甲虫双翅与吼狮鬃毛……

Larisa searches for some clear meaning to what she's hearing.
拉丽莎目瞪口呆地听着。

Hush Trooper: But what does all this mean? What are they trying to achieve?
寂静者：但这些人员调动的信息，究竟代表着什么？

Marguerite: I'm not sure, but one thing's for certain: it's all connected to the Loop Laboratory's arcane experiments.
玛格丽特：这也是我想知道的。但我可以肯定，这些材料的搜集，都必然与回环实验室在研究的特殊神秘术有关。

Marguerite: To find the truth behind the "Storm."
玛格丽特：而这一切，最终都关联到“暴雨”的真相。

Hush Trooper: All those experiments ... They must have found something, right?
寂静者：那芝诺……回环实验室，他们成功了吗？

Marguerite: No, Larisa, not even close.
玛格丽特：成功？当然没有。

Her face is expressionless, emotionless, almost blank.
玛格丽特漠然地看了一眼拉丽莎。

Marguerite: All signs point to a goal coveted by every alchemist, a feat humanity has never succeeded in achieving.
玛格丽特：回环实验室所研究的，可不是一个简单的目标，而是所有炼金术师追求的终极，拉丽莎。

Marguerite: It's hardly surprising they're still groping in the dark, looking for a key object, a step, or more likely, a person.
玛格丽特：现在，他们仍然像没头苍蝇一样乱转。也许是缺乏了某样关键的道具，也许是弄错了什么步骤，又或者是少了某些关键的人——

Marguerite: And these so-called "deserters"? Fodder. Even if they survive the experiments, they'll just be thrown out to face the next "Storm."
玛格丽特：这些“逃兵”，他们哪怕能熬过失败的神秘术研究，也不过是在下次“暴雨”到来时送死。

Hush Trooper: No way.
寂静者：怎么会这样……

Hush Trooper: I probably shouldn't ask, Marguerite, but, what did you mean by "so-called"?
寂静者：玛格丽特，也许我不该问的。但……刚刚你提起回环实验室的时候，为什么说是“所谓的逃兵”？

Marguerite seizes on the question, reading her comrade's nerves with her eyes.
面对拉丽莎惴惴不安的模样，少女只是定定地盯着她，仿佛狩猎者在观察猎物。

Marguerite: You've guessed already, haven't you?
玛格丽特：你已经猜到了，不是吗？

Hush Trooper: Guessed what? What are you trying to say?
寂静者：我猜到了什么……？玛格丽特，你究竟想说什么……

Marguerite: That these poor wretches are anything but "deserters."
玛格丽特：那些实验体，他们只不过是被捏造了罪名的普通士兵罢了。

Marguerite: Apparently, they were unfortunate enough to meet the experiment criteria, so Zeno fabricated their desertion charges and had them sent to the laboratory.
玛格丽特：那些士兵，他们似乎满足了芝诺某种特殊的挑选条件，于是芝诺伪造了他们逃跑的经历，将他们秘密送到实验室里去。

Marguerite: So, you know the truth. What are you going to do?
玛格丽特：现在，告诉我，你想要怎么做？

She has her on the edge, precisely where she wants her to be, but it isn't quite enough yet to push her over.
出乎意料的是，拉丽莎此刻的神情显得十分镇定。

Hush Trooper: *deep breath* I-I don't know. I need some time to think.
寂静者：我……我不知道，玛格丽特。我得好好想想。

Hush Trooper: In any case, I think I'll take some time to give the deserters. I mean, whatever I mean, I'll give them some medication so they can at least survive the journey there.
寂静者：至少，在我想明白之前，我想给这些逃兵……不，神秘学家们喂点药，让他们能够活过这几天，别倒在这场风雪里，才有资格谈论未来。

Hush Trooper: I know you won't stop me, Marguerite.
寂静者：我知道你不会拦我的，玛格丽特。

Marguerite shrugs.
少女耸耸肩，不置可否。

The Hush Trooper unlocks the truck. Inside, thin, sickly teenagers huddle together for warmth.
寂静者打开门锁，车厢里头，瘦骨嶙峋的青少年们抱成一团。

Deserter I: L-Larisa, will we be there soon?
逃兵Ⅰ：拉丽莎，我们什么时候能到你说的那个地方？

Deserter II: Everyone's in bad shape, but we've agreed among ourselves we don't want to cause you any more trouble, really.
逃兵Ⅱ：大家的状况都不是很好。但放心，我和他们都说好了，我们绝不会给你惹麻烦。

Deserter II: Fomá and Stepán are asleep now. I just hope their fever subsides.
逃兵Ⅱ：托马斯和斯蒂芬一直在发烧，现在还睡着。

Hush Trooper: *sigh* Don't worry. We'll reach our destination in just a few days. There'll be a warm place to stay and plenty of soup and bread.
寂静者：……放心，只要再开上三五天的路程，咱们就能到一个温暖的地方。那里有热汤，也有面包！

Hush Trooper: Sílviya, could you help hand out these antipyretics to anyone who needs them?
寂静者：我带了一些退烧药来。西尔维娅，你给生病的人分一分吧。

Deserter II: Oh, of course. We really can't thank you enough, Larisa.
逃兵Ⅱ：太好了！谢谢你，拉丽莎！

Footsteps crunch behind her, carried by the whipping wind.
就在此时，不远处的冰原上传来脚步声，离去的军官折返得比预期要快多了。

Hush Squad Commander: Alright, we just made contact with the Foundation's Far East branch and—
寂静小队军官：我联系过基金会远东分部——

Hush Squad Commander: Larisa! Marguerite! What the hell are you doing?
寂静小队军官：拉丽莎，玛格丽特，你们在做什么？！是谁把门打开了！

Hush Squad Commander: Didn't I say these deserters weren't your concern?
寂静小队军官：该死，我不是说，不要做多余的事情吗！

Hallway, Laplace
科算中心·走廊

A researcher stands before a derelict-looking office and knocks on the door.
访客站在弃置已久的办公室前，抬手敲响了门扉。

Enigma: Mr. Ludwig, were you looking for me?
哑谜：路德维希先生，你找我有什么事？

Enigma: I know I'm a little early, but if I had waited any longer, I would have been caught up by my bothersome fellow researchers again.
哑谜：是，我来得的确有点早，可再晚一些我就会被一些烦人的家伙缠住了。

Enigma: Are you in there?
哑谜：您在办公室里吗？

No answer comes. Faint burn marks on the door hint at a severe fire months ago.
无人应答，只有门上隐约的焦痕提醒着访客，在数个月前，这里曾经历过一场怎样的大火。

Suddenly, the sound of lab equipment crashing echoes from inside.
忽然间，门内传来实验器材翻倒的声响。

Enigma: Umm, Mr. Ludwig?
哑谜：路德维希先生……？

35  “最初的她” / "The Original Her"

Encrypted Telegram
加密电报

Classification: Top Secret
From: Zeno Headquarters
To: Zeno Branches

Research confirms that the truth behind the "Storm" is very likely tied to a high-value target known as "Urd." Our primary objective is to locate and make contact with this individual—note: we are seeking the "original her," prior to any influence from the "Storm." The identity of any presently existing individual holds no research value.

High command has established a facility known as the Loop Laboratory along the Don River, codename: "Amber Room." Its existence is classified at the highest level and is to be exempt from external inquiry of any kind. The Loop Laboratory has achieved structural immunity to the "Storm." Per orders, "deserters" have been transferred to the facility to undergo arcane experiments. All procedures are to be directed, documented, and overseen by our personnel only.

All branches are hereby notified of the following resource allocations:
...

Reminder: The Loop Laboratory is of the highest-priority. Any intelligence concerning the target Urd must be reported immediately.

——End of Telegram——
密级：最高
发信单位：芝诺总部
接收单位：芝诺各分部

研究确认，“暴雨”的真相极有可能隐藏在名为“兀尔德”的重要目标人物身上。当前我方的首要任务为定位并接触该目标——切记，是尚未受到“暴雨”影响的“最初的她”，其现存个体的身份不具备深入研究价值。

为达成上述目标，高层已于顿河沿岸建立回环实验室，代号“琥珀屋”，其存在为最高机密，不接受任何外部质询。回环实验室现已完成对“暴雨”的结构性免疫，依据指令，“逃兵们”已按序转移至该设施并接受神秘学实验。该实验流程由我方主导、记录与管理。

现通知各分部近期资源调配如下：
……

再次申明：回环实验室为芝诺当前最高优先级项目，有关目标人物兀尔德的一切信息须即时上报。

——电报结束——

36  拉普拉斯式事故 · 1/2 / A Typical Laplace Incident · 1/2

The substitute director immediately considers many possibilities: a lab accident, time travel, unstable alchemical elements ...
一瞬间，代理负责人脑海中闪过无数种可能：实验事故、时空穿越、炼金元素失控……

He pushes the door open and bursts inside.
最终，他焦急地推开门闯了进去。

Office, Laplace
科算中心·办公室

Enigma: Mr. Ludwig, are you there?
哑谜：路德维希先生，你在吗……？

Enigma: *cough* His office is even messier than mine.
哑谜：咳咳……这里怎么比我的办公室还要乱……

Several shadowy birds rush toward him, trailing smoke and ash.
黑暗中，猛然冲出数只裹挟着烟尘而行的不明生物。

???: Who—are—you?
？？？：你——是谁——

Enigma: ...?!
哑谜：……？！！！

37  蜂巢思维 / Hive Mind

Laplace Rehab Center Medical Record
拉普拉斯康复研究中心病历

Name: Ludwig
Department: Laplace Staff Committee
Reason: Out-of-body Experience (OBE)

Summary:
The patient recently suffered a severe episode of OBE during his research on Holiday Language. His consciousness has since fully recovered. However, his soul has now repeatedly transferred to his familiar without warning, accompanied by short-term memory loss, a weakened sense of reality, and distorted time perception. Prior records indicate the patient lacks sufficient awareness of the risks tied to OBE. Ongoing supervision and intervention are required. He also reports consuming an unidentified arcane substance resembling "honey." Its composition remains unknown and requires further analysis.

Symptoms:
Abnormal self-perception: disordered sense of time and gaps in memory.
OBE Duration: Episodes can last several hours, typically followed by short-term memory loss.

Experiment and Intervention Record:
Picrasma Candy (complementary medicine)
No significant improvement observed. The patient's only feedback concerned the taste.

Risk Assessment:
Relatively high, though the patient is showing signs of improvement. Ongoing observation is recommended, with particular attention to recurrence of the following:
1. Depersonalization-derealization disorder
2. Speech and language impairment
3. Assaulting colleagues without conscious awareness

Recommendations:
1. Extend medical observation period until symptoms subside.
2. Keep systematic records of the time, duration, and patient descriptions of each OBE.
3. Restrict the patient from high-intensity psychic research for the duration of the observation period.

Doctor's Note:
The patient has come to regard his OBE as perfectly normal. His nonchalance and near-total disregard for treatment lead me to believe that the rehab center's coffee machine, which requires weekly repairs, may actually be more "stable" than he is.
姓名：路德维希
部门：拉普拉斯人事委员会
就诊原因：灵魂出窍

病历摘要：
患者于假日语言研究期间经历了一次较为严重的灵魂离体状况，目前患者的意识已完全恢复。不过，自那以后，患者便陆续出现灵魂自主转移并附着于使魔的异常症状，多伴随短时记忆缺失、现实感知弱化及时间判断偏差。既往观察记录显示，患者对灵魂离体涉及的风险与并发症的警觉性不足，需进行持续性的监管与干预。患者声称自己曾食用过某种类似于“蜂蜜”的神秘学物质，该物质成分不明，有待进一步研究。

症状表现：
自我感知异常：表现为时间感知紊乱与记忆空白。
离体持续性：离体状态可持续数小时，事后患者通常会出现短期记忆缺失症状。

实验与干预记录：
苦目糖果（辅助药物）
服用后症状未见显著改善，患者仅对其口感作出了评价。

康复中心风险评估：
较高，但患者已有明显好转迹象。建议保持医学观察，尤其关注以下症状的复现概率：
1.人格-现实解体障碍
2.言语异常
3.无意识殴打同事

建议：
1.需延长医学观察期。
2.需系统性记录患者每次灵魂出窍发生的时间、持续时长及主观感受。
3.观察期间，需限制患者进行高强度的精神研究活动。

医师备注：
患者已将灵魂出窍视为一种日常状态，其从容的态度与近乎彻底放弃治疗的行为，让本人觉得康复中心每周必须维修一次的咖啡机，在“稳定运行”方面，似乎都比患者更加可靠。

38  拉普拉斯式事故 · 2/2 / A Typical Laplace Incident · 2/2

Ludwig: Seelentausch!
路德维希：灵魂移转！

An incantation is cast. The raven familiars lose their arcane connection, fading into mist and disappearing from the cluttered office.
咒语落下，渡鸦使魔失去了神秘术力量的来源，纷纷化作幻影，消失在一片狼藉的办公室中。

Enigma: What on ... Great! My colleagues have finally driven me to madness. Took them long enough.
哑谜：天啊，难道我终于被同事逼疯了？

Enigma: But just what the hell is going on here?
哑谜：还是说我在办公室撞鬼了？这里到底发生了什么？

Enigma: I told them the place needed a full renovation after that fire, but they didn't listen.
哑谜：我早就说过，在那场莫名的火灾后，就该把这里重新整修一次……

Ludwig: Excuse me, Mr. Adler.
路德维希：我很抱歉，阿德勒先生。

Ludwig: During my research on Holiday Language, my soul left my body for a period. Quite the spiritual adventure, I must say.
路德维希：之前在进行假日语言的研究时，我经历了一场导致灵魂出窍的事故——当然，那也可以说是一场特殊的灵魂旅程。

Ludwig: Since then, my soul has adopted a habit of transferring itself into my familiar at random. As you might imagine, it takes some getting used to—tends to muddle the memory a little too.
路德维希：自从苏醒之后，我偶尔会出现灵魂转移至使魔身上的情况，还伴随着记忆模糊的后遗症。

Ludwig: I was in the middle of sorting some files when it happened this time.
路德维希：没想到刚刚只是在收拾文件时沉思了一会儿……

Enigma: Concerning, to put it mildly. Perhaps you should have stayed at the rehab center longer.
哑谜：这听起来有灵魂离体的隐忧。也许你该在康复中心休息更长的时间。

Ludwig: Oh, no need to worry about me. I've had my share of research accidents. All part of the job.
路德维希：无碍。我知晓自身的情况，研究总是会带来各种各样的意外，我有充分的与意外共处的经验。

Ludwig: He takes a moment to straighten his collar.
路德维希：渡鸦先生细致地整理了一番衣领。

Ludwig: More importantly, Mr. Adler, have you made any progress on the arcane pigment investigation since our last meeting?
路德维希：我的个人事务并非重点。阿德勒先生，自上次会议之后，关于特殊矿物颜料的调查，是否有任何进展？

Ludwig: Be it from Institutum Lorentz or the Foundation investigator dispatched at our request.
路德维希：无论是来自洛伦兹研究所，抑或是拉普拉斯委派的基金会调查员。

Enigma: I did receive a report from Ms. Latham two days ago. She has made contact with the local Vigiles Bureau.
哑谜：两天前，莱瑟姆女士回传了最新的调查进度，她已经成功与当地的执夜队接洽。

Enigma: Apparently, some arcanist criminal organization is operating an extensive smuggling ring within Mexico City. It's been hindering her investigation in a number of ways.
哑谜：据说，这涉及墨西哥城当地的神秘学家走私势力，所以调查困难重重。

Ludwig: I worry for her safety. I do hope the Vigils look after her.
路德维希：希望夜巡特遣的人员能为她提供必要的安全保障。

Enigma: My thought exactly. I've made clear that her own safety is top priority. She should be under the Vigils' protection now.
哑谜：我已经叮嘱过，一切行动以她的安全为先。如果夜巡特遣没有玩忽职守，那她应该已经得到了保护。

Enigma: While we're ruminating on the topic, there's something I feel needs clarification.
哑谜：另外……关于颜料这档事，我想我有必要弄清一个问题。

Enigma: I had thought the pigment investigation was a request from the Staff Committee, but you're the only member who has shown any interest in its progress. The request came directly from you, didn't it?
哑谜：我本以为这是人事委员会授意的行动，但现在看来并非如此。除了你之外，委员会中没有任何人关心过任务的进展。也许我应该把这件事当成是你的个人关切？

Ludwig: Well deduced, Mr. Adler.
路德维希：你的推论没错，阿德勒先生。

Ludwig: You've heard about the conflict between the internal factions of the Foundation, I assume? Inevitably, its impacts have affected Laplace as well.
路德维希：无论你是否有听说过基金会内部趋于白热化的派系斗争，它已经不可避免地影响了拉普拉斯。

Ludwig: After I awoke from my coma at the rehab center, Commissioner Pedra paid me a visit. He warned me that over half of the Staff Committee supports the proposal of a comprehensive policy reform at Laplace.
路德维希：在拉普拉斯人事委员会，有半数的委员支持拉普拉斯进行“暴雨”人事改革，这是我苏醒之后，佩德拉委员告诉我的第一件事。

Ludwig: Should it pass, Laplace may lose its independence and become a full subsidiary of the Foundation.
路德维希：如果提案通过，这意味着在“暴雨”时期，拉普拉斯将完全成为基金会的下属机构，丧失自主研究权。

Ludwig: He asked me to join the Staff Committee and oppose this policy reform. I agreed.
路德维希：他希望我能加入拉普拉斯人事委员会，反对人事改革的推进。我同意了。

Enigma: Oh, I see. Then if Laplace develops enough groundbreaking "Storm" technologies before the Foundation does, we can turn the tables on them.
哑谜：如果拉普拉斯能够抢在基金会之前，掌握更多与“暴雨”相关的技术——

Ludwig: Indeed, it would give us better leverage, if nothing else.
路德维希：是的，这会是一项有力的谈判条件。保持独立的条件。

Ludwig: But we don't have much time on our hands.
路德维希：当然，这只是理想情况。而现实是，我们的时间已经不多了。

Ludwig: Their Hawks are gaining support from more commissioners as we speak. For now, all I can do is delay the proposal's review process.
路德维希：“鹰派”已经收拢了人事委员会中的不少人。目前我只能设法拖延提案的评审时间。

Enigma: Tch, typical politics.
哑谜：……该死，又是这一套。

Enigma: You once led the Plesetsk branch, Mr. Ludwig, and as I am sure you can see, I have never been one for matters of politics or leadership.
哑谜：路德维希先生，你也曾就任过普列谢茨克分部的负责人，你应该能看出，我并不适合坐在这个位置。

Enigma: I was only appointed to maximize the chance of preserving and continuing Director Lucy's research.
哑谜：当初的决定，只是建立在能最大程度保留与推进露西女士的研究成果的可能上。

Enigma: That is to say, is there any chance we could bring Director Lucy back to Laplace?
哑谜：假如……我是说，有没有可能，将露西女士请回来——

Ludwig: Whether possible or not, our first step in either case will be to stop this policy reform.
路德维希：这一切的前提是，人事改革无法推进。

Ludwig: If Ms. Latham's investigation yields no significant progress soon, all we can do is wait for good news from Lorentz Butterfly.
路德维希：如果莱瑟姆女士的调查没有更多进展，我们只能期待洛伦兹蝴蝶的好消息了。

Ludwig: I'm confident she'll bring us something. She's always been one step ahead.
路德维希：毕竟她总是有办法先人一步。

Enigma: You sound quite certain of her. But we know nothing about this Lorentz Butterfly aside from a few unorthodox papers and provocative letters.
哑谜：她？可是除了一些措辞挑衅的信件和思路奇异的论文，我们对洛伦兹蝴蝶一无所知。

The bird-headed scientist chooses to remain silent, but the cryptic response proves a poor choice when dealing with an expert cryptologist.
闻言，面前的鸟头科学家并没有作答，只是用沉默示意。

Enigma: Wait a minute. Impossible!
哑谜：等等，难道……这不可能！

Enigma: You know who Lorentz Butterfly is, don't you?
哑谜：路德维希先生，你知道洛伦兹蝴蝶的真实身份，对吗！

Ludwig: Hmm. It was the year of 1999. At the time, I was a visiting professor at Cambridge University. There was a student in my class, far too brilliant for her exceptionally young age.
路德维希：1999年，我在剑桥大学当客座教授讲课。在当时，班上有一个过分聪敏而年轻的旁听生。

Ludwig: The staff told me she wasn't an enrolled student. She simply came every week to attend my lecture.
路德维希：大学的职工告诉我，她并非本校的学生，只是每周都会在固定的时间前来旁听。

Ludwig: I had intended to recommend her for admission, but, of course, the "Storm" disrupted everything.
路德维希：我本想将她推荐入学，但很可惜，当时的时代剧变让我无法再延续教职。

Ludwig: I ended up leaving Cambridge and joining Laplace myself.
路德维希：最终，在课程尚未结束之时，我离开了剑桥，加入拉普拉斯。

Ludwig: After the second "Storm," I paid the university a visit and saw that young lady again.
路德维希：第二次“暴雨”后，因缘际会，我得以重返一趟剑桥。我重新见到了那个学生。

Enigma: Are you saying she survived multiple "Storms" on her own?
哑谜：她……洛伦兹蝴蝶，她用自己的方式越过了“暴雨”。

Ludwig: Yes. Though she didn't share the details, I could tell she was hiding a secret, or running from some serious danger.
路德维希：是的，如你所想。当时我察觉到，她似乎在竭力隐藏着某些秘密，或者说，在躲避着什么危险的势力。

Ludwig: She had an exceptional gift for the sciences. Naturally, I invited her to join Laplace.
路德维希：她身上有着某种天赋，毫无疑问，她是一位天生的科研者。我希望她能加入拉普拉斯。

Enigma: But from what little we know of her, she's ... dangerous.
哑谜：她和我们那些疯子科研怪人同事不一样，她……很危险。

Ludwig: I trust you would not mistake me for an idealist, Mr. Adler.
路德维希：我并不是一位理想主义者，阿德勒先生。

Ludwig: Dangerous as she may be, we need her help, especially in times like these.
路德维希：我们需要她的帮助，尤其是在“暴雨”的时代之中。

Ludwig: That said, should she become completely out of control ...
路德维希：当然，如果最终，她的危险程度变得不可控制——

Ludwig: I hope to be her one and only victim.
路德维希：希望受害者名单上只有我一人。

Enigma: You have done little to reassure me, Mr. Ludwig.
哑谜：这听起来可不是什么控制危险因素的好办法，路德维希先生。

Enigma: But perhaps you're right. We must keep pushing forward at any cost.
哑谜：是啊……“暴雨”之下，不管采用什么办法，我们都不能停止前进的脚步。

Enigma: Everyone's so doggedly determined. It's time I also mustered the strength to stay the course.
哑谜：所有人都是如此坚定，而我最好也有一样的信心。

Enigma: I will continue to monitor Ms. Latham's progress.
哑谜：我会再去跟进莱瑟姆女士的调查进度的。

39  一个普通的人 / An Ordinary Citizen

The two accused "murderers" at last reach their safehouse to regroup.
经过兵荒马乱的一夜，两名闯入拍卖会的“凶手”终于得以回到临时的居所。

Somehow, this empty little hovel, bathed in moonlight in a deserted cemetery, manages to feel cozy and welcoming.
月下空屋，荒废的墓地，无主的棺材——机械女孩从未想过这些场景也能够如此温馨。

Loggerhead: Yeah, yeah, that's right. Oh, about that ...
空脑袋：是的是的……啊这个请稍等……

Loggerhead: Could you wait, Mr. Adler? I need a second to replace my reel so I can answer you properly.
空脑袋：阿德勒先生，请让我调取另一卷胶片来回答这个问题……

Loggerhead speaks into her communicator. Beside her, Ramona sets down their fellow escapee and begins carefully cleaning her of the dirt and dusty footprints she had gathered in the fight.
机械女孩暂时结束了与拉普拉斯负责人的联系。在她的身侧，除灵师正专注地拭去画像在混乱中沾染的脚印和污渍。

Lady by the Lake: Oh, your technique is most delicate, my dear lady. Were you once a professional art restorer? Or a collector, perhaps?
《湖边的女人》：亲爱的女士，你是不是在美术馆里头工作过？还是说，你是一位专业的艺术品收藏家？你的手法轻柔、专业……

Ramona: No, none of that. I can't say I've got much experience with art. I just expel arcane energy from crime scenes.
拉莫纳：画中女士，比起美术馆，更多的时候，我在和案发现场打交道。

Lady by the Lake: Is that so? Well, you should know that I happen to be carrying quite a number of murder weapons and a few corpses within me.
《湖边的女人》：噢，怪不得！我的画里也藏着许多的尸体和作案凶器——

Loggerhead: Ah, we did it! The three of us made it out of the serpent's den in one piece. Smile at my camera and say cheese.
空脑袋：呼……三人转危为安，真是圆满的情节。请允许我记录下这一幕！

Loggerhead: Hey, you know what? This reminds me of The Three Musketeers. We're just like them. Oh, if only I could keep investigating with you guys.
空脑袋：我们就像《三个火枪手》一样！如果之后我还能和你们一起调查就好了……

Ramona: "If only"? You got other plans?
拉莫纳：什么意思——接下来的时间，你打算做什么？

Ramona notices the uncertainty in Loggerhead's voice.
除灵师敏锐地察觉出她语气之中的踌躇。

Loggerhead: N-No, nothing like that. It's just that I don't think I can continue my investigation any time soon.
空脑袋：我并没有什么打算，只是、只是……拉莫纳小姐，我可能无法参与后续的行动了。

Loggerhead: You see, I need to replace some components in my head, else I could suffer a continuous memory loss. *sigh* The components were supposed to last longer than this.
空脑袋：我需要更换脑袋里的一些零件，如果不进行处理的话，我的记忆将无法继续被妥善保存。本来尚未到损耗更换时间的……

Ramona: Did they get you?
拉莫纳：在昨晚的冲突中，你受伤了？

Ramona: I was sure I deflected that bullet.
拉莫纳：这不可能，我并没有让那颗子弹真正击中你。

Loggerhead: Yeah, you did. You saved my life.
空脑袋：不是的，你的的确确救了我！

Loggerhead: But you never can tell when a gear will foul up. Ugh, of course the mishap that tops my list of Most Unfortunate Malfunctions had to happen now!
空脑袋：但机器总会有故障的时候，这完全是“我最想避免的糟糕意外”事件榜上的第一名……

Loggerhead: The bright side is, we've confirmed the club's involvement in the murders, and knowing you, Ms. Ramona, I'm sure you can solve—
空脑袋：好在我们已经确认了俱乐部的嫌疑，凭拉莫纳小姐你对墨西哥城的熟悉程度，你一定可以——

Ramona: Hey, this isn't on me. It's on them.
拉莫纳：并非是“你”，而是“他们”。

Ramona: I reported our findings to the Vigiles Bureau after we got outta there.
拉莫纳：昨夜离开拍卖会之后，我已经把现场发生的事情上报夜巡特遣管理局了。

She has neglected to mention that they took something from said auction.
当然，她略去了她们从现场带走了一件拍卖品的事实。

Ramona: They'll send their agents to raid the club and run Pérez into the ground. I'd bet on it.
拉莫纳：接下来，他们会派人追踪俱乐部成员的去向，把躲在幕后的佩雷斯揪出来。

Ramona: I'm not sure how I let myself get roped into this mess in the first place.
拉莫纳：这起连环案件，终归不是我一个人的事儿。

Loggerhead: Oh, oh, ah, I'm, I'm so sorry. You're right. I shouldn't have assumed you were some lone wolf hell-bent on protecting the city.
空脑袋：抱歉，我只是觉得你像游走在城市背面的独行侠，而且你一直对“合作”不感兴趣……

Ramona: Yeah, you've got it right. I clear out arcane energy, not criminals.
拉莫纳：希望你没有忘记，我并不是一名执夜人，只是受雇于夜巡特遣管理局。

She tries to put on a brave face, to appear calm and detached from the situation.
她语气平直，脸上也并未见结束工作的喜悦。

But she can't help looking over to Loggerhead with concern.
也许是因为，面前机械女孩的虚弱状态并不是她期望中的结果。

Ramona: You two stay here for now. Place should be safe from the club's goons.
拉莫纳：眼下，绿松石蛇俱乐部还在外头搜捕我们的踪迹，你们先暂时留在这里避避风头吧。

Lady by the Lake: Safe and free at last. I simply couldn't ask for more. Thank you so very much for dragging me out of that torturous place.
《湖边的女人》：这太好了——女士们，感谢你们把我从那个暗无天日的地方中拯救出来！

Lady by the Lake: This might sound a little forward, but might you possibly find me somewhere to be hung up, somewhere pleasant, please?
《湖边的女人》：虽然有点冒昧，也许你们可以找到一个适合悬挂我的地方。

Lady by the Lake: Oh, how I long for a place to call home, preferably among fellow arcanists. Though I certainly wouldn't say no to such a lovely cemetery either.
《湖边的女人》：啊，我非常渴望有一个稳定的、能够与人交流的归所，最好还是神秘学家的住处！如果是一片墓地也不错～

Ramona: ...
拉莫纳：……

The lady in the painting makes her point clear.
画中女士的暗示之意已经非常明显。

Lady by the Lake: Ms. Ramona, you'll not be disappointed by my services, I assure you. I can hide all manner of wicked secrets within my frame, for example.
《湖边的女人》：亲爱的除灵女孩，我绝对可以为你的工作提供许多帮助，藏起你所有最不见光的秘密！

Lady by the Lake: *sigh* Manus Vindictae forced me to swallow all sorts of rubbish in France, only to be smuggled across the Atlantic and thrown into the darkest corner of the Turquoise Serpent Club. The memories torment me still.
《湖边的女人》：在此之前，我在法国的重塑之手据点里勉强度日，又被其中一个成员运到了墨西哥，最后被无情地丢到了俱乐部……

Lady by the Lake: Oh, which reminds me, though I was used as a dumping ground by the Manus, if I search carefully, I could still find something useful, even valuable.
《湖边的女人》：虽然大多数时刻我都被当成了垃圾场，但只要耐心在其中寻找，总能发现一两件不错的珍品。

Lady by the Lake: A moment, if you will. Allow me to find you a suitable gift.
《湖边的女人》：让我给你一些见面礼吧！

She tips over and spills out a pile of strange knick-knacks and outright junk.
她自顾自地倾斜了身体，吐出一堆很难被归类为有用的物品。

Lady by the Lake: Ah, this is a miraculous mirror I received at a market in Paris. Look into it, and you'll see a beard fit to measure sitting right upon your face. A dream come true for a bare-faced beard wisher.
《湖边的女人》：瞧，这面我在巴黎集市上得到的镜子！它会在镜中显现出你长着胡子的模样，而且绝佳适配你的脸型！它适合所有渴望胡子却无法拥有胡子的人。

Lady by the Lake: This little conch isn't half bad either. A sailor, drunk as a skunk, I might add, threw it at me when I was stranded on the Mediterranean coast.
《湖边的女人》：这个螺号也不错，是我被扔在地中海岸边时，一位酩酊大醉的水手扔进画中的。

Lady by the Lake: The music it creates could rival a siren's song, I tell you. Though, it does carry the faint whiff of that sailor's rummy breath.
《湖边的女人》：它吹奏出来的乐声无异于塞壬的咏唱，美中不足的是，它至今还残留着一丝酒气……

Seeing Ramona's expression darken, Loggerhead signals for the painting to stop her sales pitch.
眼见除灵师的脸色变得难看起来，机械女孩连忙打断了画作的自我推销。

Loggerhead: Oh, oh, painting lady, I just thought of the perfect place for you!
空脑袋：噢噢，画中女士，我想到一个地方了！

Loggerhead: The Timekeeper's Suitcase! It's clean and always bright and has a huge backyard called the Wilderness to boot.
空脑袋：司辰的箱子完美符合你的愿景。那里不仅洁净、明亮，还有一大片广阔的荒原，非常适合观光散步。

Loggerhead: More importantly, you'll meet all kinds of wonderful people there.
空脑袋：更重要的是，在箱子里，你一定可以找到志同道合的伙伴！

Lady by the Lake: Oh la la! A chance for rebirth sent straight from the heavens! I can hardly wait!
《湖边的女人》：天啊，这简直是一次重生。我等不及了。

Loggerhead: I'll ask the Timekeeper about it once I get back to the Foundation.
空脑袋：等我回到基金会，就帮你问问司辰！

Ramona gives them both a knowing look, attempting to reel the two Awakened back to the situation at hand.
无视墓地主人送客的眼神，两位意识唤醒者已经自顾自达成了协议。

Suddenly, she senses movement outside. Footsteps approach from just beyond the cemetery gate.
忽然间，感受到不远处属于生者的动静，女孩面色一凝。
随着脚步声，一道意料之外的身影出现在墓园入口。

Ramona: You just couldn't leave me and the spirits here alone, huh?
拉莫纳：真是够了，我并不想在这里见到除了鬼以外的东西。

Añil: Forgive the intrusion, Ms. Ramona. Might I have a moment of your time?
野木蓝：打扰了，拉莫纳小姐。我们能聊一会儿吗？

Ramona: Sure, if you don't mind standing. Don't have any drinks to offer, either.
拉莫纳：我这儿没有待客用的茶杯，甚至没有一张能坐下的椅子，希望你不会介意。

Ramona: Living with the dead usually means peace and quiet. Not so much tonight.
拉莫纳：当然，唯一的好处是，这儿很安静，只有死者会永恒地陪伴我们……至少是曾经很安静。

She leads the visitor aside. Nearby, the two Awakened lean in to listen.
墓园主人将访客领至一旁。身侧，两位意识唤醒者好奇地支起不存在的耳朵。

Añil: You and your friends certainly made your presence known at the auction.
野木蓝：您和您的同伴，在拍卖会上非常引人瞩目。

Añil: I hear every gang in the city is trying to sniff you out.
野木蓝：如今，绿松石蛇俱乐部发动了大半个城市的黑帮，在寻找当日凶手的踪迹。

Añil: Ms. Luisa even put a bounty of fifty thousand Sharpodonties on your heads.
野木蓝：路易莎女士说，谁能抓到你们，谁就能获得五万利齿子儿的悬赏金额。哪怕仅是提供线索，都会受到俱乐部的礼遇。

Ramona: As nice as it is for you to come here and tell me I'm a wanted woman.
拉莫纳：我并不是很高兴听到我的名下也有高额悬赏。

Ramona: I doubt those goons can find this old place. And clearly, you're not here on their behalf, either.
拉莫纳：不过，这片荒废的墓地足够偏远，俱乐部的家伙们暂时找不到这里来。想必你不是代表着俱乐部来和我说话的。

Añil: Correct. I'm here to ...
野木蓝：是的，我……

He stops himself then shifts the topic.
面前的访客欲言又止，最终抛出了毫不相干的问题。

Añil: I just wanted to ask you something. You attended the auction to investigate the serial murders, yes? Were you able to track down the culprit?
野木蓝：我记得您是为了城市中一起连环命案而前往拍卖会的。您的调查，是否已经有结果了？

Ramona: You serious? You saw what happened. I was framed for the sixth murder.
拉莫纳：你应该知晓了，第六起案件就发生在拍卖会的现场，而我莫名成了案件的真凶。

Ramona: From now on, smoking out Pérez is the Vigiles business, not mine.
拉莫纳：如今，执夜队已经正式介入，接下来就是漫长的“猫鼠游戏”——他们会逮到佩雷斯的。

Ramona: You didn't seriously expect me to catch the bastard, did you?
拉莫纳：你应该不会指望我这个除灵师，一举抓到俱乐部的幕后之人吧？

Añil: No need to be modest. The Vigiles would never have gotten this close were it not for the crucial information you provided.
野木蓝：您已经为夜巡特遣提供了足够的线索，推进了案件的调查进展。

Añil: But there's something else. I suspect Mr. Pérez isn't the true culprit.
野木蓝：只是……我担忧，佩雷斯先生并非幕后元凶。

He takes a deep breath and produces the list he got from Pérez.
他深吸一口气，取出那一份从佩雷斯身上获得的走私名单。

Añil: I've been thinking it over all morning. You should see this.
野木蓝：我思考了一整日，认为您需要看看这个。

Añil: I met a young woman. She doesn't work for the Vigiles Bureau, nor does she intend to stop the next murder. But she did help me find this list.
野木蓝：夜巡特遣管理局并非唯一调查此案的势力。我们找到了这份名单，但……那位女士，我不认为她会阻止后续的命案发生。

Añil: I dare not fathom what might happen if we don't do something. This could be your last chance.
野木蓝：我无法预测，她的旁观会带来怎样的后果。这也许是最后的机会。

Ramona: Last chance for what?
拉莫纳：什么样的机会？

Ramona: Actually, forget it. I got a hunch I won't like what you're gonna say.
拉莫纳：算了，你别告诉我。我有预感，我可能不愿意听见接下来的话。

Ramona accepts the lead, once again thrust into the role of reluctant investigator.
尽管如此，临时侦探仍然接过了对方带来的线索。

Añil: Thank you, miss. You have a kind heart.
野木蓝：谢谢您，拉莫纳小姐。您是一个非常好的人。
