Añil: I wish you every success with your investigation.
野木蓝：我衷心祝您调查顺利，如愿以偿。

As he disappears from view, the others begin to look over his list.
访客如来时那般悄声离去了。除灵师打开那份名单。

Loggerhead: Golly, a critical clue delivered right to your doorstep! You really do have influence in this city, Ms. Ramona.
空脑袋：哇，送上门的线索！拉莫纳小姐，你不愧是城市的影子，四处果然都是你的耳目。

Ramona: Hey, painting lady, you ever seen or heard of any of these people?
拉莫纳：画中女士，你对这名单中出现的人物，有什么印象吗？

Lady by the Lake: Let's see. Oh! Indeed I do. I know them quite well.
《湖边的女人》：让我看看……噢！！当、当然！我认识他们！

Lady by the Lake: These are all members of Manus Vindictae. A few I have not met in person, but I recognize their names.
《湖边的女人》：他们都是重塑之手的追随者。有一些我没有见过的家伙，但我记得他们的名字。

Lady by the Lake: To think I once considered them friends, oh, what a foolish collection of brushstrokes I am!
《湖边的女人》：虽然我后来发现，他们也许从头到尾，就没有把我当成同伴。哈哈……

Lady by the Lake: Though I must admit the days we spent in Paris were among the few truly happy moments in my life.
《湖边的女人》：我们短暂地共处过一段时间，不得不说，在巴黎的那段日子，是我人生中为数不多的快乐片段……

Ramona clears her throat, attempting to steer the lady away from an over-long trip down memory lane.
女孩不得不打断再次陷入伤感与回忆的画作，让话题回到正轨。

Ramona: This list shows the Manus members responsible for smuggling pigment and coordinating with the club.
拉莫纳：我想我弄清楚了。这个名单——他们都是负责走私矿物颜料，与俱乐部接洽的重塑之手成员。

Loggerhead: So if we find them, we find the arcane pigment. Let's do it.
空脑袋：那他们身上，一定有我正在寻找的矿物颜料！

Ramona: Settle down, Latham. See the time stamps here? Their last smuggling run was a while ago.
拉莫纳：别冲动，莱瑟姆。你看上面的日期，最近的走私时间，都已经是一周以前。

Ramona: For all we know, they've already skipped out of Mexico entirely.
拉莫纳：据我所知，这份名单上的家伙们在走私结束后，就都离开了墨西哥。

Ramona: Then again, why would Añil give me this list?
拉莫纳：但野木蓝把这份名单交给我，一定有他的理由。

Ramona: Pérez isn't our guy? What did I miss? Is the answer somewhere on this list?
拉莫纳：如果他说的是真的，凶手并非佩雷斯，那代表着至今的调查出现了疏漏之处……而关键就藏在这份名单上。

She looks over the names, attempting to find some connection.
她的视线扫过这些稀奇古怪的名字，尝试与自己的记忆关联起来。

Inside her mind, a map of the city lights up spot by spot.
脑海中，城市的地图被逐一点亮。

Ramona: Ledru-Rollin. Stayed at Rosa's inn for three days. Booked it after burning down her kitchen.
拉莫纳：勒德-罗朗。他在罗莎的饭店住了三天，把罗莎的厨房烧毁后逃之夭夭。

Ramona: Rosa asked me to track him down using my "ancestral psychic ability." Hah, pretty disappointed when I told her I'm not a psychic, neither were my parents—frauds the both of 'em.
拉莫纳：罗莎找上了我，希望我能用家族的神秘术来追缉这位可恶的房客。我只能遗憾地告诉她，我并不会所谓的“通灵”。我的父母都是骗子。

Lady by the Lake: I remember him. I often saw him tinkering with dangerous-looking alchemical materials. The poor man seemed to have lost his mind the moment he put on that mask.
《湖边的女人》：我记得这个家伙，他总是在捣鼓着一些危险的炼金术材料，自从他戴上面具之后，头脑就没有一天是清醒的。

Loggerhead: Maybe that's why he blew up Rosa's kitchen.
空脑袋：也许就是他失败的炼金实验摧毁了那个厨房。可怜的罗莎！

Ramona: Diggers. He's broken into more than one government facility, claiming he only wanted to see the works of the muralism movement up close.
拉莫纳：挖掘艺术。他尝试潜入一些政府要地，宣称要近距离观摩壁画复兴运动的巨作。

Ramona: The guards and patrol officers threw him out, of course. Got a friend in the government, Diago. He told me all about this guy.
拉莫纳：但因为行踪诡异，最终被巡逻的警察赶了出去。在政府就职的迪亚哥和我提过这件事。

Lady by the Lake: Oh, that hip fellow! Still alive, is he? I haven't heard a word from him since he went to Tierra del Fuego with the Preacher.
《湖边的女人》：那个嬉皮士，原来他还活着！自从他跟随传道者前往火地岛后，我就再也没有听过他的消息。

Ramona: Fitzwilliam. A journalist. Recently published an article in El Semanario Heraldo de México, featuring his interview with a foreign diplomat.
拉莫纳：菲茨威廉。他在《墨西哥城先驱周报》发表了一篇采访外交官的报道。

Ramona: Style was totally bloated. Only made it into the city's best-selling paper 'cause of the diplomat's status. Rumor is he gave him all kinds of stuff to make sure he got the interview.
拉莫纳：那是一篇言辞浮夸的文章，能登上本地最大的报纸，只是占了题材之便——听说为了接触这位外交官，他给对方送了不少好东西。

Lady by the Lake: Alas, Fitzwilliam! Mr. Pérez was sick to death of the greed of that man.
《湖边的女人》：佩雷斯先生对这个贪婪的家伙厌烦极了。

Lady by the Lake: One day, as I was sitting in the warehouse, I spied him barge in and make off with all manner of valuables.
《湖边的女人》：在那天，哪怕我待在仓库的角落，都能听到菲茨威廉的动静：他闯了进来，像个强盗一样带走了好多东西！

Lady by the Lake: A golden pendant, an antique statuette, a turquoise mask, and even a most exquisite quetzal tiara. Talk about greedy!
《湖边的女人》：他要走了佩雷斯先生用作展览的黄金挂饰、神像、绿松石面具、绿咬鹃羽毛头饰……

Lady by the Lake: He also stole a few paintings, ones depicting indigenous people and Mexican mythology, I believe, before stuffing everything into a giant bag and disappearing.
《湖边的女人》：最后，他又要走了几幅画，把这些东西都打包起来。

Ramona: Paintings?
拉莫纳：你刚刚提到了，画？

Lady by the Lake: Yes, dear. There are countless works of art in the club.
《湖边的女人》：亲爱的，俱乐部里最不缺的就是艺术品！

Lady by the Lake: In fact, I heard that diplomat has a particular interest in art exhibitions.
《湖边的女人》：那位外交官先生，对于即将要举办的大众艺术展非常感兴趣。

Lady by the Lake: Perhaps it was those very paintings that Fitzwilliam used to curry favor with him.
《湖边的女人》：也许菲茨威廉就是借着那些描绘原住民与墨西哥神话的画作，来和对方攀上关系的。

Every detail from each crime scene flashes through Ramona's thoughts.
所有犯罪现场的细节在女孩脑海中一一闪过。

Then it clicks into place. The picture, once fuzzy, becomes clear.
电光石火之间，如同在一团乱麻中找到了线头，她抓住了一切的关键。

Ramona: How could I not see it? The key to solving these killings isn't in the Turquoise Serpent Club.
拉莫纳：我完全错了……将这一切连环案件串联起来的，并非绿松石蛇俱乐部。

Lady by the Lake: Whatever do you mean?
《湖边的女人》：你说什么？

Ramona: The first murder, a vagrant drowned next to a pile of trash out in the desert. Case was freaky. Got passed to the Vigiles Bureau cause of it.
拉莫纳：最初的一起案件，发生在干旱的荒郊中。一位流浪汉在沙漠中溺水，他的死状引起了警察注意，最终此案被转移至夜巡特遣管理局。

Ramona: The second, a businessman drowns before falling into his own vivarium, only to be shredded to pieces by his own Critters.
拉莫纳：第二起案件，发生在一位富商的玻璃房内。由于在死亡后跌入了生态缸，他被自己违法饲养的魔精撕成了碎片。

Ramona: The third collapsed in a bustling plaza.
拉莫纳：第三起，则在一处人来人往的开放广场。

Ramona: The fourth, in a monastery. The fifth, in an apartment. No link found between any of 'em, except for the way they died.
拉莫纳：第四起与第五起，分别发生在修道院与文化公寓里。除了死状，执夜人在这些案件中间找不到任何相似之处。

Ramona: It was right in front of me the whole time, in the junkyard, in the monastery's office, and on the plaza's wall.
拉莫纳：但其实，真正的关键，就明晃晃地躺在流浪汉聚集的垃圾场，挂在修道院的办公室，绘制在广场的外墙上——

Ramona: Latham, you remember that scorched strip of cloth we found at the fifth crime scene?
拉莫纳：莱瑟姆，你还记得我们在第五处案发现场的灶台里找到的布条吗？

Loggerhead: Yeah, I remember. The one with three symbols on it, right?
空脑袋：噢，我记得！你说的是那个绘制着三个符号的布条？

Loggerhead: It's the logo of the Turquoise Serpent Club. That's what led us to the auction.
空脑袋：后来我们发现，那是绿松石蛇俱乐部的符号，所以我们才会前往拍卖会寻找线索。

Ramona: But it isn't about the logo. What matters is the strip itself.
拉莫纳：是的，但其实重点并非在那个符号，而是布条本身。

Ramona: It was linen, part of the canvas of a burned-up painting.
拉莫纳：那是亚麻布——它是一张油画布。那张烧焦的布条，是某张画作遗留下来的一部分。

Loggerhead: Painting? But what about the sixth murder? That lady was killed right in front of us.
空脑袋：那第六起案件呢？那位参加拍卖会的母亲，她是在我们身边遇害的。

Loggerhead: And there weren't any paintings near—
空脑袋：在当时，我们身边并没有任何画作——

Her reel stops in place.
空脑袋：机械女孩忽然顿住了。

Loggerhead: Wait.
空脑袋：不对。

Ramona: Exactly. A painting was being auctioned off at the time. We should've seen it sooner.
拉莫纳：你发现了。我们竟然忽视了如此明显的线索：那位观众遇害时，台上正在进行着画作的拍卖。

Ramona: It all makes sense. Every single one of the crime scenes had a painting near it.
拉莫纳：所有的受害者，他们身边都必定有一幅画作的存在——这正是凶手展开神秘学仪式的关键。

Ramona: The key to solving the case is in the paintings.
拉莫纳：画作，才是这起连环案件的关键！

Lady by the Lake: Then, what Fitzwilliam brought to the diplomat would be ...
《湖边的女人》：如果画作是谋杀案的关键，那菲茨威廉交给外交官的……

Loggerhead: We need to warn him!
空脑袋：我们得赶快警告他这件事！

Ramona: He's at a party near Ms. Juárez's family ofrenda, a businesswoman.
拉莫纳：今夜是诸灵节。这位外交官参加了富商华雷斯女士所举办的庆典，地点就在华雷斯宅邸的家族祭坛附近。

Ramona: If we move fast, we might still be able to get to him before the killer does.
拉莫纳：如果我们现在赶过去，也许能来得及找到他。活着的他。

Loggerhead: Roger that, partner. Operation Save-the-Day is a go.
空脑袋：那我们出发吧，搭档！

Even with a solid lead on the means and the killer's next target, Ramona can't shake the feeling that something is still missing.
尽管已经锁定了目标，但除灵师心中诡异的感觉仍缭绕不散，仿佛有什么细节并未厘清。

Ramona: No, Latham.
拉莫纳：不。你不能去。

Ramona: You're injured. You should stay here with the lady.
拉莫纳：你受伤了，莱瑟姆。你必须和画中女士留在这里。

Loggerhead: But lone wolves in the movies almost always meet a tragic end. I can't let a friend get into trouble by themselves.
空脑袋：等等，拉莫纳——我知道你习惯了独来独往地办事，但电影里的“独行侠”大多结局并不美好，我不能让同伴孤身一人面对凶手，最后陷入危险！

Ramona: Neither can I.
拉莫纳：我也同样。

Loggerhead: H-Huh? Wait, you mean I'm your friend too?
空脑袋：什、什么……？！等等，你的意思是，我是你的——

Ramona fights against responding with a smile by diving into her thoughts.
没来得及理会陷入震惊的机械女孩，除灵师仍然独自思索着。

Ramona: But something's still fishy.
拉莫纳：这件事——它有点不对劲。

Ramona: All these victims, diplomat included, look familiar to me. But why?
拉莫纳：这些案件的受害者，前往拍卖会的母亲，外交官……为什么这些人的外貌，总有着说不上来的熟悉感？

Ramona: Where else have I seen them besides the newspapers and reports?
拉莫纳：新闻报纸，夜巡特遣管理局的案件报告，除了这些，我还在哪里见过这些面孔？

Ramona: And more importantly, why me?
拉莫纳：最重要的是：为什么是我？

Ramona: Why am I the one who found all these clues, huh? Just because I was at the scene? 'Cause I "know the city like the back of my hand"?
拉莫纳：为什么，是我发现了这一切？仅仅因为，我是接触过犯罪现场的除灵师吗？如他们所说，我在这座城市无所不知？

Ramona: Or because I happen to be a descendant of a famous "psychic" family? Pft. Don't make me laugh.
拉莫纳：多可笑的名头……就和他们尝试让我成为的“通灵大师”一样。

Ramona: No, this isn't a coincidence. It can't be.
拉莫纳：我是不会相信，这一切仅是巧合的。

Ramona heads toward the street without hesitation.
女孩转身，没有丝毫迟疑地离开了墓地。

Loggerhead: Ramona, hold on.
空脑袋：等等，拉莫纳——

Loggerhead, limping, can't keep up.
一瘸一拐的机械女孩无法追上除灵师的脚步，对方消失在了无边的黑暗之中。

40  亡灵小径 / Path of the Dead

Family Ofrenda
家族祭坛

The night is made hazy with the smoke of an uncountable number of candles.
夜色朦胧，烛光绚烂。

The ofrenda holds food and drinks. Marigolds glow in vivid colors. Pictures and paintings of the dead smile down on the living.
祭坛上摆满食物与饮品，万寿菊色泽明亮，已故之人的照片与画像含笑注视着人间。

Wealthy Businesswoman: Ladies and gentlemen, it is my great honor to join you in celebrating All Souls' Day on this fabulous evening.
富商：欢迎！有幸邀请各位共度诸灵之夜。

Wealthy Businesswoman: Ah, you must be Mr. Mack from the United States. How are you enjoying your stay in Mexico City? Just as I promised, is it not?
富商：迈克先生，还记得您在美国时和我的通信，怎么样？是否如我所承诺那般不虚此行？

Guest: Mighty fine indeed! Thanks again for your invitation, ma'am. I have to say, I've been eager to meet you in person.
宾客：当然！百闻不如一见，华雷斯女士，非常感谢您慷慨的邀请，我期待这一天实在太久太久。

Guest: I think I'm falling in love with this city already.
宾客：我已经开始爱上墨西哥城了。

Wealthy Businesswoman: Well, the party's only just begun. Many more truly unprecedented experiences await. Believe me.
富商：相信我——您一定会拥有一次与众不同的亡灵之旅。

An unexpected visitor appears at the entrance.
一道意料之外的身影出现在入口。

Wealthy Businesswoman: Well, look who it is. A pleasant surprise indeed.
富商：噢，瞧瞧这是谁！意外之喜！

Wealthy Businesswoman: I thought you weren't coming, Mr. Añil.
富商：之前邀请你的时候，你并没有应下，我还以为你不会来了，野木蓝。

Añil: Good evening, Ms. Juárez.
野木蓝：晚上好，华雷斯女士。

Wealthy Businesswoman: I owe you my thanks for your beautiful restoration of the murals on my family's tomb. I do so hope you enjoy yourself tonight.
富商：你可是替我们家族重绘墓碑的大功臣。好好享受这个夜晚吧！

Añil: I'm sure I will. Thank you for your kind invitation, ma'am.
野木蓝：感谢您的盛情邀请。我会的。

He walks toward the brilliantly decorated ofrenda, his grim-faced presence clashing with the joyful atmosphere.
他走入布置了家族祭坛的庭院中，与四周欢庆的氛围格格不入。

Añil: This was a mistake, for her and for me.
野木蓝：也许这是一个错误的决定，无论对她还是对我来说。

Añil: Ms. Ramona is nowhere to be seen. Perhaps she isn't coming? No, she'll show her face. She's sure to recognize the significance of that list.
野木蓝：但拉莫纳小姐尚未现身……她会来吗？不，她肯定能读出名单的含义……

Añil: And Ms. Butterfly must be hidden somewhere nearby. Taken her seat for the drama, no doubt.
野木蓝：至于蝴蝶女士，此刻想必正躲藏在某个角落，愉快地欣赏着一切吧……

The place falls silent. A deep, official voice speaks over the microphone.
场地逐渐安静下来，一个深沉、带着十足官方口吻的男声透过麦克风响起。

Foreign Diplomat: Ahem, I would like to express my thanks to Mrs. Juárez for kindly hosting us tonight. Here in Mexico City, I have been able to witness and feel the beauty of your culture.
外交官：感谢华雷斯女士的邀请，能够在今日到现场亲身见证墨西哥本土亡灵节的绚丽与魅力，我深感荣幸。

Foreign Diplomat: The Day of the Dead celebration has shown me the extraordinary way in which the Mexican people remember, celebrate, and reunite with their departed loved ones, and I have truly been moved by it.
外交官：墨西哥城的景象让我看到墨西哥人对“死亡”另一重温暖的解释，它是值得庆祝的、与逝者再次欢聚的时刻。

Foreign Diplomat: Now, please join me in raising a toast. To all those passed and to all who yet live.
外交官：所以，在这一刻，我想举杯，致敬诸灵，致敬众生。

Foreign Diplomat: Let the party begin!
外交官：让今夜的庆典开始吧！

Guests laugh and toast, raising their glasses in a show of respect to the diplomat.
来宾在笑谈中举杯，对外交官表达敬意。

Yet as the attendees sip their drinks and cheer, someone approaches and whispers to the diplomat. The glasses have not even lowered before the two of them step away from view.
推杯换盏中，有人匆匆赶到外交官身边耳语了数句，外交官随即与对方一起离开了。

Añil: He seems on edge. Did something happen?
野木蓝：他的神色看起来有些紧张。出什么事了？

Añil: I should follow him.
野木蓝：我得跟上他——

But his motion is cut off by another arrival.
拉莫纳：忽然间，一位宾客挡住了他的去路。

Ramona: You didn't say you'd be here.
拉莫纳：我不记得你说过会来这里。

Añil: I couldn't help but worry about you, after I gave you that list.
野木蓝：把名单交给你之后，我一直在担忧我给你的信息会带来不好的后果。

Ramona: Hah. You can't even protect yourself. Why are you worrying about anyone else?
拉莫纳：你这样做有什么意义？你甚至没有能力保护自己，又为什么要担心别人？

Ramona: Get outta here before the killer makes a move.
拉莫纳：算了，这不重要。我建议你马上离开。

Ramona: Place is crowded. No one's gonna bother to save you when they strike.
拉莫纳：不该来这里的是你，如果凶手在人群里动手，没有人能够抽空照顾你的安危。

She shifts her eyes over to the diplomat as she speaks. He is standing in a quiet corner, speaking with someone who appears to be a younger aide.
她一边说着，目光依旧紧随外交官的身影。对方走到角落处，正神色严肃地听着手下的汇报。

Foreign Diplomat: What? How could that be?
外交官：什么？！怎么会这样！

Foreign Diplomat: Enough of your excuses! In any case, we mustn't let Mrs. Juárez get wind of this.
外交官：别辩解，你究竟怎么办事的！绝对不能让华雷斯女士发现这件事——

The diplomat scolds him, and they begin to argue. The aide shifts nervously and then shoots his hand into his pocket.
在外交官的斥责下，二人陷入了争执，那名下属将手伸进了衣服的口袋中。

Ramona: Oh, no!
拉莫纳：不好！

Ramona: He's got a weapon. We gotta stop him.
拉莫纳：他好像揣着什么凶器——他要动手了！

The thought-to-be assailant falls to the ground. He lifts his hand from his pocket, revealing nothing more than festive candy likely taken from an ofrenda far too soon.
“凶手”倒地，手从衣服口袋中脱出，里面揣着的只是一把从祭坛上偷来的糖果。

Foreign Diplomat: What on earth is wrong with you? Think you could go around roughing up my men?
外交官：你们在干什么？！为什么要袭击我的手下？！

Foreign Diplomat: Who are you, some local gangsters?
外交官：难道你们是黑帮的人？！

Ramona: Sir, listen, you're in serious danger. He was gonna assassinate you.
拉莫纳：先生，我们怀疑他对你有袭击的意图——

Foreign Diplomat: What nonsense are you spouting? He was just delivering a report.
外交官：你在说什么？！他刚刚只是想向我汇报情况！

Foreign Diplomat: It's the painting I gave Mrs. Juárez. Apparently the color has faded in one spot. My assistant was only asking me to check it over.
外交官：我赠送给华雷斯女士的画作出现了一些异常，我的助理不过是提醒我去检查一番而已！

Ramona: A painting from the Turquoise Serpent Club? Where is it? You need to give it to me, now.
拉莫纳：你说的是，那幅来自绿松石蛇俱乐部的画作吗？它在哪里，马上把它交给我！

Ramona: If you want to live, you need to stay away from that painting.
拉莫纳：如果你还想要命的话，就离它远一些。

The diplomat's mood shifts. He seems frightened, certainly, but not of the painting she warns him about.
闻言，外交官却越发恐慌起来。

Foreign Diplomat: You're from one of those xenophobic arcanist groups, aren't you? Mrs. Juárez warned me about people like you.
外交官：你们是破坏庆典的黑帮吗？华雷斯女士和我说过，本地不乏一些反对外国人的极端神秘学家组织。

Foreign Diplomat: What are you planning to do, huh? Kill me in front of all these people?
外交官：你们想在人这么多的地方动手吗？！

Añil: No, no, sir. Listen to me. I'm trying to help you.
野木蓝：等等，先生，我们并无恶意——

Foreign Diplomat: Help! Intruders! Hel—argh ...
外交官：救命，快来人啊！！呃……啊……

His words stop. A painful rasp replaces them. He grabs his throat, struggling for air.
他的话戛然而止，取而代之的是痛苦的嘶喊。他用手掐住自己的脖子，试图找回正常的呼吸。

They have moved without noticing to the part of the courtyard where gifts are displayed.
原来在不知不觉中，他们已经走到庭院里展示礼物的角落。

Añil: What on earth ...? Sir, are you all right?
野木蓝：发生了什么……？先生，您还好吗？

Ramona: Don't get too close. That arcane power could drown you along with him.
拉莫纳：该死，别碰他！！那个致人死亡的神秘学仪式会把你也卷进去的，野木蓝！

Foreign Diplomat: Argggh. Wh-Where am I? Is that a lake beneath the water?
外交官：呃……这是什么地方……怎么会有水里的湖……

Foreign Diplomat: H-Help ...
外交官：救……救我……

The diplomat's voice falls into a rasp, then silence. He crumples into a heap on the ground.
数分钟前还在衣冠楚楚地发言的外交官徒劳地向前伸手。最终，他的身体无力地倒下，发出一声闷响。

Añil: This is just like ...
野木蓝：拉莫纳小姐，这……

Ramona: Like what happened at the auction.
拉莫纳：和拍卖会完全一致的情况。

Ramona: The killer can't be far. Must still be hiding somewhere near the ofrenda.
拉莫纳：凶手不可能离得太远，必定仍然藏身于祭坛附近……

Ramona hears a rustle in the grass behind her. She turns with just enough time to spy a figure darting away.
女孩敏锐地捕捉到身后草丛细微的窸窣声，她猛地转过头去，看见一道人影闪过。

Ramona: There! Get 'em!
拉莫纳：在那里！快追上！

The city street is crowded with people celebrating. The raucous mood and shifting mass of people obscure sight in every direction.
一门之隔，外头竟然是熙熙攘攘的街道，来往的行人皆面色如常，无从辨认其身份。

They have lost sight of their culprit.
他们彻底失去了凶手的踪迹。

Ramona: *pant* Damn it.
拉莫纳：啊……该死。

Añil: *pant* We lost them.
野木蓝：我们跟丢了。

Añil: This is a wealthy area. The mansions on this street must have guards at their entrances. We can ask them if they saw anyone suspicious.
野木蓝：这里是罗马区的要道，宅邸都必定有佣人看守。我们可以询问附近的门房，刚刚是否有见到什么人出入。

Ramona: No time for that.
拉莫纳：不，这样太费时间了。

Ramona: The paintings are the key to the killer's m.o., but what else am I missing?
拉莫纳：我已经知道凶手犯案的关键在于画作，但一定有什么线索是我所遗漏的。

Ramona: The diplomat already had the painting for a while before the party.
拉莫纳：在参加庆典之前，受害的外交官先生已经与画作独处了许久。

Ramona: That journalist must have given it to him a few days ago.
拉莫纳：他在数日之前就已经从那名记者手上得到了这幅致命的画作。

Ramona: So, why'd the killer wait so long, and why strike in front of so many people? Why do it on the night of one of our biggest festivals?
拉莫纳：今日是整个墨西哥最热闹的节日之一，也是最危险的时刻。为什么凶手偏偏要在今天，在众目睽睽之下动手？

Ramona: That's gotta be it. It's all about the timing.
拉莫纳：这证明，对于凶手而言，时机也是非常重要的一环。

Añil: Timing, you say? So, it has something to do with All Souls' Day.
野木蓝：时机么……今夜是亡灵节的第二日，诸灵之夜。

Ramona: That's right, the second night of Day of the Dead. The night we welcome back and reunite with the faithful departed.
拉莫纳：在今夜，我们得以迎接逝去的成年亲属的回归。

Ramona: Death is just a part of the cycle of life, right? I'd bet the killer thinks so too.
拉莫纳：人们虔诚地相信着，死亡是生命循环的一部分。而对于凶手而言，亦是如此。

Ramona: And they chose tonight because they're trying to lead the spirits of the victims somewhere? Someplace they aren't meant to be?
拉莫纳：凶手究竟是想让这些回归的灵魂，去往何处？如果并非前往来世的乐土——

She looks down. Countless marigold petals spread out beneath her feet, forming a path to guide the dead. It stretches far into the distance.
她凝视着面前的街道。
无尽的万寿菊以脚下为起点，铺就成指引亡灵的道路，延伸向远方。

Ramona: Huh. That may be the answer. See, someone's laid a trail of marigolds out the back door.
拉莫纳：真是离奇。有人在后门这儿铺了一条万寿菊的小径。

Añil: The vivid glow of marigolds is what's said to guide spirits back to the land of the living.
野木蓝：万寿菊有着如同太阳一般的光芒与温暖，它指引着灵魂的路途。

Ramona: They want us to follow this path, huh? Fine, then let's go.
拉莫纳：引路的作用么……我们顺着这条花瓣小径走。

A quiet, stately building marks the end of the marigold trail.
一栋寂静的建筑矗立在亡灵小径的尽头。

Ramona: You're kidding.
拉莫纳：……这不可能。

Ramona: Of all the places ...
拉莫纳：怎么会是……这里。

41  汇聚点 · 1/2 / Convergence · 1/2

White smoke from burning copal fills the air, mixed with the scent of old books.
柯巴脂燃烧的白烟弥漫，混杂着属于书本的油墨味。

Ramona: The Mexico City People's Library.
拉莫纳：索卡洛广场的墨西哥城人民图书馆。

Ramona: Home to the Vigiles Bureau's temporary branch.
拉莫纳：而如今，是夜巡特遣管理局临时驻外分部。

Ramona: Why here?
拉莫纳：怎么会是夜巡特遣管理局……？

She lights a candle. The small flame does little to brighten the vast darkness of the room before her.
她点燃了一支蜡烛，微弱的光芒照亮了面前一小片区域。

Corridor, Temporary Branch
临时驻外机构·走廊

Ramona: Wait, Añil.
拉莫纳：你留在这里，野木蓝。

Ramona: I'm not sure what might lie ahead. You better stay back.
拉莫纳：我不确定前面会遇到什么危险，也不能保证在关键的时刻留意你的安全。

Añil: Understood. I wouldn't want to be a liability.
野木蓝：我知晓，强行跟随只会拖你的后腿。

Añil: I'll await a certain young woman here and do my best to ensure she doesn't interfere with your actions.
野木蓝：我会在这里，迎接一位在阴影中伺机而动的女士，不让她影响到您的行动。

Añil: Be careful, Ms. Ramona. Your safety is more important than anything.
野木蓝：万事小心，拉莫纳小姐。您的安危比一切都重要。

In total silence, she walks through the familiar corridors, made unfamiliar in the dim light and strange circumstances.
一片寂静之中，她走进了这栋每日必须造访的建筑。

She reminds herself that it is still that same place she's entered a hundred times before. The bookshelves stand in their usual order. The lobby floor shines. The only difference is that no one is here.
熟悉的书架排布，光洁的大厅地板——与平时的区别只在于，此刻，这里空无一人。

Ramona: They must've dispatched some top officers to the club after they got my report.
拉莫纳：按常理来说，分部在收到我的汇报后，就已经派出精锐执夜人前往俱乐部的拍卖会现场调查。

Ramona: Others will be out on patrol, making sure the festival doesn't have any arcanum-related accidents.
拉莫纳：有一些执夜人则留在城市中心，负责预防节庆会发生的神秘学案件。

Ramona: Still, I can't believe they would just leave the place empty. That makes no sense. There must be—
拉莫纳：现在虽然是分部人最少的时刻，但必定会有人留在这里——

Her words fade into the still air.
她的话顿住了。

Around the corner, the receptionist lies passed out on the floor. Even unconscious, her face is tight with fear, as if trapped in a nightmare.
转角处，接待员人事不省地躺在地板上。即使处于昏睡之中，她仍然面色惊恐，仿佛陷入了最为痛苦的梦魇。

Receptionist: Sea? No. Help ...
接待员：我……海水……我不要……

Ramona: Still breathing. Trapped in a living nightmare or something.
拉莫纳：她仍有呼吸，只是无法从噩梦中苏醒。

Ramona: A whole lotta pneuma gathered here.
拉莫纳：“灵”的流向都汇聚在此处。

Ramona: Someone's activated a ritual, a big one. Power of it's knocked out anyone in the area.
拉莫纳：某种至终的仪式启动了，外泄的神秘术波动使留在这里的人都陷入了昏睡。

Ramona: Who the hell'd be able to set up a large-scale arcane ritual like this in a Vigiles Bureau branch? And how?
拉莫纳：想在专门负责神秘学案件的夜巡特遣管理局，提早秘密布置如此复杂的大范围神秘术，可不是一件容易的事情。

Ramona lifts the candle and looks up toward the stairs.
女孩举起蜡烛，望向楼梯上的高处。

Ramona: That mural, the one with the missing artist, but now it's finished.
拉莫纳：那幅因艺术家失踪而搁置的大型壁画……它完成了。

How many times has she seen that mural? Those faces, the familiar scene, vivid yet silent, unfinished for as long as she had worked with Vigiles here.
她看向壁画，每一天，她路过此处，与壁画擦身而过。它是如此鲜明，又如此沉默，已经成为所有人认知中的背景板。

She had never truly committed the faces of those young people in the mural to memory, but now they almost seem familiar.
日复一日，壁画上青年们的面庞逐渐印刻在她的脑海中。

Ramona: Could this be the killer's final piece? And I ...
拉莫纳：这就是凶手最后的作品。而……

She takes a deep breath.
她深吸一口气。

Ramona: I followed their marigold trail here. Am I their final target?
拉莫纳：来到此处的我，就是凶手的最后一个目标。

Muriel: You've finally come. Teresita, my dear friend!
穆列尔：你终于来了，特蕾莎。如我所期待的。

Ramona: Teresita?
拉莫纳：特蕾莎……？

Ramona: You think I'm a friend of yours? Is that why you chose me?
拉莫纳：你认为我是你的某位友人，这就是你将我选作目标的原因。

Muriel: You've truly forgotten everything. That's quite alright. I'll help you remember, just as you once helped me.
穆列尔：你果然不记得了，但没有关系……我会让你回忆起所有的。就像你当年教导一无所知的我一样。

Muriel: This mural is my first and final piece of the era.
穆列尔：这幅大型壁画，是我在这个时代绘制的第一幅作品，也是最后一幅。

Muriel: A product of blood, sweat, and tears. A tribute to the Mexican mythos you held so dear, one that honors both the sacred traditions of our ancestors and our courageous struggles in the modern world.
穆列尔：我绘制了你向我描述过的，你心中的墨西哥神话——一个根植于古老神圣的传统，与现代人作的英勇斗争所结合的神话，属于墨西哥人的神话。

Muriel: You see, no matter how strange this world becomes, I will never abandon our shared dream.
穆列尔：你瞧……无论如何，我都绝不会丢下我们的理想。

Muriel: I incorporated each of us into the painting, just as I've done many times before. Think of it as a celebration of our rebirth.
穆列尔：如往常无数次那般，我将我们的形象融入了画中。这同样是为了庆贺我们的新生而创作的壁画作品……

Muriel: I've waited a long time for this reunion.
穆列尔：为此，我已经等待了四年，我的朋友。

Muriel: Four long years, crossing several "Storms" under Manus Vindictae's protection, I chased all sorts of myths and legends, all to find a way to resurrect the dead, and I've finally found it.
穆列尔：在这四年间，我在重塑之手的庇护下跨越了数次“暴雨”，只为追寻那些隐藏在神话背后的真相。我已经掌握了死而复生的方式。

Muriel: The Aztecs believed that the manner of one's death determines the soul's reincarnation.
穆列尔：阿兹特克人相信，死亡的方式可以决定灵魂的来世。

Muriel: And so, tonight, the souls of our warriors will be reborn like butterflies emerging from their cocoons, carried on sacred winds from the north.
穆列尔：勇士的灵魂，在亡灵节的今夜，我会为你举行献祭的仪式，你将如蝴蝶般蜕变，重获新生，从北方归来——

Muriel: This is our reunion, one not even death can keep us from.
穆列尔：最终，我们将跨越死亡而重聚。

42  必然一步 / The Necessary Step

Murals and Turning Points
壁画与转折

This is a land where traditions are told through murals. After the revolution, the young government turned to its artists. This newborn Mexico had so many stories to tell, so many to shape—and paintings adorning the walls of its buildings became the perfect medium. In the 1920s, the muralist movement swept through the country.

Look, this was an art form both ancient and brand new. It no longer lived in auction houses, galleries, or the private collections of the wealthy. Instead, it unfolded across vast walls open to the public. On those walls, artists spoke of justice, of freedom, of this nation's history and memory. Imagine it! A new country, a new canvas, and so many stories to tell! From the very beginning, these murals were inseparable from politics. The brush was the artist's voice. They spoke for their ideals, for beauty, for all the suffering people of the world ...

But voices are difficult to harmonize. And they can turn harsh.

As the 20th century entered its third decade, cracks began to appear—between artist and artist, between artist and state. Should the murals depict suffering or glory? Whose praises should they sing? Which path forward? Rivera, Orozco, and Siqueiros—three names that once symbolized hope, our "Los Tres Grandes"—held different visions for the future of both their art and their country.

The rifts only deepened. In the small "Midnight Kitchen," young painters listened to this "fight of the century."
这片土地上生长着以壁画诉说的传统。在革命之后，年轻的政府向艺术家们伸出了橄榄枝。新生的墨西哥有太多故事需要表达，需要塑造，公共建筑上的壁画是绝好的载体。1920年代，一场名为墨西哥壁画运动的浪潮席卷了此处。

瞧，这是一种传统而崭新的艺术。它不再待在拍卖会、画廊或富人的收藏室，它在巨大的、向民众开放的墙面上铺展开来。艺术家在墙壁上讲述着——关于何为正义，何为自由，何为这个国家的历史与记忆。请想象吧！一个新的国家，一片新的画布，许多、许多新的故事！从诞生之日起，这些新壁画就是同政治息息相关的。画笔是画家们的喉咙，他们为理想发声，为美发声，为全世界受苦的人们发声……

声音，声音是难以统一的，是会变得刺耳的。

二十世纪进入了第三个十年，画家与画家、画家与国家的声音逐渐出现了参差。该描绘苦难还是荣光？该歌颂谁？该选择哪一条道路？里维拉、奥罗斯科和西凯罗斯——三个象征希望的名字，我们的“墨西哥壁画三杰”——对国家与绘画的前途有着不同的见解。

纷争越来越大。在小小的“午夜厨房”里，年轻的画家们亦听到了这场“世纪之战”。

43  汇聚点 · 2/2 / Convergence · 2/2

A dripping sound breaks the quiet, but its source remains hidden.
不知从何处传来的滴水声，回荡在空无的寂静里。

The sense of space fades away as her mind sinks into darkness.
空间仿佛不存在了，意识也溶解在黑暗之中。

Muriel: Sleep tight, Teresita. Fall into the dream, our irreplaceable memory of the past.
穆列尔：晚安，特蕾莎。这会是一场回顾往昔的好梦。

Muriel: When you awake, everything will have returned to how it was, to the beauty of our glory days.
穆列尔：等你醒来后，一切都会变成最初，也是最美好的模样。

Ramona: Teresita? Who are you talking about?
拉莫纳：特蕾莎……究竟是谁……？

Endless water stretches out before her, a lake as wide as Texcoco once had been.
她看见了无边的水域。

A cold color guides her awareness as it moves through the Depths of Myth.
一抹冰冷的色彩指引着她的意识在潜意识之海中逡巡。

Her long journey ends when she finds her destination and slips into a fragile container made of fragments of memory.
漫长的旅途结束，她终于寻觅到自己的目的地，钻进了那座由记忆碎片构成的容器里。

???: I am ...
？？？：我是……

Teresita: I am Teresita.
特蕾莎：我是特蕾莎。

This memory container, as thin as a paper boat, floats to a stop on the water.
记忆容器如同脆弱的船只，缓缓停泊在水域中。

Around her, a rough memory forms on a stage. Happiness hangs like fog, showing how its owner once felt.
舞台之上，四周勉强搭建出记忆的场景。此处充盈着喜悦，如同薄雾笼罩，昭示着记忆主人的情绪。

Muriel: Teresita? Teresita!
穆列尔：特蕾莎——特蕾莎！

Teresita: Pardon me. My mind was elsewhere. You are ...?
特蕾莎：抱歉，刚刚我有些走神了。你是……？

Muriel: My name is Muriel.
穆列尔：我叫穆列尔。

Muriel: María asked me to welcome you on her behalf. From this day forth, you're officially a fellow member of the Midnight Kitchen.
穆列尔：欢迎来到玛利亚举办的艺术家集会！从今往后，你就是“午夜厨房”的一员了。

Teresita: Heh. You're María's new student? Oh, sorry, I don't mean to be rude.
特蕾莎：是玛利亚让你过来的吗？请见谅，我没有别的意思。

Teresita: I'm just surprised you're so young.
特蕾莎：只是，作为玛利亚的徒弟来说，你看起来太年轻了。你只是个孩子。

Muriel: No need to apologize. You're not the first to say so. But I've been painting for as long as I can remember.
穆列尔：没关系，很多人都这么说过。我从记事起就开始学习绘画了。

Teresita: The Midnight Kitchen. An interesting name. An homage to Montmartre's "Laundry Ship," is it?
特蕾莎：“午夜厨房”是什么意思……类似于蒙玛尔特的“流动洗衣房”吗？

Muriel: Hahahaha, no, nothing like that. We first started gathering in María's kitchen late at night, so Antonio suggested we name our little club after it.
穆列尔：哈哈，一开始的时候，咱们的艺术研讨会都是在晚上偷偷进行的，地点就在玛利亚家的厨房里。安东尼奥觉得这个名字非常有纪念意义。

Muriel: María founded it in hopes of creating a space for like-minded artists to have open and meaningful discussions. She's been planning to start a magazine on Mexico's artistic transformation.
穆列尔：玛利亚正在筹划创办一本关于墨西哥艺术变革的杂志，她希望志同道合的艺术家们能够有一个共同研讨的平台。

Muriel: And she said you have some wonderful ideas and insights on the art of indigenous peoples. She said you could teach us all about it while she's away on her exhibition tour in the United States.
穆列尔：听说你一直在做原住民相关的调研。玛利亚说你有非常棒的想法，当她前往美国参与巡展时，她说你可以代替她给我们授课。

Teresita: Well, I, I'll do my best. Though, I have to say, teaching has never been my strong suit. Don't set your expectations too high.
特蕾莎：我……尽量吧。不过我并不是一个好老师，也不确保能教给你什么。

Teresita: Besides, I'll need to look over your paintings first.
特蕾莎：这一切都得等我看过你的画作才能做决定。

Muriel: Trust me. You won't be disappointed.
穆列尔：请放心，我不会让你失望的。

The atmosphere thickens and darkens. Only uncertain, worry, and conflict remain.
四周的情绪变得沉重粘稠起来，只能感受到疑惑、忧虑与矛盾。

Teresita: Ah. You're a genius painter, Muriel. Your work will cause a stir all over the world.
特蕾莎：你是个绘画上的天才，穆列尔，你的作品将来一定会引起轰动的。

Muriel: You're too kind, but as much as I appreciate such praise, you know fame has never been my goal.
穆列尔：老师，你知道的，这一直都不是我的追求。

Muriel: And I wouldn't accept the title of genius either. I simply love to paint, to create alongside my dear friends.
穆列尔：而我也并非什么天才。我只是喜欢绘画，希望能够和大家一起创作而已。

Teresita: Your goals are admirable in themselves. Though more than genius, your greatest gift lies in your unending passion.
特蕾莎：是啊……我喜欢这个说法。最大的天赋就是永不间断的喜爱。

Teresita: Still, I can't help but wonder if this movement is on the right track.
特蕾莎：我只是在担忧，我们的运动是否行进在正确的道路上。

Muriel: Hm? Is this about that article in New Masses?
穆列尔：老师，你也看了《新大众》的那篇文章吗？

Teresita: Yes. We once saw our movement as revolutionary, a force to change our society for the better, but, as Siqueiros said, it seems it may have already fallen under the thumb of the petite bourgeoisie.
特蕾莎：是的，曾经我们都坚信这是一场颠覆性的运动。但如果真如同西凯罗斯所说，它已经被小资产阶级掌控……

Teresita: We wanted to reconceptualize our identity, history, and culture as a people. We believed that only through common understanding could we ever truly be united.
特蕾莎：我们迫切想要知道，我们自己是谁，我们的历史与文化是什么。只要能够构建共同的理解，我们就能把所有人团结起来。

Teresita: We wanted to create a unique story. A story of Mexico.
特蕾莎：我们想要创作一个纯粹的、只属于墨西哥的故事。

Teresita: But have we succeeded in our goal?
特蕾莎：但我们真正做到了吗？

Muriel: Teresita ...
穆列尔：老师……

Teresita: I'm having second thoughts. Painting these murals on the walls of grand buildings—perhaps it's been a mistake all along.
特蕾莎：我无法确定。也许追求技术上的革新，在宏伟的建筑物里头创作壁画，从头到尾就是一个错误。

The world around her shifts like a maelstrom. The memory flickers in and out.
记忆变得狂乱起来。

Words and feelings slash her like swirling debris caught in the storm.
无序的语言与情绪，像是被狂风裹挟的刀片，划过意识。

Teresita: Why did you turn down Lucas's invitation, Muriel?
特蕾莎：穆列尔，为什么你要拒绝卢卡斯的邀请？

Teresita: Paris is the center of the art world. You know Rivera went to Europe to study around your age, don't you? An opportunity like this doesn't come all that often.
特蕾莎：能去欧洲的机会并不是天天都能有的。巴黎是世界艺术的中心，里维拉也是差不多在你这个年纪前往欧洲游学。

Muriel: I don't want to leave Mexico, Teresita. I can't.
穆列尔：但我不想离开墨西哥，老师。

Muriel: I want to stay here, side by side with my friends.
穆列尔：我……我不想离开这里，不想离开我的伙伴们。

Muriel: You saw the debate at the Palace of Fine Arts. We're at a critical turning point for the muralist movement.
穆列尔：特蕾莎，那场发生在美术宫的辩论，你不是也在现场么……你应该知晓，现在正是壁画运动转折的关键点！

Teresita: Which is precisely why I want you to refine your skills in Europe, Muriel. It would do you good to free yourself from this mental turmoil.
特蕾莎：正因如此，穆列尔。我需要你前往巴黎和巴塞罗那的艺术家协会，精进自己的画技，从现在的思维和漩涡里挣脱开来。

Teresita: Art can change the world. I still believe that. But there are many obstacles we need to overcome ...
特蕾莎：我们的使命在于将艺术与政治结合起来，才能实现真正的改变。

Teresita: A challenge that has kept María very busy, and for which the progress has hardly been satisfactory. *sigh* I shouldn't distract you with all this.
特蕾莎：玛利亚正在为此事奔波，但结果……唉，我不该和你说这些。

Muriel: I'll go, if that's what you wish, Teresita.
穆列尔：我明白了，老师。

Teresita: Then do it. Make our dream come true, kiddo. The future depends on you.
特蕾莎：去未来完成我们的理想吧，孩子。

Teresita: Future ...
特蕾莎：未来。

Teresita: Dream, whose dream? Mine?
特蕾莎：这是属于我的愿望吗，我的理想吗？

Teresita: It sounds inspiring, but ...
特蕾莎：这真的很美好，也让人向往。但……

Her thoughts drift out of her body, caught helpless in the tide of a deep and roiling sea.
女孩的思绪在无尽的记忆碎片中沉浮。

She reaches out and stirs the emotions before her. Joy and happiness fade at her touch like mist.
她伸手拨弄面前的情绪——就像一阵轻薄的雾气，那些喜悦和快乐很快就消散了。

Teresita: Why do I feel no joy at all?
特蕾莎：为什么，我的心中感受不到一丝喜悦？

Teresita: There's nothing but sorrow here.
特蕾莎：就像是这里只有无尽的悲伤一样。

She whispers the words, too difficult to speak aloud.
她困难地呢喃着。

Teresita: There's something—something long forgotten. I've got to find it.
特蕾莎：我得找到……某样我忘记了的东西。

44  世上无永恒 / Nothing Is Everlasting

Is it real, our elusive existence?
               No eternity on earth, only a fleeting moment.
               Be it as resilient as jade, it shatters;
               Be it as revered as gold, it crumbles;
               Be it as resplendent as quetzal plumes, it will fall away.
               No eternity on earth, only a fleeting moment.

                                             —Cantares mexicanos
是否在世上，我们真正存在过？
               世上无真正的永恒，只有短暂的一瞬。
               纵是玉石，也有破碎的一日；
               纵是金子，也会被无情碾碎；
               纵是翠羽，也容易腐坏难存。
               世上无真正的永恒，只有短暂的一瞬。

                                             ——《墨西哥圣歌集》

Guided by instinct, she moves forward, step by step.
凭着本能，她一步一步地向前走去。

Muriel: Finally, above this endless churning water, she sees a figure standing still as a statue, pallid as a ghost.
穆列尔：终于，在这无边的水域中，她看见了一道人影。他静静地站着，如同幽灵一般。

Muriel: It's been a long time, Teresita.
穆列尔：好久不见，特蕾莎。

Muriel: María, Antonio, Carmen, and I ... We've all missed you very much.
穆列尔：我、玛利亚、安东尼奥、卡门……我们都非常想念你。

Muriel: Welcome back.
穆列尔：欢迎回来。

Teresita: Muriel! Ah, when did you return from Europe?
特蕾莎：穆列尔！你是什么时候从欧洲回来的！

Teresita: How many years have passed since then? I can't seem to remember at all.
特蕾莎：已经过去多久了……？真奇怪，我一点都记不起来。

Teresita: Well, please, tell me all about your time in Paris.
特蕾莎：有空的话，和我详细讲讲你在巴黎的见闻吧。

He wears a strange, sorrowful expression.
少年的面容呈现出一种古怪的忧伤。

Muriel: Where do I begin? A lot has happened over the past four years.
穆列尔：四年了。这四年间，发生了很多事情。

Muriel: I encountered an arcanist organization called Manus Vindictae. With their help, I survived an event called the "Storm." Many times, in fact. But by the time I returned home, I realized everyone I knew had perished.
穆列尔：我接触过一个名叫重塑之手的神秘学家组织，也越过了数次“暴雨”……但等我回到墨西哥的时候，已经不是同样的年代，大家都消失在了“暴雨”之中。

Muriel: What I returned to was no longer the Mexico I knew.
穆列尔：一切都不一样了。

Teresita: Oh, my dear Muriel. I'm so sorry to hear it.
特蕾莎：这听起来太让人伤心了。我很抱歉，穆列尔。

Teresita: Perhaps María and I shouldn't have sent you to Europe.
特蕾莎：也许当初我和玛利亚强行把你送去欧洲是个错误的决定。

Muriel: But I learned so much there.
穆列尔：虽然有些曲折，但我也有不少收获。

Muriel: While I was in Paris, I learned from Professor Roseau's research. His efforts to find a resurrection ritual led us to discover Depths of Myth, the sea of humanity's collective unconscious.
穆列尔：在巴黎的时候，重塑之手的罗西教授正在进行有关“起死回生”的研究。我们发现了人类集体潜意识之海——迷思海的存在。

Muriel: His failure proved that a successful resurrection requires the alignment of multiple nearly impossible conditions, but at least the professor's research pointed me in the right direction.
穆列尔：虽然后续证明，“起死回生”有着相当苛刻的条件，但罗西教授基于荣格学说的研究给予了我非常大的启发。

Muriel: Do you recall the Temazcal ceremony we once talked about?
穆列尔：你还记得你曾经和我提过的特马斯克[Temazcal]吗？

Teresita: The cleansing ritual?
特蕾莎：那个原住民的净化仪式？

Muriel: That's the one. Drawing from the Temazcal ceremony, Professor Roseau's research, and countless other texts, I have finally created the ultimate ritual of resurrection.
穆列尔：是的，我翻阅了无数神话与历史的典籍，以特马斯克为灵感，终于研究出了全新的起死回生术！

Muriel: Using special herbs and arcane skills as a catalyst, we may transfer our existence into a painting, where we'll be enveloped by absolute darkness.
穆列尔：使用特殊的草药引导，通过神秘术，我们得以进入画作，处于一个完全黑暗的环境之中。

Muriel: And within that primal void, we can regain our purest consciousness and rediscover who we truly are, who we were meant to be.
穆列尔：而在这原始的黑暗里，我们得以寻找到最原始的、非理性的思想，重新发现我们自身的身份。

Muriel: The ritual requires a unique arcane silver ore, which I was fortunate to find here in this era's Vale of Tears.
穆列尔：经历了四次“暴雨”，我终于在这个年代中，找到了眼泪之谷里未被挖掘枯竭的秘银矿石。

Muriel: From it, I crafted a pigment of infinite malleability, one capable of recreating almost anything, even our memories and identities.
穆列尔：我用它制成了一种特殊的、具有无限延展性的颜料。它可以重绘许多事物，甚至包括我们的记忆与身份认知。

Muriel: The sea of collective unconscious is the deepest layer of the human mind, the origin of all things.
穆列尔：人类集体潜意识，是人格的最底层，也是一切的原型与起点。

Muriel: Rest assured, Teresita. I will reconstruct you completely, body and mind, from the Depths of Myth.
穆列尔：在我们所身处的这片水域，迷思之海中，我会为你重新构建属于特蕾莎的一切。

Teresita: I'm not sure I entirely follow. But I see that you are every bit the genius I always knew you were, Muriel.
特蕾莎：我不确定我听明白没有。但你确实是神秘学的天才，穆列尔。

Teresita: Who else would connect resurrection rituals to an ancient sweat lodge ceremony?
特蕾莎：天啊，你是怎么从一个简单的桑拿仪式上联想到这些的？

Muriel: I've tried everything over the years, but I finally found a method that works, Teresita.
穆列尔：四年了……我尝试了所有我知晓的方式。这是我仅剩的道路了，特蕾莎。

Muriel: I'm so thankful that I've found you again.
穆列尔：幸好现在我找回了你。

Teresita: I'm happy you have, too, from the bottom of my heart. But I almost feel as if I've forgotten something important.
特蕾莎：我由衷地为你感到高兴，只是……我仍然感觉我忘记了什么东西。

The roiling water slows into soft and silent ripples.
沉默如同水域中泛起的涟漪。

Muriel: Now is the year of 1920, the start of a decade you and María once longed for.
穆列尔：1920年，这是你和玛利亚曾经梦寐以求的年代。

Muriel: The muralist movement has just begun. I'll ensure we won't make the same mistakes we did last time.
穆列尔：这时壁画复兴运动才刚刚开始，这次我们一定不会走向错误的路途了。

Teresita: Haha, it isn't like you to care about anything beyond your paintings.
特蕾莎：哈哈，我怎么记得你以前是完全不在乎这些东西的，只一心想着画画。

Muriel: People change.
穆列尔：……人是会变的。

Muriel: It was only after I left you and the others that I realized how lucky I was to live a life completely dedicated to my art.
穆列尔：自从离开大家之后，我才发现，能够让画画成为生命中的全部是多么幸运的一件事。

Teresita: But we have changed for the better, Muriel. You've grown up. Can take care of yourself now. You no longer need María or me to protect you.
特蕾莎：我很欣慰，穆列尔。你的生活不再真空了，也不需要我和玛利亚保护你了。

Teresita: Knowing that, I can continue my search for what I've forgotten.
特蕾莎：这很好。我可以继续去寻找我所遗忘的事物了。

Muriel: Is it really so important? More important than our dream? Than María, Antonio, all our friends of the Midnight Kitchen?
穆列尔：这件事物很重要吗？比我们的梦想，比玛利亚、安东尼奥，比“午夜厨房”的大家还重要吗……？

Teresita: It is. It's only a haze to me, but deep down I know it is more important than anything.
特蕾莎：是的。虽然我不记得了，但我感觉那是我最重要的东西。

Teresita: I'm sorry, Muriel. Until I find it, I can't pursue our dream with you.
特蕾莎：穆列尔，在我找到那样东西之前，我很抱歉我不能和你继续实现我们的梦想了。

Though confused, she keeps walking forward.
她有些疑惑，但仍然坚定地向前走去。

Muriel: You mustn't go any farther, Teresita.
穆列尔：停下脚步吧，特蕾莎。

Muriel: The deeper you go, the weaker my ritual becomes. Your mind's essence might be completely erased from the Depths of Myth.
穆列尔：你不能再往前走了。越深入迷思海，我所构建的仪式就会越脆弱，你的意识有可能会彻底地消融在迷思海之中。

Muriel: Are you truly certain you want to leave, knowing your very existence could be wiped out?
穆列尔：哪怕你会彻底地消融，不复存在，你也要继续往前走吗？

She doesn't stop.
女孩的脚步并未停下。

False memories in vivid color begin to peel from her skin like new paint stripped from a canvas.
在她身上，那些由颜料绘制的虚假记忆，如同灰烬般层层剥落。

Their joy, their dreams, the time they shared—layer by layer, they fall into the water below.
那些愉悦，那些昔日的梦想，那些共同度过的时光……它们在水域中安静地燃烧，最终消失不见。

Teresita: It's so beautiful.
特蕾莎：这……真漂亮。

Ramona: Those memories, they shine, glitter like crystal.
拉莫纳：这些回忆，它们就像水晶一样闪闪发亮。

Ramona: Thank you for showing them to me, Muriel.
拉莫纳：我要谢谢你，让我能够看见这样奇异的光景。

Ramona: But you have to understand: nothing lasts forever. Death is part of the cycle of life, just like any other stage.
拉莫纳：我很抱歉，穆列尔，但这个世上没有什么是永恒的，我们必须接受，死亡是生命的一部分。

Ramona: The future you dream about warmed my heart, it did, but it doesn't belong to me.
拉莫纳：你所描绘的未来确实非常动人，不过那不是属于我自身的命运。

Ramona: Just like those memories you forced into my mind.
拉莫纳：如同这些强加在我脑海中的记忆。

Ramona: Now, I need to find my true self. I need to find Ramona.
拉莫纳：而现在，我必须找回我自己，找回曾经发生在拉莫纳身上的一切。

Muriel: Please stop, Teresita.
穆列尔：特蕾莎，停下……

Muriel: Please don't do this to me. I can't lose you again.
穆列尔：别这样做……我不能眼睁睁地看着你再次消失。

His dim figure begins to glow.
他那黯淡的身影燃烧起来。

Memories cling to him like shards of sharpened glass until he takes on the form of a shining broken god.
记忆的碎屑旋转着，附着在他的身上，最终让他成为一个辉煌的、破败的神明。

Lorentz Butterfly: The spirits followed the marigolds all the way from the mansion in Roma to the People's Library, a final touch to complete the mural. Quite a night.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：从罗马区的府邸至墨西哥城人民图书馆，所有的灵魂沿着万寿菊回归此处，终究让画作变得完整。真是一个漫长的夜晚啊。

Lorentz Butterfly: I've got you now.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：抓到你了。

Muriel: Ah!
穆列尔：啊……

Muriel looks up toward the finished mural.
少年安静地凝视着面前的巨幅壁画。

The paint has cracked alongside the plaster. At last, the trapped form inside returns to the world.
那些覆盖的颜料随着灰水泥碎裂，最终，被困的身影得以重返人间。

Muriel: Teresita, it's you.
穆列尔：特蕾莎……是你……！

Muriel: The ritual worked! Professor Roseau was right.
穆列尔：仪式成功了……罗西教授说的果然是对的……

Muriel: What are we to do next, Teresita?
穆列尔：老师，接下来我们该怎么做？

His eyes shine with the joy of a dream so long awaited, finally realized in the flesh.
他陷入了莫大的喜悦之中。

Standing there in front of him, Ramona takes a deep and weighty breath.
在他面前，少女有些艰难地深吸一口气。

Ramona: It's over, Muriel.
拉莫纳：一切都结束了，穆列尔。

Ramona: You're the serial killer. It was all you.
拉莫纳：你……就是这七起神秘学凶案的始作俑者。

Ramona: You used a special pigment to make paintings with arcane powers—your murder weapons—and passed them out to the victims through the club.
拉莫纳：你潜伏在绿松石蛇俱乐部，利用俱乐部的渠道，将使用特殊颜料绘制的画作，通过各种方式交给你所挑选的受害者。

Ramona: And then, you activated a ritual through the painting to kill your target.
拉莫纳：然后，再利用画作来启动仪式……

Ramona: You killed all seven of them, all to get the friends you lost to the "Storm" back.
拉莫纳：而你之所以这么做，是为了寻找在“暴雨”之中失散的友人。

She glances back to the cracked and battered mural behind her.
她看向那幅残缺不堪的壁画。

Ramona: Those people in the mural ... Your friends from the Midnight Kitchen, aren't they?
拉莫纳：这上面绘制的，都是你曾经在“午夜厨房”的朋友，对吗？

Ramona: María, Carmen, Antonio, Laura ...
拉莫纳：玛利亚、卡门、安东尼奥、劳拉……

She names each face that appeared in the shattered memories. Her eyes rest on the very center.
女孩一一指认那些在破碎的记忆中出现的面容，最终，她的视线在最中央的位置停了下来。

Ramona: And Teresita.
拉莫纳：还有特蕾莎。

Ramona: Who'd have ever thought those faces would match the victims almost perfectly?
拉莫纳：我竟然从来没有发现，这些壁画上的面孔，竟和连环案件的受害者一模一样。

Ramona: To think how often I walked through this hall and never made the connection.
拉莫纳：也对，哪怕每日都会路过这里，但谁又会刻意去这样想呢。

Muriel: They were my best friends.
穆列尔：是啊……他们都是我最好的朋友。

Muriel: My countrymen. My comrades.
穆列尔：我们是属于这片土地的子民，是最亲密、坚实的战友。

He lifts the flowerpot in his hands, eager.
他迫切地举起手上的花盆。

Muriel: Before I left Mexico, you gave me some dahlia seeds. Do you remember that?
穆列尔：特蕾莎，当年在我离开墨西哥前，你交给了我一些天竺牡丹的种子。

Muriel: You said we'd meet again when the dahlias were in full bloom.
穆列尔：你告诉我，只要我把它们都种出来，等这些花朵开花的那一天，我们就能够重新相见。

Muriel: So I carry the pot wherever I go. I care for them no matter the circumstances.
穆列尔：四年了，我每天都精心地、不惜一切代价地照顾它，无论去到哪儿，我都要把它带着。

Muriel: I'm so glad we finally met again, just as you promised.
穆列尔：你果然没有骗我，我们终究还是重逢了。

Ramona struggles to find a response. She studies his face, hoping to find something in it that might help her decide between pity and horror.
女孩并没有着急反驳他，反而陷入了沉思。

Ramona: In all seven murders, you didn't manage to pull off a single one of your resurrection rituals, did you?
拉莫纳：七起案件之中，你的起死回生仪式从来没有成功过，对吗？

Muriel: I don't understand what you're saying.
穆列尔：……特蕾莎，你在说什么？

Ramona: I saw you in the painting, Muriel, in that void.
拉莫纳：刚刚，在画作之中，在那片原始的黑暗里，我见到了你，穆列尔。

Ramona: A little younger, a hell of a lot saner than you are now, but you all the same.
拉莫纳：他比如今的你要显得年轻一些，也看起来要清醒许多。

Ramona: I'd been thinking, the fragmented memories I saw in the Depths of Myth, where are they from? Who do they belong to?
拉莫纳：我一直在想一个问题——既然你的仪式并未成功，那些我在集体潜意识之海中所经历的记忆碎片，它们是属于谁的？

Ramona: But seeing you now, I think I get it.
拉莫纳：如今，看到你的模样，我想我明白这个问题了。

Ramona: You didn't reconstruct Teresita's consciousness from the Depths of Myth, not one bit.
拉莫纳：你并非从集体潜意识之海中成功地塑造了属于“特蕾莎”的个人意识……

Ramona: Those memories and feelings were painted into the mural by you. They all came from you, Muriel.
拉莫纳：那些记忆，那些情绪，那些你封印在画作之中，用来构建仪式的东西……都是属于你的回忆，穆列尔。它们都是你生命中的一部分。

Ramona: That's why I could only see memories of you and Teresita together.
拉莫纳：所以我在其中，只能看见你和特蕾莎接触的片段。

Ramona: All the moments you shared with your friends, the memories you held so close that you couldn't just let the dead be dead. You've given them up, sealed their spirits inside your paintings.
拉莫纳：所有用于仪式的画，你都封印了与友人相关的记忆，封印了那些困住你的、挥之不去的幻影。

Ramona: You completed the rituals, but you destroyed yourself.
拉莫纳：你为了完成这个仪式，毁灭了你自己。

Muriel: When? H-How did I?
穆列尔：我……怎么可能？

Muriel: No, Teresita, I-I didn't.
穆列尔：特蕾莎，我没有……

Ramona: See? I'm not Teresita, and you still can't see it.
拉莫纳：就像如今，你也认不出来，站在你面前的，并非特蕾莎。

Ramona: Your dearest friend and mentor ... And yet you still mistake me for her, a complete stranger.
拉莫纳：她本该是你最亲近也最为熟悉的导师，你又怎么会将一个陌生人错认成她呢？

Ramona: The moment you finished this mural, you scrubbed every last trace of her clean from your mind. You aren't even yourself anymore.
拉莫纳：在完成这幅画作的同时，你就忘记了她的模样，忘记了与她有关的一切。你不再是你了。

Ramona: All those people killed, and for what? The delusions of a man in the middle of destroying himself?
拉莫纳：为了自己的幻梦，你只是在让无辜者牺牲性命。你只是在杀人。

Muriel speaks to himself, clutching his head as if in pain.
少年喃喃自语着，痛苦地抱着自己的头。

Muriel: You mean there's no way to save my friends? I killed them with my own hands.
穆列尔：你是说，他们早就不在了是吗？而我为此夺走无辜之人的性命？

Muriel: I ... No, Teresita.
穆列尔：我……不对……特蕾莎……

Muriel: Why can't I remember your face? Your voice?
穆列尔：为什么我什么都想不起来？为什么我想不起来你的脸，你的声音，你曾经对我说过的话……

Muriel: The memories ... Why won't they come to me? My most cherished friends, my family ...
穆列尔：我怎么会忘记这一切……？他们都是我最重要的家人和朋友……

Muriel: No. If I forget, then they will truly be gone. What must I do?
穆列尔：我不能忘记他们。如果连我都遗忘了这一切，就代表着他们真正地死去了……

Muriel: Wait. You said my memories are sealed within these paintings. Is that true?
穆列尔：噢……对了！你刚刚说，我的记忆在画里对吗？

He raises his paintbrush, lost in abject sorrow, yet sparkling with sudden determination.
少年茫然地举起手中的画笔。

Ramona: What are you doing, Muriel? Stop!
拉莫纳：穆列尔，停下！你要干什么！

Ramona: The Vigiles will get here any minute. You don't stand a—
拉莫纳：外出的执夜人很快就会回来，你已经没有机会再——

Muriel: I remember it now. You're Ms. Ramona. You work with the Vigiles Bureau.
穆列尔：我记起来了，你是夜巡特遣的拉莫纳小姐……对吗。

Muriel: I apologize for all the trouble I've caused. It seems I've made one mistake after another.
穆列尔：抱歉，一直以来，我似乎总是在做出错误的决定……

For the last time, he uses his arcane skill.
最后一次，他启动了神秘术。

Perfect strokes of colorful paint wrap around him like a consuming fire.
无瑕的颜料将其包裹，如同最纯粹的烈焰。

Muriel: And it's too late to right all my wrongs. Thank you for putting an end to this tragedy of mine.
穆列尔：已经太迟了……但谢谢你最终阻止了这一切。

Muriel: Now the only thing left to do is to cross the tides of time and return to their warm embrace.
穆列尔：这是一段充满遗憾的旅途。而现在，我只是想穿过时间的潮水，回到他们的怀抱之中。

A rare smile touches his lips, as if he were drifting into a warm and pleasant dream as he fades away.
他如同陷入了最恬静、温暖的梦境之中，逐渐溶解，罕见地露出了笑容。

Light, shadow, and color coalesce around him, shrinking as he disappears until all that's left is a single painted hummingbird. It flies up toward the mural and joins the circle.
最终，他化作一只色彩斑斓的蜂鸟，飞向壁画上方，飞向那些旧日之物。

His brush clatters on the solid floor.
那根从不离手的画笔，掉落在空荡的长廊上。

Ramona: ...
拉莫纳：……

Ramona: All these deaths ... If only I'd realized the truth sooner ...
拉莫纳：我……如果我能早些发现这一切，或许就不会有这么多人牺牲……

Ramona: I'm sorry, Muriel. You tried so hard, but I'm not Teresita. I could never be.
拉莫纳：只是，穆列尔，我始终不可能真正成为特蕾莎。

Ramona: There are still so many things only Ramona can do.
拉莫纳：因为有许多只有“拉莫纳”才能做到的事情，还等着我去完成。

A long silence follows. It takes Ramona a moment to regain her composure, and she becomes that jaded exorcist she tries so hard to be.
漫长的寂静之中，少女许久才回过神来。

Ramona: Looks like I gotta report this now. Case is closed.
拉莫纳：我得赶紧告诉执夜人这件事。凶手伏诛，这起连环案件已经水落石出了……

Ramona: Tch, can't a girl get one peaceful night?
拉莫纳：该死，这可真是个不平静的夜晚。

Ramona: Never should've taken this damned job.
拉莫纳：当初就不该签那个该死的合同。

She gathers her feelings and leaves the building without another thought.
她收拾好自己的心情，快步走出大楼。

Some time after Ramona departs, another figure emerges from the darkness.
少女离开片刻之后，躲藏在黑暗处的身影才缓缓走出。

Añil: ...
野木蓝：……

He raises a hand toward the bullet hole in the mural, as if soothing a scar that will never heal.
他抬起手，轻轻地抚上壁画中由子弹所造成的裂痕，就像在安抚一道无法抹去的创伤。

???: Quite the tragedy.
？？？：真是个精彩至极的故事。

Añil: I was wondering which corner you might be watching from. So you came, after all, Ms. Butterfly.
野木蓝：您果然来了，蝴蝶女士。我还一直在想，您会在何处观看，又充当着怎样的角色。

Lorentz Butterfly: I couldn't miss the grand finale.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我怎么能错过这样的时刻呢？

Lorentz Butterfly: Tell me, are you satisfied? You are the one who orchestrated it all.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：看到这个结局，感觉如何？真正的——幕后凶手。

45  隐喻 / Metaphor

The man shows no fear as he turns toward the assassin.
即便被道破身份，男人也没有展现出任何惊慌，只是沉静地看向洛伦兹蝴蝶。

Añil: What do you make of this? Are we to have some sort of puppet master's tea party?
野木蓝：这算什么，凶手的茶话会吗？

Añil: You figured this all out some time ago, didn't you?
野木蓝：蝴蝶女士，您不是早就知道这一切了么。

Lorentz Butterfly: I did. But there is still some satisfaction in watching the dominoes fall, even when you know where they'll land. A little more shock would've been welcome, though.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：是啊，但我仍然享受这种道破关键的时刻。当然，如果你的脸色能变化得剧烈一些会更好。

The two of them speak with the same casual ease.
身份成谜的女士好整以暇地说。

Añil: Pray, begin then.
野木蓝：那么，请开始吧。

Lorentz Butterfly: Let's start with the mural. Look at the symbolism.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：让我们从这幅壁画开始说起。看看它上面的图案。

Lorentz Butterfly: In Aztec mythology, hummingbirds and butterflies represent death and rebirth.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：在阿兹特克神话传说中，蝴蝶与蜂鸟，是死亡与重生的象征。

Lorentz Butterfly: Fallen warriors entered the realm of the sun god. After four years, they would be reborn as butterflies and hummingbirds, returning to drink the eternal nectar of the earth.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：死去的战士在太阳神的领域生活四年后，他们会转世成蝴蝶和鸟类，重返人间汲取花蜜。

Lorentz Butterfly: To Muriel, his fellow artists of the Midnight Kitchen were like those brave warriors, destined to return, having transcended death.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：穆列尔认为，“午夜厨房”的艺术家们都是勇敢的战士，他们必将成为越过死亡而回归的人。

Añil: Spot on.
野木蓝：确实如此。

Lorentz Butterfly: This circle of hummingbirds and butterflies in the mural represents ten reborn souls.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这十只环绕在画面上的蜂鸟与蝴蝶，代表着十个复生的灵魂。

Lorentz Butterfly: But if you look closely ...
洛伦兹蝴蝶：但这幅壁画上——

Lorentz Butterfly: There are only nine figures in the mural: the seven victims, Muriel, and Ramona.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：七起凶杀案的死者，包括穆列尔自己和最后才完成的除灵师拉莫纳在内，却只有九个人的形象。

Lorentz Butterfly: So, where's the tenth? Did Muriel hide them away somewhere?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：还有一个人，他究竟藏在哪里？

Lorentz Butterfly: That's exactly what he did. It's quite clever, really, merging a metaphor, or a pun, if you will, into the mural.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这是穆列尔在壁画上进行的精心设计——一个明显的隐喻，一个无伤大雅的玩笑。

Lorentz Butterfly: Everyone, including Muriel, calls you Añil.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：他叫你野木蓝。所有人都这么叫你。

Lorentz Butterfly: Añil, the name of a plant native to southern Mexico used to create a rich indigo dye. Over time, it also became the name for that deep, vibrant shade of blue.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这是一种产自墨西哥南部的草本植物，通过复杂的提炼过程，可以制作出光彩夺目的靛蓝色颜料。它昂贵，鲜亮，不易褪色，是一种珍稀的材料。

Lorentz Butterfly: For centuries before the arrival of the Europeans, indigenous peoples used it to dye the cloaks of their leaders. Like Tyrrhenian red, this deep indigo blue symbolized power in their culture.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：早在哥伦布时代以前，原住民就使用它来为领袖的斗篷染色。靛蓝与胭脂红一样，代表着权力，是原住民文化的一部分。

Lorentz Butterfly: And behold, that very color is hidden in this mural. The rivers and trees in the background glow with its intensity.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：看这些河流，背景上的树木，它们都呈现出如此明亮、仿佛能令人灼伤的颜色……

Lorentz Butterfly: Muriel used that blue añil to paint those sections, blending your presence into the mural in the most conspicuous yet subtle way.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我猜测，壁画上的这些部分，都是使用野木蓝颜料所绘制的。你就藏在这个画作最显眼的地方，无处不在。

Lorentz Butterfly: Muriel wasn't the only member of the Midnight Kitchen to cross the "Storm," was he?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：穆列尔并不是那个唯一越过“暴雨”的人，对吗？

Lorentz Butterfly: There was another artist of the Midnight Kitchen who went to Europe with him.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：在当年，还有一个人，一个“午夜厨房”艺术家集会的成员，也同样想要学习欧洲的绘画技术，于是陪伴着他一同前往巴黎。

Lorentz Butterfly: That artist was you, Añil.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：那个人正是你，野木蓝。

Añil: And?
野木蓝：这能代表什么？

Añil: Like Muriel, I survived the "Storms." What does it matter?
野木蓝：我确实越过了“暴雨”，我从未否认过这点。

Lorentz Butterfly: Oh, it matters in many ways.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：噢，这能代表的事情就非常多了。

Lorentz Butterfly: Those victims, your handpicked vessels, were all arcanists who bore some resemblance to the friends you lost in the "Storm."
洛伦兹蝴蝶：那些受害者，你们所挑选的意识容器，都是一些与昔日友人有一定相似之处的神秘学家。

Lorentz Butterfly: You lured them to the Turquoise Serpent Club and presented each with a meticulously crafted gift, a painting that would become their death warrant.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：他们被各种各样的方式引诱，最终都接触俱乐部，带回了让自己殒命的那一幅画作——一封精心炮制的死亡来信。

Lorentz Butterfly: True, Muriel was a genius in arcanum. He single-handedly devised a resurrection ritual based on Roseau's research and ancient legends.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：诚然，穆列尔是一个神秘学研究上的天才。他在罗西教授的启发下，仅通过查阅神话典籍，就能研发出他想象中的“起死回生术”。

Lorentz Butterfly: But with every painting he completed, he lost fragments of his memory. A flawed method.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：但他错误的方式，使他每绘制一幅画作，就会失去一部分的记忆。

Lorentz Butterfly: In the end, he was no longer whole.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：到最后，他已经不能称得上是一个完整的存在了。

Lorentz Butterfly: How could he have planned and executed all this, alone, in such a state?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这样的他，又怎么具备实施如此精密计划的能力？

Lorentz Butterfly: Finding eight suitable targets among hundreds of thousands in Mexico City, coaxing them to accept paintings they probably had no interest in.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：试想一下，在墨西哥城数十万的人口中，寻觅到八个与记忆最接近的目标，又引导他们接触一幅自己本不感兴趣的画作……

Lorentz Butterfly: Each step required meticulous observation and careful planning.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这需要多么缜密的安排与细致的人性观察，才能将这些人一步一步地引导到对应的位置。

Lorentz Butterfly: It was all you pulling the strings, Añil. A pigment dealer kidnapped by gangsters? Just a cover.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：你才是那个在背后操纵一切的人，野木蓝。你从来不是什么黑帮的受害者，缺乏自保手段的颜料商人只是你的伪装。

Lorentz Butterfly: The most brilliant part is that you orchestrated this entire series of murders just to bring Ramona to center stage!
洛伦兹蝴蝶：最精妙绝伦的部分在于，这一系列的凶杀案，本身就是引导除灵师拉莫纳走入布局的一部分！

She laughs softly but without a trace of warmth.
她笑了起来。

Lorentz Butterfly: Haha. You watched her, studied her nature, leveraged her hatred of injustice and her love for this city.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：你观察她，利用她的性格，知晓她是多么嫉恶如仇，又热爱着这片土地，热爱着这座城市。

Lorentz Butterfly: You lured her to this mural, intending to offer her as the final sacrifice to fulfill your ultimate wish.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：你指引她走到这幅壁画前，完成你们最后的愿想。

Lorentz Butterfly: But I still have one question.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：因此，我只有一个疑惑。

Añil: Please, ask it. I'll not hold my tongue.
野木蓝：请问吧，蝴蝶女士。无论是什么，我都言无不尽。

Lorentz Butterfly: You completed every step, executed every detail according to your plan. You could have succeeded. So, why didn't you stop me?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：告诉我，这样一个心思细致、掌控一切的凶手，为什么在最后关头，没有尝试阻止我，而让一切付诸东流？

His response is muted. There's almost a hint of surprise, but it rests beneath his calm composure.
闻言，男子反而露出了然的神色。

Añil: That's all you want to ask? I see.
野木蓝：这竟然是您最感兴趣的部分吗，女士？我想我明白了……

Añil: Your deductions are perfect, of course, Ms. Butterfly. I am merely an amateur by comparison.
野木蓝：您的推论非常准确，蝴蝶女士。在您这样的行家面前，我不过是班门弄斧罢了。

Añil: In the past four years, Muriel and I have survived six "Storms."
野木蓝：四年间，我们总共穿越了六次“暴雨”。

Añil: As more "Storms" fell, returning to Mexico City to find suitable candidates grew increasingly difficult.
野木蓝：每一次重返墨西哥城，寻找合适的人选，都是一个充满困难的过程。

Añil: To put our full focus into our resurrection research, we left Manus Vindictae, not that they had any use for two unmanageable pawns anyway.
野木蓝：穆列尔的研究逐渐深入，为了完成这一切，我们脱离了重塑之手，毕竟他们不需要一颗不受控制的棋子。

Añil: Time and again, our work was interrupted by the "Storm," and the ritual's results were never promising to begin with.
野木蓝：数次，研究被“暴雨”打断；而仪式本身的不断失败，更是让情况变得越来越糟糕。

Añil: I observed each candidate carefully, searching for some trace of our lost friends' souls.
野木蓝：我寻找、观察着每一个人选，寻找他们与友人“灵魂”的契合之处。

Añil: But the closer I looked, the clearer it became: They weren't the same.
野木蓝：但我越是观察，就越清楚地意识到：她们并非同一个人。

Añil: Ramona, for instance, was in many ways the perfect candidate. And yet, she wasn't Teresita. She could never be Teresita.
野木蓝：拉莫纳小姐，她很好。只是，她不是特蕾莎，也永远不可能成为特蕾莎。

Añil: The mentor we all admired, the friend who brought warmth and light into our often bleak lives—she was gone. And nothing we did could bring her back.
野木蓝：那个我们所有人都敬仰的，如同太阳一般温暖与热烈的特蕾莎，她永远不会回到我们身边了，对吗？

Añil: *sigh* Ramona's story, however, isn't over. Her future, her dreams, her fierce love for this city ... I realized I couldn't bring myself to destroy that.
野木蓝：拉莫纳小姐有着自己的故事，自己的向往与未来。她守护着这里，而我……无法去摧毁这一切。

Añil: That's all.
野木蓝：仅此而已。

Lorentz Butterfly: That's all? Really?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：……真的吗？仅此而已？

Her doubt is written plain on her face, but Añil can only smile bitterly.
她的面上写满怀疑，而男子只是浅笑一声。

Añil: My answer disappoints you. Allow me to put it another way.
野木蓝：如果这样未能令您满意，那么请让我换个说法吧，蝴蝶女士。

Añil: The "Storms" have carried me across time, even into the future. And there, I saw the tedious repetition of human history.
野木蓝：经历过这么多次的“暴雨”，我也曾数次前往未来，见证了我们追求与愿想的结局。

Añil: And the hollow, meaningless cycle of our pursuits. In the end, art cannot change the world.
野木蓝：最终我只是发现，我们的生活周而复始，艺术实际上并不能做到任何真正的改变。

Añil: Our ideal of reshaping society for the better through fine arts eventually became just another capitalist venture, a product, another ignoble chapter in the history of art.
野木蓝：那些反叛与变化的思想，最终都被资本吸收，成了广告与商品上的精美包装，成为艺术史上的一个段落。

Añil: And my partner, my last remaining friend, lost himself along the way.
野木蓝：而唯一仍与我一同的伙伴，他已经失去了所有，变得面目全非。

Añil: I've walked this tortuous path alone for a long, long time.
野木蓝：我好像一个人走了很远的路程……

Añil: I'm tired.
野木蓝：我只是感到累了。

Doubt turns to annoyance. This web of intrigue once again turns out to be nothing more than a cat's cradle, child's play to unravel.
非常可惜，她并没有寻觅到任何超出想象的阴谋与危机。

Lorentz Butterfly: Tch, what a disappointment.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：啧。你的回答当真是让我有些失望了。

Lorentz Butterfly: I would have hoped people like us never get tired.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我一直认为像我们这样的人是不会感到累的。

Añil: "People like us"? *chuckle* I'm honored to be considered among the likes of you.
野木蓝：“我们”……？哈。真是荣幸。

Añil: But enough about me, Ms. Butterfly. Would you care to talk about yourself a bit?
野木蓝：蝴蝶女士，谈论了如此多关于我的话题，您是否有兴趣，来聊聊您自己？

Lorentz Butterfly: Me?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：——我？

The playful light leaves her eyes. The man notices but continues anyway.
她眼中的玩味褪去了。但对方无视了危险的信号，只是继续淡然说着。

Añil: On our way to the auction, just before we teleported via that Sun Stone.
野木蓝：从俱乐部前往拍卖会现场的时候，我们曾经接触过一块用作传送仪式的太阳石。

Añil: I realized you couldn't distinguish certain colors, even ones that appear quite different to most.
野木蓝：那个时候，我意识到了一件事情：蝴蝶女士，您对某些颜色极其不敏感，具体来说，您无法区分一些对常人来说差异非常明显的色彩。

Añil: I imagine that's why you couldn't crack the case earlier.
野木蓝：我想，这也是像您这样的犯罪天才，无法在第一时间确认真凶的原因。

Añil: It must have taken you considerable effort to discern that the most critical clue lay in the painting's colors.
野木蓝：您大概耗费了许多时间才发现，一切秘密隐藏在画面的颜色里吧。

Lorentz Butterfly: You've uncovered a minor flaw of mine. Do you intend to threaten me with it?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：所以呢，这个小小的发现能发挥什么作用？你该不会以为这样能威胁到我吧？

Añil: If I'm honest, I'm not entirely sure.
野木蓝：老实说，具体它会发挥什么作用，我并不知道。

Añil: What if my name were a color you couldn't recognize? How very fortunate you were that I was named Añil.
野木蓝：如果我的名字，正巧是您无法辨认的颜色呢？您应该庆幸，野木蓝色并不属于此列。

Añil: It may be a "minor" flaw, as you say, something easily concealed.
野木蓝：于您而言，也许它只是一个微不足道的问题，您可以使用各种各样的方式来掩盖它。

Añil: But someone as meticulous as you would never allow anyone to know of it, be they friend or enemy.
野木蓝：但我相信，像您这样心思细致、想要掌控一切的人，是无法允许任何人得知这样的小缺陷的，无论是您的朋友还是敌人。

Lorentz Butterfly: I could kill you to keep this quiet. Did you consider that?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：你就不担心我杀了你吗？

Añil: You won't.
野木蓝：您不会的。

The threat of death washes over him without effect.
面对死亡的威胁，他却十分平静。

Añil: You crave total control. You need to perfectly discern your target's mind, predict their every move, before you pull the trigger.
野木蓝：如您自己先前所言，您在扣下扳机之前，必须完全掌握目标的性格，推演目标的行动。

Añil: You enjoy playing god, holding all the cards, so that life and death become yours to give. You won't kill me until you've mapped out every crevice of my mind.
野木蓝：您想要扮演操纵他人生死命运的神灵，因此，在能够完全掌控我的思维之前，您是不会动手的。

Lorentz Butterfly: ...
洛伦兹蝴蝶：……

Lorentz Butterfly: Impressive, Añil.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：你让我印象深刻，野木蓝。

Lorentz Butterfly: I'll be sure to keep your insights in mind.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我会记住这、份、大、礼的。

She emphasizes her words and extends a hand to him in as earnest a gesture as she can offer.
她加重了咬字，“友好”地朝对方伸出手。

Lorentz Butterfly: Guess we'll be keeping our little secrets between us.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：那我们就好好地保护对方的小秘密了。

Añil: With pleasure. If you need anything regarding the Manus, information, items, reach out to me. I'll do my best to assist.
野木蓝：作为诚意，如果您有任何关于重塑之手的需求，无论是信息抑或是物品，我都会尽力为您办到。

Añil: And please, take this. My gift.
野木蓝：另外，这份小礼物请您收下。

Añil: Use it sparingly.
野木蓝：请节约使用。

Añil: It's no easy task to craft this pigment now.
野木蓝：如今，想要再次制作这种颜料，并非易事。

Lorentz Butterfly: Ha, your contribution to science is appreciated.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：哈，感谢你为科学所做的贡献。

46  美德 / Virtues

The commander's furious shouting snaps Marguerite back to reality.
脚步声由远及近，军官愤怒的吼声唤回了玛格丽特游移的思绪。

Marguerite: She's back.
玛格丽特：她回来了。

Hush Squad Commander: You again, Larisa?
寂静小队军官：拉丽莎，是你干的对不对？

Hush Squad Commander: It's one problem after another with you!
寂静小队军官：这一路上，你可没少给我惹麻烦——

Hush Trooper: I-I didn't—
寂静者：我、我不……

Before she can say anything, the commander grabs her subordinate by the front of her shirt.
还没来得及反驳，军官已经一把揪起下属的衣领。

Hush Squad Commander: How many times do I have to tell you? You're a Hush Trooper. This is no place for a softy.
寂静小队军官：我说过多少次了，在成为寂静者之后，给我收起你那无用的善心！

Hush Squad Commander: Who do you think you are, huh? A hero fighting against authority? A savior to these people?
寂静小队军官：你以为你在干什么正确的事情吗？想要反抗权威，改变这些逃兵的命运？

Hush Squad Commander: Even if you save their lives here, they'll probably end up dead where they're going.
寂静小队军官：我告诉你，哪怕你在这里救下了他们，他们也未必能熬过下一次的实验！

Hush Squad Commander: All you've done is delayed the mission. You want to be kicked out of Zeno and left to the "Storm"?
寂静小队军官：每一次你违背上级的指令，都有可能导致我们的任务延误——然后被芝诺放逐，被“暴雨”冲走。

Hush Squad Commander: Is that what you want? To get the whole squad killed to feed your ego?
寂静小队军官：这就是你想要的下场吗——想害死我们所有人？！

Hush Trooper: Y-You knew all along. They aren't really deserters.
寂静者：你……你早就知道了……他们并不是真正的逃兵，只是实验的耗材……

Larisa's voice trembles as she dares to speak the words.
她颤抖着问出早已知晓答案的话。

Hush Trooper: That's what we've been doing all this time?
寂静者：这些日子以来，一次又一次……

Hush Trooper: Sending innocent arcanists to their deaths one after another.
寂静者：我们只是在送这些神秘学家去死……

Hush Squad Commander: So what?
寂静小队军官：所以呢？

Their commander's reply is as stark and biting as the wind lashing their faces.
军官冷漠地注视着陷入绝望的她。

Hush Squad Commander: Even more will die if we can't figure out how to stop the "Storm."
寂静小队军官：如果我们改变不了“暴雨”的现状，牺牲的人只会越来越多。

Hush Squad Commander: Ugh, I'm wasting my time talking to the both of you.
寂静小队军官：但我已经意识到了，反复地和你说这些根本没有用处。

Hush Squad Commander: Once this mission is over, I'm filing a request to have you removed from my squad. As for where you'll end up, or whether Zeno will even keep you, I don't give a damn.
寂静小队军官：等这次任务结束了，我会回去申请，让你退出寂静小队。至于你将来的去处，是否还能留在芝诺，你就好自为之吧。

Hush Trooper: You-You can't do this.
寂静者：你……你不能这样做！

Hush Squad Commander: Why not? Without Zeno's shelter, there's nothing you can do against the "Storm."
寂静小队军官：离开了芝诺，你不仅失去改变一切的能力，还马上会消失在“暴雨”之中。

Then the commander turns to Marguerite. She's leaning against the truck without emotion. In that moment, it seems as if their conflict does not concern her in the slightest.
军官将视线移向一旁的少女，她默不作声地靠在车上，仿佛面前爆发的争执与她无关。

Hush Squad Commander: And you, Marguerite!
寂静小队军官：还有你，玛格丽特！

Hush Squad Commander: You think staying silent will get you off the hook? I know you're the one who unlocked the door for her.
寂静小队军官：别以为你一声不吭就可以逃过这一切。我知道是你给她打开车门锁的。

She lunges for Marguerite's collar in the same way, but the young woman twists out of the way and catches her commander's wrist.
她想要如法炮制地揪住少女的领子，却被她猛地握住了手腕，无法移动一寸。

Marguerite: You're wrong.
玛格丽特：你有一句话说错了。

Hush Squad Commander: What? L-Let go of me! What do you think you're doing?
寂静小队军官：什么话……快放手！你在干什么？！

Hush Squad Commander: Argh!
寂静小队军官：啊——！！

The commander falls to the ground with a sharp cry.
军官倒在地上，发出一声惨叫。

Hush Trooper: What is going on, Marguerite? Have you lost your mind?
寂静者：你在做什么，玛格丽特？！你疯了吗……

Hush Trooper: You'll face a military tribunal for attacking our commander, especially at a time like this.
寂静者：这可是要上军事法庭的重罪……！！更别提现在是“暴雨”时期……

Marguerite: Hush Squad's number-one rule: "No communication with mission targets." That's what she said.
玛格丽特：长官之前说，芝诺寂静小队的第一条准则，就是不许与任务目标有任何接触。

Hush Trooper: Huh?
寂静者：什么？

Marguerite: I just wanted to tell her she was wrong.
玛格丽特：我只是想告诉她，这句话说得不对。

Marguerite: If you really want to play with life and death, you need to get inside the target's head, know how they think and move. That's how you assert control.
玛格丽特：如果不能掌握目标的所有行动，窥探对方思想的最深处，就想要扮演掌控他人生死的上帝——这是多么傲慢的想法。

Marguerite: That's the best part.
玛格丽特：再说了，这是一个很有意思的过程。

Hush Trooper: This is no time for jokes, Marguerite.
寂静者：现在可不是开玩笑的时候……

Marguerite: Let me show you. Our dear commander here always keeps a bullet tucked in her sleeve.
玛格丽特：比如说，我们的这位长官，她总是习惯将一颗子弹藏在袖口处。

Marguerite: Ever since the war in 1999, she's carried it around as a little "lucky charm."
玛格丽特：自99年的那场战争以后，这是她一直以来留给自己的“护身符”。

Marguerite pulls a bullet from her sleeve, revealing it purposefully to the frightened commander at her feet.
在军官惊恐的眼神之中，少女蹲下身来，从对方的袖口摸出了那颗子弹。

Marguerite: As expected.
玛格丽特：果然如此。

Hush Trooper: So you know the commander's secret. What does it matter? She isn't your target, is she?
寂静者：玛格丽特，你即使知道这些，那有什么用……？长官又不是你的行动目标……

Marguerite: You're right. She's not my target.
玛格丽特：是啊，她并非我的目标。

Hush Trooper: Th-Then why did you ...
寂静者：什、什么……

She loads the round into a pistol and pulls back the slide.
那颗子弹被少女填装入手枪中，她将枪上膛。

Then she turns to place the gun in Larisa's hand. The terrified girl is forced to level the weapon at their commander's head.
手枪被放入同行的寂静者手中，玛格丽特抬起她的手腕，瞄准地上无法动弹的军官。

Marguerite: She's yours, Larisa.
玛格丽特：而是你的目标，拉丽莎。

Hush Trooper: M-Mine?
寂静者：我……我……

Hush Trooper: Marguerite, this is wrong. We-We can't do this. I can't do this.
寂静者：玛格丽特，我们不能这么做……我、不能这么做……

Hush Squad Commander: You're insane, both of you! Put the gun down, and let me go.
寂静小队军官：疯了……你们两个疯子！快把枪放下！！！

Marguerite: Think, Larisa. Why should you let her go?
玛格丽特：你想清楚了，拉丽莎，你真的要放过她吗？

Marguerite: She'll report everything you did. They'll make sure you quietly disappear in the "Storm."
玛格丽特：只要你让她逃出这里，马上，她就会上报芝诺，最终你只会悄无声息地消失在“暴雨”里。

Marguerite: You don't really care what happens to you, I know.
玛格丽特：不——对你来说，这些都不重要。

Marguerite: But think about those poor kidnapped "deserters." They're just innocent arcanists, some of them children.
玛格丽特：想想那些被关在车厢里，还在生着病的所谓“逃兵们”……他们都只是一些普通的神秘学家，甚至还有孩子。

Marguerite: They'll be sent to that lab, subjected to torture you can't even imagine, unless you do something now.
玛格丽特：如果你今天不做出选择，他们就会按照原来的计划，被送到实验室里去，遭受你所不能想象的神秘术折磨。

Marguerite: You've known this all along. It's time you faced it.
玛格丽特：这些，你都想象过的，你只是不敢面对。

Hush Trooper: ...
寂静者：……

Hush Trooper: You're right. I-I have to face it.
寂静者：你说得对，我……我要面对这一切……

Hush Trooper: I have to ...
寂静者：我要……

Hush Squad Commander: Larisa, don't!
寂静小队军官：拉丽莎，住手！！！

The crack of the pistol dies against the swirling winds. Snow already covering the evidence of what had just occurred—scattered footprints and a body oozing life into the cold permafrost.
雪地上一片寂静，仅有凌乱的脚印和一具尸体昭示着刚刚发生过的事情。

Marguerite looks into her commander's fading eyes as they take on a deathly gloss. The snow is already settling on her frozen skin, burying the last moment of terror still written on her face.
玛格丽特凝视着军官苍白的面庞，上面残留着惊恐的神色。很快，这张脸也要被掩盖在白雪之下。

Marguerite: I like that look on your face. It suits you.
玛格丽特：我喜欢你现在的表情，比之前好多了。

Marguerite: Drako   Drought.
玛格丽特：火龙鳞鳞。

Arcane fire burns away every trace of what transpired there, even the transport truck marked with Zeno symbols.
神秘术燃起的烈焰焚烧了所有现场遗留的痕迹，包括那台带有芝诺标记的装载车。

She looks out over the snowy expanse. She can still just about spot the many footprints leading away from the scene. Whether Larisa and her liberated "deserters" will survive their trek is a mystery even she hasn't calculated to its end.
她又看向雪地上的脚印。数分钟前，那群半大的孩子们跟随着拉丽莎离去了，他们能否活着穿过雪原，仍然是未知数。

Marguerite: Time to run, dear. Follow that dark mix of kindness and viciousness in your heart straight into the quagmire.
玛格丽特：跑吧，跑吧，追随你心中的善念与恶意，踏入这片泥沼……

Marguerite: I look forward to seeing you fully submerged in it in the future.
玛格丽特：未来重逢之时，亲爱的拉丽莎，我期待看到你彻底沦陷的模样。

Marguerite: Well, I suppose that wraps up my days in Zeno, boring as they were, besides the death.
玛格丽特：真是无趣的芝诺生活。除了死人，这里没有发生过任何好事。

Marguerite: Where to next? Maybe back to Cambridge?
玛格丽特：接下来该去哪儿呢？也许重返剑桥是个不错的选择……

Marguerite: One day, I'll figure out what Zeno, and a certain white house behind it, is really up to.
玛格丽特：我会弄清楚芝诺——乃至是那个躲在芝诺背后的白色石膏小房子，究竟在做些什么。

She turns away, walking alone into the icy wilderness.
她转身离去，身影消失在风雪之中。

47  纯属虚构 / Pure Fiction

Hall, Foundation
基金会·办事大厅

The gray and white tiled floor is polished smooth. It reflects people hurrying past.
灰白格子地板纤尘不染，反射出来往的匆匆身影。

On a weekday afternoon, the St. Pavlov Foundation office is nearly empty. Waiting in a chair, a young writer scribbles her thoughts down in a notebook.
工作日的午后，圣洛夫基金会的办事处门可罗雀。窗口前，手持笔记本的青年作家正在“唰唰”地记录着什么。

Recoleta: I've got it now. The core of this story lies in the metaphor woven into the mural. It's the thread that ties the entire case together.
虚构集：我已经构思好了这个故事的核心切入点：一个贯穿案件始终，在壁画上构建的隐喻！

Recoleta: Thank you again, Ms. Marguerite. Thank you for allowing me to weave your experiences into my novel. I wish I could travel to a different era in Mexico, too. It would be great for gathering material.
虚构集：非常感谢你，女士！感谢你愿意让我以你的经历作为小说原型。我一直很想回到不同年代的墨西哥采风，只是至今没找到合适的机会外出。

Recoleta: Oh, and may I call you Ms. Butterfly? It has a sonorous quality to it that is positively brilliant.
虚构集：噢，或许我也可以称呼你为蝴蝶女士——我喜欢这个名字。

Lorentz Butterfly: Of course, my dear girl.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：当然，亲爱的小作家。

Recoleta: You know what? The moment I saw you, I sensed something special about you.
虚构集：在我看到你的那一刻，我就知道你身上绝对有些不同寻常的地方。

Lorentz Butterfly: I think I should take that as a compliment.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这算是一个赞美吗？那我就接受了。

Another employee speaks up from behind his desk.
闻言，端坐在办事窗口后的职员终于忍不住开口。

Name Day: I've heard you say something along those lines at least three times in the past hour, Ms. Recoleta.
命名日：虚构集小姐，这半个多钟头里，我听见了类似的句子至少三次。

Name Day: It's a nice little opening line to get people talking about themselves, but—
命名日：作为搭讪开场白，它固然有效，能从别人那里捞到些隐藏的故事，但……

Recoleta: Ahem, that's not entirely accurate, actually.
虚构集：咳咳……你这么说是不对的！

Recoleta: Only Ms. Butterfly has shared her story with me.
虚构集：因为只有蝴蝶女士真正把自己的故事告诉我了。

Recoleta: Director Noire was right. Instead of revisiting my own experiences again and again, I should try writing about those around me.
虚构集：菲林士多导演建议，我应该跳出只描写自身经历的怪圈，写一些身边的人与事。

Recoleta: Maybe then, I'll finally break free of this restless river.
虚构集：我觉得她的提议非常有道理。也许这样，我就能不再受困于那片不安的水域……

Name Day: Yeah, sure. In other words, you'll be diving into a new topic for a while.
命名日：是的，是的，我已经明白，接下来的一段时间，你要打算沉迷于什么了——新的题材、新的观点。

Name Day: Your instincts always pull you toward uncharted—and dangerous, might I add—territory.
命名日：你的本能总在告诉你，新颖又危险的事物是好的。

Recoleta: Sounds like an exhilarating adventure, don't you think?
虚构集：这难道不是一场精彩绝伦的冒险吗？

Recoleta: Hmm. Maybe I should write from Muriel's perspective. It adds a melancholic twist to this already sad story.
虚构集：如果想让剧情呈现得更曲折一些，我应该从穆列尔的角度来写这篇悲伤的故事！

Recoleta: The loss of his friends to the "Storm," and the gradual loss of his own mind.
虚构集：在“暴雨”中失散的友人，逐渐支离破碎的自我——

Recoleta: A story like this could serve as a kind of a tribute to all our friends washed away by the "Storm."
虚构集：也许我可以通过这样的故事，来永远铭记那些被“暴雨”回溯的友人们。

Lorentz Butterfly: *scoff* So the dead gain eternal life through your book.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：这也算是一种文字永生了。

Name Day: You really have a dark sense of humor.
命名日：我喜欢这个地狱笑话。

Recoleta: Well, it could also work if the protagonist is the mysterious, elegant assassin. Her antisocial nature would certainly stand out.
虚构集：当然，神秘、优雅、美丽的女杀手视角也很好，甚至其中的反社会性格部分也抵抗了经典的市场潮流……

Name Day looks at the outspoken writer as if about to say something, but stops short.
基金会职员欲言又止地看着面前口无遮拦的青年作家。

Lorentz Butterfly: Write your story however you like, Ms. Recoleta.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：请随意，虚构集小姐。你完全可以按照你喜欢的方式来编写这个故事。

Lorentz Butterfly: My only requirement is that it help me to lure someone out, the one who invited me to join Lorentz.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我只是需要通过这个故事，去找到一个人——当初那个让我加入洛伦兹的人。

Lorentz Butterfly: If she still has eyes in the Foundation, this story will be more than enough to draw her attention.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：假设她如今仍在基金会留有眼线，这样的“寻人启事”，已经足够让她露面了。

Recoleta: Just leave it to me. I'll do all I can to spread it throughout the Foundation. Of course, with names and locations adjusted as necessary.
虚构集：请放心，蝴蝶女士。我会尽力把这个故事传遍基金会的——并且对其中的情节进行适当的加工，模糊掉里面的人名与地点。

Recoleta: But at its heart, it must remain a story about cultural and class conflict. Hmm. I'd better build an intricate narrative structure to carry all that dramatic weight.
虚构集：噢，当然，故事的核心仍然是文化现象的变更、阶级冲突和社会矛盾……还需要构建一个足够复杂的情节关系，这样才有剧情张力……

Name Day: And maybe just a speck of authenticity.
命名日：还有一粒灰尘那么点大的“真实性”。

Recoleta: Hey, are you seriously criticizing a novel for being fictional?
虚构集：你无权指责一篇小说的真实性！

Recoleta returns to her notebook, only for her colleague to pipe up once again. And as if to make things worse, he's come bearing forms. Work—real, actual work—slams her back into reality.
眼看青年作家已经忍不住在笔记本上书写起来，基金会职员连忙打断了她。

Name Day: Here, Recoleta. I've finished reviewing the forms you submitted.
命名日：来，虚构集，给你。你的表格已经处理好了。

Recoleta: Oh, thank you, Mr. Name Day. I almost forgot why I came here in the first place.
虚构集：谢谢你，命名日先生。我险些忘了我来这儿是要干什么的。

Name Day: Latham must be thrilled you've joined the Arcanum Containment Department. She finally has a subordinate to boss around.
命名日：莱瑟姆小姐应该非常开心吧——毕竟在你加入神秘学藏品管理部之后，她也终于能够拥有一名下属了。

Recoleta: Now I see why you were banished out here and left to stamp papers all day. Do you always put your foot in your mouth like this?
虚构集：命名日先生，你就是因为说错了太多话，才会被发配至这里盖章。

Recoleta: In fact, I couldn't be happier to be working with Ms. Latham. She's an amazing colleague and has taught me all kinds of things.
虚构集：莱瑟姆小姐是个称职的前辈!

Name Day: Sure, sure. But just to be clear, I wasn't "banished." Stamping papers here is just a step on my path to becoming head of the Far East branch. Bureaucracy, you know how it is.
命名日：请注意，这可不是发配。这只是远东分部办公室主管上任前的必经之路。一些官僚主义流程，你懂的。

Recoleta: I don't, actually, and I have absolutely no interest in finding out. Ever since Ms. Latham went on medical leave, I've hardly seen my desk beneath all the paperwork.
虚构集：我对这些官僚主义的产物一丁点儿兴趣都没有。自从莱瑟姆开始休病假后，神秘学藏品管理部的这些文书工作才不得不落到我头上。

Name Day: It seems you're getting a crash course.
命名日：欢迎来到官僚主义的大本营。

Name Day: Wait, medical leave? Is Ms. Latham alright?
命名日：对了，你刚刚说起病假？莱瑟姆小姐怎么了吗？

Recoleta: She's in the rehab center getting some of her parts replaced. If you ask me, it was Vertin who gave her the false confidence to do field missions.
虚构集：现在她正躺在康复中心里更换零件呢。说实在的，是维尔汀给了她能出外勤的虚假自信。

Recoleta: Now I have to fight alone against this colossal, cumbersome institution and its endless piles of paperwork.
虚构集：为了能尽快摆脱一无所知的状态，我正在尝试和这个累赘又庞大的机构以及它无效的文书工作和谐共处。

Recoleta: Words were never meant to be wasted on this sort of thing.
虚构集：文字真不该被用作干这种事——

Recoleta: If Vertin ever plans a round two against the Foundation, call me immediately. I'm a bit of an expert in rebellion.
虚构集：如果维尔汀有第二次对抗基金会的计划，请迅速通知我。在这方面我可是专家。

Name Day: Yeah. I'll keep an ear out for you.
命名日：我会帮你打探消息的。

Name Day: But I'll need a copy of your latest book in return, just like last time.
命名日：和之前一样，把你的新作寄一本来当报酬就行。

Recoleta: I'd be delighted to. You are my number one reader after all.
虚构集：当然，我最忠诚的读者！

Name Day: Aleph, Marcus, Sonetto, Latham, Noire, Blonney, Barbara, and now me. Seems like you have a lot of number one readers.
命名日：阿莱夫先生、马库斯小姐、十四行诗小姐、莱瑟姆小姐、菲林士多女士、金蜜儿小姐、数羊羔小姐，现在还有我，你“最”忠诚的读者真的很多。

Recoleta: Don't forget Pickles.
虚构集：还有皮克勒斯！

She hurriedly shoves the stamped papers into her notebook.
女孩胡乱地将这些盖着各种印章的公文夹进笔记本里。

Recoleta: I do have one more question, Ms. Butterfly.
虚构集：蝴蝶女士，我还有一个疑问。

Recoleta: It doesn't look like you have any forms to stamp, and you're clearly not just here to chat with us, so ...
虚构集：你一直站在这儿，看起来并不是为了处理什么事务，也并非和我们闲聊——

Recoleta: Are you waiting for someone?
虚构集：你是在等什么人吗？

Butterfly doesn't get a chance to respond. The office door swings open. Two Laplace researchers enter, arguing in quick rallies, like it was a sport for them.
贡献了精彩故事的杀手女士还没来得及回答，办事处的大门被猛地推开，伴随着争吵，两名拉普拉斯研究员大步走了进来。

Enigma: This makes no sense.
哑谜：这根本不合常理！

Enigma: She even found time to write a full-fledged paper after all that? How is this possible?
哑谜：我搞不明白她到底哪来的时间写论文的。

Enigma holds up a letter. His body bristles with an anger to match his complete frustration.
代理负责人高举手中的挑衅信，语气充满愤怒。

Ulrich: Calm down. Lorentz Butterfly has already sent us the lab reports.
乌尔里希：冷静些，洛伦兹蝴蝶已经把所有的实验报告都寄给了我们。

Ulrich: Strictly speaking, Institutum Lorentz is also part of Laplace, which means we've achieved this major scientific breakthrough together.
乌尔里希：严格来说，洛伦兹研究所也是拉普拉斯内部的机构，这代表着拉普拉斯取得了重大的科研突破。

Enigma: Don't you "together" me! When was Institutum Lorentz ever formally recognized?
哑谜：什么时候、由谁承认过洛伦兹研究所的合理性了！

Enigma: Besides, we need first-hand experimental data and materials, not just reports.
哑谜：我们需要一手的实验数据和材料！

Ulrich: But the Staff Committee is quite satisfied with this operation's outcome. Mr. Ludwig himself said it's the results that matter.
乌尔里希：人事委员会对这次行动的结果非常满意，路德维希先生也表示不要再追究其中的细节。

Enigma: Well, he didn't refuse my investigation permit either.
