哑谜：但他没有反对我签发调查许可文件。

Enigma: There's something suspicious in Latham's report.
哑谜：莱瑟姆女士的调查报告中，提到了一个不起眼的异常。

Enigma: According to Ramona, the Vigiles exorcist who assisted Latham in her investigation, a Foundation employee named Jones was involved in the case.
哑谜：曾经协助莱瑟姆女士调查的夜巡特遣除灵师推断，在那段时间里，有一名基金会工作人员在此事中牵扯颇深。

Enigma: This Jones visited the crime scenes and repeatedly requested access to case data. Multiple Vigils saw her.
哑谜：据说对方申请调用了不少夜巡特遣的资料，并且多次出入案发现场。有不少执夜人都见过她。

Ulrich: And what does that matter?
乌尔里希：所以呢？

Enigma: Are you serious? That's your reaction? Did it not occur to you that this Jones is likely the Lorentz Butterfly herself, taunting us?!
哑谜：所以？！所以这个名叫琼斯女士的职员，极有可能就是洛伦兹蝴蝶！

Enigma: She has access to Foundation intel. That's probably why she's always one step ahead.
哑谜：她掌握着基金会内部的信息渠道，这就是她总是先我们一步的原因。

He walks over to the desk before spotting a familiar face. At once, he lets his mood settle and offers a pleasant-ish greeting.
代理负责人走至窗口前，马上认出了等候在此处的拉普拉斯同事。

Enigma: Oh, Marguerite. What are you doing here?
哑谜：噢，玛格丽特。你在这儿干什么？

Lorentz Butterfly: Good afternoon, Mr. Adler.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：阿德勒先生，下午好。

Lorentz Butterfly: I'm here to submit the "Storm" meteorological observation data requested by the Foundation.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我来这儿上交基金会需要的“暴雨”气象观测数据……

She carefully tunes her words: just a touch of concern, not enough to imply any interest in the topic.
她的面上恰到好处地流露出担忧的神色。

Lorentz Butterfly: Oh, Mr. Ulrich is here too. Did something happen at Laplace?
洛伦兹蝴蝶：您和乌尔里希组长……是科算中心发生什么事情了吗？

Enigma: No, nothing to worry about. You've heard of Institutum Lorentz, haven't you? The mysterious organization hiding within Laplace.
哑谜：没什么。你听说过洛伦兹研究所的传闻吧，那个潜伏在拉普拉斯内部的组织。

Lorentz Butterfly: Yes. Though I can't really fathom what they're after.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：是的，当然。真不知道他们的目的是什么……

Enigma: We just received another provocation from Lorentz Butterfly, with her new research paper attached.
哑谜：最近我们又收到了洛伦兹蝴蝶的挑衅信。还有她顺带写的论文。

Enigma: It mentions an experiment of utmost importance. The details are still classified as of now, but it won't be long before they're published.
哑谜：当中涉及的实验非常重要——具体仍处于保密阶段，但要不了多久你就会知道了。

Offering a polite nod, Enigma turns back to the desk and hands over his papers.
简单的寒暄过后，代理负责人转向柜台，递出文书。

Enigma: Hello, um, I'd like to check the file of a Special Task Investigator, Ms. Jones, from the Foundation's Buenos Aires branch. Umm, which department was it again?
哑谜：你好，我想申请调查……圣洛夫基金会布宜诺斯艾利斯分部……“暴雨”时代……什么部门来着？

Enigma: Storm Era, um, Historical Monitoring Department ... and Field Agent Administration?
哑谜：历史监测部门……委外合约人才管理组织，特遣调查官琼斯女士的详细资料。

Name Day peers up at him with a strained look.
柜台后的职员神色有些古怪。

Name Day: Oh, apologies, sir. I didn't catch that. Could you please repeat it?
命名日：不好意思，先生，我没有听清。能麻烦您再重复一遍吗？

Enigma: *sigh* Special Task Investigator Ms. Jones, of the "Storm" Era Historical Monitoring Department and Field Agent Administration from the Buenos Aires branch of the St. Pavlov Foundation.
哑谜：……圣洛夫基金会布宜诺斯艾利斯分部“暴雨”时代历史监测部门并委外合约人才管理组织特遣调查官，琼斯女士。

Name Day: Sorry, I'm quite certain the Buenos Aires branch has no such department.
命名日：我很确定布宜诺斯艾利斯分部并没有这么一个部门。

Name Day: There are sixty-three Foundation employees with the surname Jones. Four of them are assigned to the Buenos Aires branch.
命名日：至于姓氏为琼斯的女士，在基金会中共有63名，身处布宜诺斯艾利斯分部的则有整整4名。

Ulrich: Hahaha, I told you it was a fake identity.
乌尔里希：哈哈哈，这果然是个假身份！

Enigma: ...
哑谜：……

Enigma: I really shouldn't have brought you along.
哑谜：我开始后悔让你一起跟来了，乌尔里希。

Ulrich: Come now, Adler. Look at the bright side. Haven't you read Lorentz Butterfly's paper?
乌尔里希：乐观点。你没有认真看过洛伦兹蝴蝶发的论文吗？

Ulrich: Hissabeth studied the data and concluded that the experiment itself consumed very little of the pigment that was recovered, meaning Lorentz Butterfly must still have a considerable amount of it left.
乌尔里希：冷周六仔细研究过里面的实验数据，她推断，实验消耗的颜料并不多，洛伦兹蝴蝶手上必定还有一定量的矿物颜料剩余。

Enigma: We have to get hold of it for Laplace's sake. Maybe we should conduct a grid search of headquarters.
哑谜：拉普拉斯一定要得到剩余的矿物颜料。也许我们可以搜查总部的大楼——

Ulrich: Only if your goal is to make Laplace even more chaotic, Substitute Director.
乌尔里希：看来你确实想让拉普拉斯变得更混乱一些，阿德勒代理负责人。

Ulrich: Please, don't worry yourself. We have more materials-research experts than anywhere in the world. Ms. Hissabeth is already working on replicating the pigment. We'll have our own supply before long.
乌尔里希：再说，拉普拉斯并不缺乏材料研究的专家，冷周六已经在尝试复制这种矿物颜料了！过不了多久，我们就能制作出一模一样的东西来。

Enigma: I had better get back there and see if there is anything I can help with.
哑谜：我需要赶紧回去看看有没有能帮上忙的地方。

Ulrich: Come on, Adler, you're a physicist!
乌尔里希：得了吧，你只是个物理学家。

The two researchers exit together, immediately returning to their argument as if all the events preceding were just a brief ceasefire.
两位研究员争执着走远了。

Lorentz Butterfly: See you later, Mr. Adler, Mr. Ulrich.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：慢走，阿德勒先生，乌尔里希组长。

Enigma: Of course. See you, Marguerite.
哑谜：再见，玛格丽特。我们先回去了。

Nearby, the young writer jots down another new idea.
一旁的青年作家连忙在笔记本上记下最新的灵感。

Recoleta: Ah, I understand. Ms. Butterfly was waiting here just to savor her victory. She foresaw it all!
虚构集：蝴蝶女士等候在此处，是为了享受最完整的胜利，她早已预判了一切！

Recoleta: Oh, by the way, where is the rest of the pigment? It sounds like it could be the perfect beginning to our sequel!
虚构集：嗯？对了……故事的续集就从矿物颜料的去向展开吧！

Lorentz Butterfly: It's no longer in my possession.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：其实，剩余的矿物颜料，并不在我的手上。

Lorentz Butterfly: I passed it along to a "business partner" of mine.
洛伦兹蝴蝶：我把它交给了一位“合作伙伴”。

In the Suitcase
箱中一隅

Mamá Pérez: Heads up, my children! Let's get this done.
佩雷斯妈妈：打起精神来，孩子们！把今天的活儿办好了！

Mamá Pérez: We mice are natural experts at this kind of work.
佩雷斯妈妈：毕竟这可是我们老鼠最擅长的事情。

Pérez Jr. #1: That means we're stealing food today. Right, Mamá?
佩雷斯二世1号：我知道了，妈妈，今天我们要去偷东西吃！

Pérez Jr. #2: Of course not, you cheese-brain! We're rummaging through trash!
佩雷斯二世2号：不对，你这个蠢货！是去翻垃圾！

Mamá Pérez: Hey. Wrong again, my dearies. We're doing true gangster work!
佩雷斯妈妈：……孩子们，是做黑色生意！

Pérez Jr. #1: I see, Mamá. That's definitely something mice are good at.
佩雷斯二世1号：好的，妈妈。这确实也是老鼠所擅长的。

Pérez Jr. #2: But we're even better at rummaging through trash.
佩雷斯二世2号：不过还是不如翻垃圾来得擅长。

Pérez Jr. #1: Mamá, yesterday a human boy asked if I could stand on his head and help him paint. Could I do that when I grow up?
佩雷斯二世1号：妈妈，昨天有人问我能不能站在他脑袋上，操纵他画画——我长大之后，能去干这个吗？

Mamá Pérez: We'll talk about it when you're old enough to hold your own, alright?
佩雷斯妈妈：等你长大之后再说吧。

Mamá Pérez: Now listen carefully, my children. In exchange for a steady supply of arcane pigment, that cold-hearted bipedal witch forced me to sign over a thousand agreements.
佩雷斯妈妈：听好了，孩子们，那个可怕的雌性神秘学家胁迫我签订了一千多条的协议，作为后续提供矿物颜料的条件交换。

Mamá Pérez: Until we're strong enough to overthrow her, we have no choice but to do her bidding.
佩雷斯妈妈：在我们有足够的能量去反抗她之前，咱们都得替她办事了。

Mamá Pérez: Our first job was to tie up all the loose ends at the Turquoise Serpent Club.
佩雷斯妈妈：她交给咱们的第一件活儿，就是把绿松石蛇俱乐部的全部手尾都扫干净。

Mamá Pérez: It took me days to scrub all the clues leading back to her.
佩雷斯妈妈：我花了好几天才处理完俱乐部的事情，保证让后来调查的人找不到一丝痕迹。

Mamá Pérez: All that's left is to silence a certain individual. So, I contacted that exorcist girl, and somehow she ended up persuading me to help this peculiar painting find a home.
佩雷斯妈妈：现在是最后的封口工作了。为此，我还和那个夜巡特遣的除灵师私下联系过，最终答应要帮她好好安置那幅画……

Pérez Jr. #1: Yes, Mamá! We worked our tails off cleaning up the inside of that painting lady!
佩雷斯二世1号：噢，是的，妈妈。我们费了好大的劲儿才把画里的垃圾场清理干净！

The lead mouse knocks on a door within the Suitcase.
老鼠敲响了箱子里的门扉。

Vertin: Hmm?
维尔汀：嗯……？

Mamá Pérez: Hello, Ms. Two-Legged Lady, you must be the famous Timekeeper!
佩雷斯妈妈：尊敬的女士，想必您就是众人经常提及的司辰了！

Mamá Pérez: I hear your Suitcase holds many empty frames. Perhaps they are waiting for a painting to call them home!
佩雷斯妈妈：听说您的箱子中有许多画框，里面空空如也，等待着一幅画的入驻——

Mamá Pérez: Might you be interested in taking in this fabulous artwork?
佩雷斯妈妈：请问您是否有兴趣，收留这幅艺术品呢？

Several mice work hard to lug over a cleaned painting and place it at her feet.
几只老鼠卖力地将一幅收拾干净的画作搬来，放在脚边。

Lady by the Lake: Good evening, Timekeeper.
《湖边的女人》：你好，亲爱的司辰……

Lady by the Lake: This may sound a little brash, but ...
《湖边的女人》：虽然这样有些唐突，但——

Lady by the Lake: Might I stay inside your Suitcase?
《湖边的女人》：我能留在你的手提箱里吗？

48  返程 / Return

What's beneath your hat?
？？？：你的帽子下是什么？

选项 / Choices

1. My head
1. 头脑

2. My face
2. 我的脸

分支 1 / Branch 1

Just a head, no creepy black bones sticking out from your temples?
？？？：所以没有奇怪的东西贯穿了你的太阳穴。

Thank goodness. I'm not sure I could handle another conversation with those lunatics.
？？？：太好了，我可受不了第二个神经病。

I do hope, if you ever find a bone stuck through your brain one day, that you won't hesitate to see a doctor.
？？？：如果有根骨头卡在你脑子里，那你最好去看医生。

分支 2 / Branch 2

But why bother covering such a pretty face? You're young and good-looking.
？？？：那你可得趁现在多炫耀炫耀。

Don't be so shy to show it off. If that boneheaded lunatic doesn't bother to hide her face, you shouldn't either.
？？？：能以外貌为傲是年轻人的特权，到老了就只能炫耀脑子上的褶皱了。

But seriously, if you do find a bone stuck through your brain one day, don't hesitate to see a doctor.
？？？：所以为了晚年着想，如果你脑子里卡了根骨头，就赶紧去看医生。

I don't call that woman a lunatic without good reason.
？？？：而不是觉得自己好得很还到处闲逛。

选项 / Choices

1. I doubt I would survive it
1. 那我早就死了

2. It might hurt a bit
2. 听上去很疼

分支 2.1 / Branch 2.1

So you say, but you know what happened to her: she died and came back.
？？？：她也死了，然后又活了。

That is a rare privilege. How many can say they had the same gift? Death tends to be terribly final for most.
？？？：不该死的人有很多，偏偏是她享有了特权。

She was the exception, and just look at what she's made of it.
？？？：她还仗着这些特权为所欲为。

分支 2.2 / Branch 2.2

Life is pain. Lately it seems like every bone in my body aches ... Worse, the pain seems to come no matter the weather.
？？？：我也很疼，膝盖、腰椎、肩膀……不管下不下雨，每天都在疼。

But I wasn't rewarded with immortality for all my pains.
？？？：但我没有因此获得什么足以随心所欲，甚至超越死亡的力量。

It seems unfair that a privilege like that should be given to such a troublemaker.
？？？：这样一个只知道到处添乱的东西，偏偏得到了永恒……

This whole damned world is coming to an end!
？？？：世界真是完蛋了。

共同接续 / Shared continuation

Excerpt from the New York Times
《纽约新闻报》节选

Around midnight two days ago, a large fire broke out in the oak forest on the western outskirts. The delay of the warning and the complex terrains full of inflammables in the forest resulted in a fire that lasted a whole night till dawn and 13 deaths.
The victims entered the forest at dusk. Identities of 6 of them have been confirmed: the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers Tomson Joyce, Shelley Gerald, Kelly Keith, and the project manager Russell James. The others' are still being verified.

The Weyerhaeuser used to be a leader in the wood industry, yet it went bankrupt not long ago due to the wood supply crisis these years. After donating the formula of the Resurrection Potion (basic version) to the Maria Sanatorium in New York, the Sacred Heart Sanatorium in Ohio, and the Presbyterian Church Health Center on certain conditions, the Weyerhaeusers sold all of their forestry lands.
On the night of the fire, the Weyerhaeusers, their buyers, and the workers went into the forest to start the disafforestation project before they died. The police has found traces of arcanist's activities and a small amount of gasoline at the scene, which shows the case is an arson attack. More investigations are needed to identify the suspect.

Reporter: James Welles/Dorey Johnson
前日深夜，位于市郊西侧的橡木树林发生大范围火灾，由于预警不及，林中地形复杂且可燃物繁多，抢险困难，火势持续直至清晨时分才得以扑灭。
     于当日黄昏时分进入森林的十三名人员全部遇难。死亡人员中已确认身份的六人，包括韦耶豪瑟夫妇，与其交易者汤姆森·乔伊斯、雪莱·杰拉德、凯利·基思，施工负责人罗素·詹姆斯。其余人员身份均在进一步核实中。

     因近年来的木材供应风波，原本根基深重的韦耶豪瑟在前些日子宣告破产。韦耶豪瑟夫妇将基础版起死回生剂的配方，有偿捐赠与纽约玛利亚疗养院、俄亥俄州圣心疗养所与长老会医疗中心三方后，又将名下的所有林业用地对外抛售。
     火情发生当晚，韦耶豪瑟夫妇与购买者三人带领工作小组进入森林，预备开始铲除作业，随后遇难。经市警署排查与分析，火情现场的残留有神秘学家活动与少量汽油的痕迹，已可确定为人为纵火案件。更多关于犯罪嫌疑人的信息仍在进一步调查当中。

     报道记者：詹姆斯·威尔斯/多丽·约翰逊
